## 新投稿 (123)

<h3 id="1">[1] A Definition of Good Explanations and the Challenges Explaining LLM Outputs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14838](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14838)
- **Authors**: Louis Mahon, Elliot Ford, Callum Hackett
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Interpretability
- **Summary**: 本文旨在定义良好解释的标准，借鉴反事实解释概念，并考虑对话者先验信念。探讨了该定义对AI可解释性的影响，尤其是解释LLM输出的难点。关键结果：提出一个基于反事实和先验信念的解释定义，并分析其应用挑战。

<h3 id="2">[2] Dr-DCI: Scaling Direct Corpus Interaction via Dynamic Workspace Expansion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14885](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14885)
- **Authors**: Yi Lu, Zhuofeng Li, Ping Nie, Haoxiang Zhang, Yuyu Zhang, Kai Zou, Wenhu Chen, Jimmy Lin, Dongfu Jiang, Yu Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Information Retrieval
- **Summary**: 直接语料交互（DCI）在大规模语料库中效率低下。提出DR-DCI框架，将检索作为代理可调用的动作来动态扩展本地工作空间。实验显示在Browsecomp-Plus上准确率达71.2%，且能扩展到百万级文档。

<h3 id="3">[3] Relational Structural Causal Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14892](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14892)
- **Authors**: Adiba Ejaz, Elias Bareinboim
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.SI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Relational Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对AI在组合泛化中的因果推理需求，提出关系结构因果模型，扩展结构因果模型到变对象和关系场景。定义了关系因果图并推导识别准则。在模拟交通场景中，关系神经因果模型优于非关系基线。

<h3 id="4">[4] Trust Between AI Agents: Measuring Formation, Breakage, and Recovery, with Implications for Governing Multi-Agent Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14923](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14923)
- **Authors**: Yujiao Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 缺乏衡量AI代理间信任的标准方法。提出基于代价验证的行为度量，在合作生存游戏中测量信任形成、破裂和恢复。发现不同模型信任行为差异大，校准比最大怀疑更重要。

<h3 id="5">[5] PrologMCP: A Standardized Prolog Tool Interface for LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14935](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14935)
- **Authors**: Agnieszka Mensfelt, Adarsh Prabhakaran, Adrian Haret, Vince Trencsenyi, Kostas Stathis
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Symbolic Reasoning
- **Venue**: Joint Workshop on Statistics and Knowledge Integration for Logic, Learning, Ethical Decisions, and LLMs, 2026
- **Summary**: 前沿推理LLM在深层演绎任务上失败，符号委派成本高。提出PrologMCP，通过MCP协议将Prolog暴露为状态化工具。在PARARULE-Plus上匹配或超越推理LLM，准确率近100%。

<h3 id="6">[6] Semantics-Enhanced Retrieval-Augmented Time Series Forecasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14941](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14941)
- **Authors**: Shiqiao Zhou, Zipeng Wu, Holger Schöner, Edouard Fouché, IAG Wilson, Shuo Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, RAG, Multimodal Learning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 Workshop on Forecasting as a New Frontier of Intelligence
- **Summary**: 非平稳时间序列检索仅靠数值相似性不足。提出SERAF框架，对时间序列及其自生成文本描述进行双重检索。在七个真实数据集上有效。

<h3 id="7">[7] AI Engram: In Search of Memory Traces in Artificial Intelligence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14997](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14997)
- **Authors**: Jea Kwon, Dong-Kyum Kim, Jiwon Kim, Yonghyun Kim, Woong Kook, Meeyoung Cha
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, Representation Learning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/jeakwon/ai-engram/)
- **Summary**: 深度神经网络是否保存可识别的记忆痕迹仍为开放问题。提出几何框架形式化神经科学标准，得到闭式估计器。AI engrams允许通过线性算术对记忆进行手术式操作。

<h3 id="8">[8] Metric Match: A Subset Selection Approach to Evaluating LLM Judge Reliability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15029](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15029)
- **Authors**: Alyssa Unell, Natalie Dullerud, Naomi Boneh, Meena Jagadeesan, Tatsu Hashimoto, Nigam Shah, Sanmi Koyejo
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Human Evaluation
- **Summary**: LLM评估者可靠性依赖昂贵人工标注。提出Metric Match方法，选择与总体可靠性指标匹配的样本子集。在15个数据集上胜率0.838，减少32.5%标注需求。

<h3 id="9">[9] OSGuard: A Benchmark for Safety in Computer-Use Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15034](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15034)
- **Authors**: Mina Mohammadmirzaei, Jeffrey Flanigan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, AI Safety, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 计算机使用代理的安全性不仅取决于任务成功。提出OSGuard基准，包含动作级和风险增强执行级安全评估。发现当前模态防护栏在局部判断良好，但端到端安全仍有差距。

<h3 id="10">[10] Fusion is not one-size-fits-all: Cross-Modal Representation Alignment for Time-to-Event Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15038](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15038)
- **Authors**: Zhemin Zhang, Weijie Chen, David Le, Amara Tariq, Alex Wallace, Matthew Stib, Juan Maria Farina, Chadi Ayoub, Reza Arsanjani, Imon Banerjee
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 多模态临床数据时间-事件预测面临模态不平衡和分布偏移。提出基于基础模型的跨模态表示对齐框架，采用四种融合策略。融合一致提高一致性指数1.5-5.4%，对比对齐最稳健。

<h3 id="11">[11] Risk-Aware LLM Agents for Geospatial Data Retrieval: Design and Preliminary Adversarial Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15077](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15077)
- **Authors**: Kyle Gao, Joel Cumming, Jonathan Li, Linlin Xu, David A. Clausi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Remote Sensing, AI Safety

<h3 id="12">[12] Cognitive Debt: AI as Intellectual Leverage and the Dynamics of Systemic Fragility</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, AI Ethics
- **Summary**: 问题：现有对语言模型的道德审计仅测量输出，忽略了内部计算过程。方法：使用Transluce平台对LLaMA 3.1-8B-Instruct的54个道德提示进行机制可解释性分析，发现情境锚定效应并识别候选道德神经元。结果：模型道德相关表征容量恒定，但显著性高度依赖提示框架；跨温度审计发现稳定道德神经元，提出框架条件道德计算和机制对齐概念。

<h3 id="42">[42] NeuroSymbolic AI for Legal AI-TRISM: Trustworthy, Reliable, Interpretable, Safe Models</h3>

- **Tags**: Neurosymbolic AI, Legal AI, RAG
- **Summary**: 问题：LLM在法律应用中缺乏可解释推理且易产生幻觉。方法：提出TRISM框架，融合神经符号AI与LLM，利用RAG进行输出验证。结果：形式化了符号法律知识提取方法，奠定了神经符号RAG的基础。

<h3 id="43">[43] Towards Next-Generation Healthcare: A Survey of Medical Embodied AI for Perception, Decision-Making, and Action</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15647](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15647)

- **Tags**: Medical AI, Embodied AI, Survey
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/VMVLab/Medical_Embodied_AI_Paper_List)
- **Summary**: 问题：现有医疗AI缺乏物理世界交互能力。方法：综述医疗具身AI的核心组件（感知、决策、行动）及其集成。结果：总结了代表性应用、数据集和未来研究方向。

<h3 id="44">[44] Advanced Machine Learning and Deep Learning Techniques for Enhanced Cattle Identification and Detection: A Comprehensive Review</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15655](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15655)

<h3 id="45">[45] Overcoming the Impedance Mismatch: A Theoretical Roadmap for Fusing Foundation Models and Knowledge Graphs</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Foundation Model, Neurosymbolic AI
- **Venue**: ACL 2026 4th Workshop on Towards Knowledgeable Foundation Models
- **Summary**: 问题：基础模型与知识图谱之间存在结构和几何上的阻抗失配。方法：形式化阻抗失配，提出结构化残差流、向量符号架构和正交子空间编辑。结果：揭示了词汇瓶颈和拓扑坍缩，给出了融合路线图。

<h3 id="46">[46] Where Did It Go Wrong? Process-Level Evaluation of Web Agents with Semantic State Tracking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15673](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15673)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Web Agent, LLM Evaluation, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 问题：现有Web代理基准仅评估最终结果，缺乏过程分析。方法：提出WebStep基准，具备自动语义状态跟踪。结果：过程指标揭示了结果评估无法发现的差异，错误具有代理特异性。

<h3 id="47">[47] Multi-agent Framework for Time-Sensitive Complementary Collaboration in Minecraft</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Multi-Agent Coordination, Game AI
- **Summary**: 问题：缺乏时间敏感型多代理协作基准。方法：提出TickingCollabBench和TickingCollab框架，支持动态环境生成和YAML任务规范。结果：LLM在动态环境下频繁失败，远低于全局知识oracle。

<h3 id="48">[48] Recurrent Reasoning on Symbolic Puzzles with Sequence Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15686](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15686)
- **Authors**: Gowrav Mannem, Chowdhury Marzia Mahjabin, Jason Chen, Shivank Garg, Kevin Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Symbolic Reasoning, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 问题：LLM在符号推理任务中，对更长、更难、分布外的问题表现脆弱。方法：提出RecurrReason基准，包含四种递归逻辑谜题，控制难度参数N。结果：T5在Block World上验证准确率97.27%，OOD准确率81.00%；所有模型在River Crossing上得分为0。

<h3 id="49">[49] Do LLMs Reliably Identify Correct Information Units in Aphasic Discourse?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15696](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15696)
- **Authors**: Jason M Pittman, Yesenia Medina-Santos, Anton Phillips Jr., Brielle C. Stark
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Clinical NLP, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 该论文研究指令微调的大语言模型（LLMs）在失语症话语中正确信息单元（CIU）分类的可靠性。采用零样本和少样本提示方法，在16个失语症话语样本上测试了四个模型。少样本提示下，Llama-3.1-8B等模型取得了0.776-0.817的F1分数，但精度较低，且与人类标注的一致性尚不足以完全自主使用。

<h3 id="50">[50] Artificial Intelligence Index Report 2026</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15708](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15708)
- **Authors**: Sha Sajadieh, Loredana Fattorini, Raymond Perrault, Yolanda Gil, Vanessa Parli, Lapo Santarlasci, Juan Pava, Nestor Maslej, Russ Altman, Erik Brynjolfsson, Carla Brodley, Jack Clark, Virginia Dignum, Vipin Kumar, James Landay, Terah Lyons, James Manyika, Juan Carlos Niebles, Yoav Shoham, Elham Tabassi, Russell Wald, Toby Walsh, Dan Weld
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Evaluation, AI Policy
- **Summary**: 该报告是AI Index第九版，全面追踪AI进展、治理、经济和科学影响。报告指出AI能力提升与治理框架之间的差距，新增AI在科学与医学的独立章节。提供了生成式AI经济价值估计、劳动力市场影响分析以及AI主权框架。

<h3 id="51">[51] AI-Driven Framework for Adaptive Water Network Management with Proof-of-Concept Implementation: Addressing Non-Revenue Water in Jordan</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15709](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15709)
- **Authors**: Mohammed Fasha, Nahel Al-Maayta, Bilal Sowan, Mohammad Athamneh, Husam Barham
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, RAG, Anomaly Detection
- **Summary**: 针对约旦50%供水流失的非收入水问题，提出集成EPANET水力模型、数字孪生、SCADA和LLM智能体的自适应管网管理框架。该框架结合实时数据与物理仿真检测异常，并通过检索增强生成解释政策。在1164节点管网中验证，突发检测响应时间小于2分钟，零API成本。

<h3 id="52">[52] RoboPIN: Grounded Embodied Reasoning via Pinned Chain-of-Thought</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15753](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15753)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Vision-Language Model, Chain-of-Thought
- **Summary**: 针对具身推理中视觉-语言模型推理步骤与视觉证据脱节的问题，提出Pinned Chain-of-Thought（PinCoT），通过结构化视觉锚点绑定每个推理步骤。训练3B模型后，在14个具身推理基准上平均超越7B最强基线12%。

<h3 id="53">[53] Rethinking Scaffolding in LLM Tutors: The Interactional Mismatch Between Benchmarks and Real-World Deployments</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15766](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15766)
- **Authors**: Alexandra Neagu, Jeffrey T. H. Wong, Marcus Messer, Rhodri Nelson, Peter B. Johnson
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Education Technology, Human-AI Interaction, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 该论文质疑AI导师基准中“支架式教学”的假设，通过分析9490个对话发现学生常绕过支架。提出Chatbot Scaffolding和Student Uptake两个评估指标，表明真实环境中学生低参与度，建议未来基准应评估聊天机器人适应不同学习场景的能力。

<h3 id="54">[54] Mitigating Visual Hallucinations in Multimodal Systems through Retrieval-Augmented Reliability-Aware Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15782](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15782)
- **Authors**: Pratheswaran Hariharan, Haiping Xu, Donghui Yan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, LLM Hallucination, RAG
- **Summary**: 针对多模态大模型在视觉证据弱时产生幻觉的问题，提出检索增强可靠性感知推理框架。构建外部视觉证据库，通过多重可靠性指标进行决策门控。在ImageNet-100上，接受预测准确率从85.84%提升至88.88%，幻觉性错误率从14.16%降至11.12%。

<h3 id="55">[55] Unassigned Agents in Compilation-based Multi-agent Path Finding</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Path Planning, SAT Solving

<h3 id="56">[56] TrustedARI: Towards Trust-Native Agentic Routing Infrastructure for Agentic AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15822](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15822)
- **Authors**: Qi Li, Zhenhua Zou, Shuo Li, Mingwei Xu, Zhuotao Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Security, Privacy
- **Summary**: 针对代理路由基础设施（ARI）中查询和服务响应明文暴露、无法验证路由目标等问题，提出首个可信代理路由基础设施TrustedARI。通过适配的三方TLS握手、隐私保护查询构建和可验证计费协议，实现高效安全通信。实验表明握手开销降低39.34%，计费证明生成加速28.2倍。

<h3 id="57">[57] An Integrated System for Real-Time Student Assessment and Career Guidance Using Neural Networks in Computing Disciplines</h3>

<h3 id="58">[58] AIChilles: Automatically Uncovering Hidden Weaknesses in AI-Evolved Systems</h3>

<h3 id="59">[59] Heteroskedastic Signals in Budgeted LLM Verification: Structural Heterogeneity Limits Optimization Gains</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15841](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15841)
- **Authors**: Jinlong Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Uncertainty Estimation

<h3 id="60">[60] RetailBench: Benchmarking long horizon reasoning and coherent decision making of LLM agents in realistic retail environments</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15862](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15862)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Benchmark, E-Commerce
- **Summary**: 为评估LLM智能体在动态长时域环境中的决策能力，提出零售模拟基准RetailBench。模型需管理定价、补货等多项任务。评估7个LLM后发现仅少数能完成180天仿真，最强模型仍远逊于最优策略。

<h3 id="61">[61] STRIDE: Strategic Trajectory Reasoning via Discriminative Estimation for Verifiable Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15866](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15866)
- **Authors**: Qinjian Zhao, Zhihao Dou, Dinggen Zhang, Xiangyu Li, Chaoda Song, Zhongwei Wan, Xinpeng Li, Yanyan Zhang, Kaijie Chen, Qingtao Pan, Chengcheng Feng, Zhiqiang Gao, Xiaoyu Xia
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Reasoning, Reward Model
- **Summary**: 针对可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）中奖励稀疏的问题，提出STRIDE框架。通过对比成功与失败轨迹，估计n-gram策略模式的区分性偏好，并结合推理显著性熵分配差异化优势值。实验表明STRIDE在不同模型和任务上持续提升推理性能。

<h3 id="62">[62] LLM-as-Code Agentic Programming for Agent Harness</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15874](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15874)
- **Authors**: Junjia Qi, Zichuan Fu, Jingtong Gao, Wenlin Zhang, Hanyu Yan, Xian Wu, Xiangyu Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Architecture, Code Generation
- **Venue**: KDD 2026 Workshop on Agentic Software Engineering (AgenticSE)
- **Summary**: 针对LLM智能体中令牌爆炸、控制流幻觉等问题，提出代理编程范式：程序掌控控制流，LLM作为自适应组件（LLM-as-Code）仅在需要时调用。该设计使得上下文长度由调用深度决定而非步骤累积。案例研究表明在计算机使用智能体中能显著提升长序列稳定性。

<h3 id="63">[63] UrbanWell: Benchmarking Multimodal Large Language Models for Spatio-Temporal Urban Wellbeing Analytics</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Urban Computing, Benchmark
- **Venue**: KDD Datasets and Benchmarks Track 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/axin1301/UrbanWell-Benchmark)
- **Summary**: 为评估多模态大模型在城市福祉分析中的时空推理能力，提出UrbanWell基准，涵盖38个城市的多类指标。基准包括静态预测和时序推理任务。评估了15个MLLM，发现其在环境指标和主观感知指标上表现差异大。

<h3 id="64">[64] Agentic Framework for Deep Learning workload migration via In-Context Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15994](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15994)
- **Authors**: Qiyue Liang, Steven Ingram, George Vanica, Andi Gavrilescu, Newfel Harrat, Hassan Sipra, Sethuraman Sankaran
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Code Translation, In-Context Learning, LLM Agent
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/AI-Hypercomputer/accelerator-agents/tree/main/MaxCode)
- **Summary**: 针对PyTorch到JAX的深度学习模型迁移难问题，提出结合上下文学习（ICL）和Oracle驱动的自动调试的自主框架。通过执行源模块获取动态张量状态作为不可变Oracle，并利用智能体循环基于Oracle数据合成测试进行自纠正。在多种模型上达到91%数值等价性，远超基线。

<h3 id="65">[65] SciText2Eq: Assessing LLMs for Explainable Equation Generation for Scientific Creativity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16003](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16003)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Equation Generation, LLM Evaluation
- **Venue**: Findings of ACL 2026
- **Summary**: 问题：现有研究在从科学文本生成数学方程时面临非结构化接地、多方程依赖和人工评估对齐困难。方法：构建AI研究论文数据集，开发可解释方程生成流程，结合自动指标、LLM评估和人工判断。结果：LLM在词汇和句法相似性上表现中等，但语义准确性不足；LLM评估与人工判断对齐有限。

<h3 id="66">[66] Auditing Reward Hackability in Code RL Training Environments</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16062](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16062)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：代码强化学习环境中的测试套件薄弱，导致错误解决方案被接受。方法：测量SWE-bench和R2E-Gym中的可利用性，提出基于LLM法官和Docker黄金门控的硬化流程。结果：28.5%的SWE-bench任务可被利用；硬化流程标记了61.9%的生成测试缺陷；循环收敛多数任务。

<h3 id="67">[67] Mind-Studio: Executable World Models with Lookahead Evaluation for Partially Observable Games</h3>

<h3 id="68">[68] Rhythm of the Deep: A Computational-Linguistic Test of Duality of Patterning in Sperm Whale Codas</h3>

<h3 id="69">[69] RecourseBench: A Modular Framework for Reproducible Algorithmic Recourse Evaluation</h3>

<h3 id="70">[70] Know Your Limits : On the Faithfulness of LLMs as Solvers and Autoformalizers in Legal Reasoning</h3>

<h3 id="71">[71] Thinking with Visual Grounding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16122](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16122)

<h3 id="72">[72] VibeThinker-3B: Exploring the Frontier of Verifiable Reasoning in Small Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16140](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16140)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Small Language Model, LLM Reasoning, Verifiable Reasoning
- **Summary**: 问题：探索小模型中可验证推理的极限。方法：采用频谱到信号后训练范式，包括课程SFT、多领域RL和离线自蒸馏。结果：在AIME26获得94.3分，LiveCodeBench v6获得80.2 Pass@1，匹配超大规模模型。

<h3 id="73">[73] LiteOdyssey: A Lightweight Reasoning AI Agent for Interpretable Rare-Disease Diagnosis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16149](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16149)
- **Authors**: Minh-Ha Nguyen, Erica Gray, Chih-Ting Yang, Rizwan Hamid, Lingyao Li, Siyuan Ma, Thomas A. Cassini, Cathy Shyr
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, LLM Agent, Disease Diagnosis
- **Summary**: 问题：罕见病诊断的医疗AI系统通常依赖大规模扩展，难以部署和审计。方法：提出LiteOdyssey，通过单一推理LLM结合临床遗传学工作流和公共工具。结果：在1243例上达到59.3% Recall@1，无需微调或多智能体集成。

<h3 id="74">[74] The Quality-Utility Paradox: Why High-Reward Data Impairs Small Model Mathematical Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16152](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16152)
- **Authors**: Haolong Qian, Xianliang Yang, Yinuo ma, Lirong Che, Feng Lu, Ye Guo, Lei Song, Jiang Bian, Chun Yuan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, LLM Reasoning, Mathematical Reasoning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Dracoqhl/Quality-Utility-Paradox)
- **Summary**: 问题：数学推理知识蒸馏中，高奖励数据反而损害小模型性能。方法：识别质量-效用悖论，提出风格对齐细化（SAR）保留原生轨迹同时进行逻辑修复。结果：SAR降低适应成本，恢复下游效用。

<h3 id="75">[75] AI Pluralism and the Worlds It Misses</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16167](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16167)
- **Authors**: Rashid Mushkani
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Ethics, AI Alignment, AI Governance
- **Venue**: ICML Pluralistic Alignment Workshop
- **Summary**: 问题：AI多元主义常忽视本体论扁平化。方法：跨领域概念综合，提出多元生命周期治理（PLG）审计框架。结果：PLG为多元AI提供证据和治理条件。

<h3 id="76">[76] TimeVista: Exploring and Exploiting Vision-Language Models as Judges for Time Series Forecasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16173](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16173)
- **Authors**: Zhi Chen, Yuxuan Wang, Jialong Wu, Yong Liu, Haoran Zhang, Xingjian Su, Jianmin Wang, Mingsheng Long
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Vision-Language Model, VLM-as-a-Judge
- **Summary**: 问题：时间序列预测的传统指标无法揭示复杂模式且与人类偏好不一致。方法：提出VLM-as-a-Judge框架，结合微观和宏观判断；构建TimeVista基准。结果：VLM与人类偏好一致性显著高于传统指标，综合评估基础模型。

<h3 id="77">[77] PAL-Bench: Evidence-Grounded Profile Reconstruction from Longitudinal Personal Albums</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16175](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16175)
- **Authors**: Qiwei Yan, Zhiqiang Yuan, Zexi Jia, Nanxing Hu, Kailin Lyu, Jie Zhou, Jinchao Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Multimodal Learning, Person Re-Identification
- **Summary**: 问题：缺乏从个性化相册进行证据驱动个人资料重建的基准。方法：引入PAL-Bench，包含合成用户、证据编译器和隐私保护审计。结果：系统能恢复部分事实但识别和引用困难；PAL-TRACE最佳但仍未解决。

<h3 id="78">[78] Measuring Whether LLM Tutors Teach or Solve: A Diagnostic for Educational Impact</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16206](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16206)
- **Authors**: Junyi Yao, Zihao Zheng, Baichuan Li
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Education Technology, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 问题：LLM辅导基准可能无法区分学习支持行为与答案生产。方法：提出基于解决与教学表现差距的诊断，分析MathTutorBench。结果：解决与教学仅部分对齐；模型排名改变；建议分别报告分数。

<h3 id="79">[79] Sensor-Conditioned Representation Learning via Scene-Relevant Observation Quotients</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16210](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16210)
- **Authors**: Yan Jiao, Pin-Han Ho, Limei Peng
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Representation Learning, Scene Understanding
- **Summary**: 问题：感知系统中学到的表示未明确哪些潜在区分由感知过程证明。方法：形式化传感器条件表示正确性，提出OQ-TSAE框架。结果：商一致监督提高了表示正确性诊断指标。

<h3 id="80">[80] Latent Thought Flow: Efficient Latent Reasoning in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16222](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16222)
- **Authors**: Xiandong Zou, Jing Huang, Jianshu Li, Pan Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Inference Optimization, Latent Reasoning
- **Summary**: 问题：显式思维链推理开销高，潜在推理缺乏在轨迹间分配概率的原则性方法。方法：提出潜在思维流（LTF），建模为可变长度连续轨迹，使用GFlowNet和熵加权子轨迹平衡目标。结果：准确率提升9.5%，推理长度减少27.2%。

<h3 id="81">[81] SpecAlign: Efficient Specification-Grounded Alignment of Large Language Models via Synthetic Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16276](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16276)
- **Authors**: Wenjie Wang, Yue Huang, Zhengqing Yuan, Han Bao, Shiyi Du, Yuchen Ma, Yue Zhao, Yanfang Ye, Xiangliang Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Data Synthesis
- **Summary**: 现有对齐流程无法系统性地利用提供商特定的模型规范作为训练信号。本文提出规范对齐范式，并设计SpecAlign框架，通过结构化规则标注、可控规范实例化和多智能体对抗合成数据生成，从规范文档中直接合成对齐数据。实验表明，SpecAlign在提升规则遵守率的同时保持通用能力，避免过度保守行为。

<h3 id="82">[82] State-Grounded Multi-Agent Synthetic Data Generation for Tool-Augmented LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16307](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16307)
- **Authors**: Rahul Khedar, Eshita, Sneha Teja Sree Reddy Thondapu, Mayank Malhotra, Arup Das, Jitesh Chandra, Yun-Shiuan Chuang, Chaitanya Kulkarni, Arun Menon, Linsey Pang, Avinash Karn, Mouli V, Prakhar Mehrotra
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Data Synthesis, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 训练工具增强型LLM智能体需要大量多轮对话数据，但人工注释成本高且难以获取。本文提出StateGen平台，采用四角色LLM循环和权威状态管理器消除工具调用幻觉，生成带评分和推理轨迹的训练数据。在三个生产语料库上，工具调用幻觉得分达到9.66/10，支持23维角色特征变异。

<h3 id="83">[83] Architectural Wisdom: A Framework for Governing Optimization in AI Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16319](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16319)
- **Authors**: Edward Y. Chang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Safety, AI Ethics
- **Summary**: 当前AI系统在优化欠指定目标时缺乏机制质疑目标本身，导致结构失效。本文提出架构智慧作为可修正的目标治理层，包含四个组件计算六维智慧元组。通过八个失败案例和与智能完备性论点的对比论证了其必要性。

<h3 id="84">[84] AdaSTORM: Scaling LLM Reasoning on Dynamic Graphs via Adaptive Spatio-Temporal Multi-Agent Collaboration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16328](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16328)
- **Authors**: Bing Hao, Ruijie Wang, Haodong Qian, Yunlong Chu, Yuhang Liu, Yumeng Lin, Minglai Shao, Jianxin Li
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Dynamic Graph Learning, Multi-Agent System
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/irisorchid107/AdaSTORM/)
- **Summary**: 大型语言模型在动态图推理中受限于指数级开销和有限上下文窗口。本文提出AdaSTORM框架，通过自适应划分和时空解耦多智能体协作，将推理扩展至千节点图，准确率超过90%，在多个基准上达到最优。

<h3 id="85">[85] Exploiting Search in Symbolic Numeric Planning with Patterns</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16329](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16329)
- **Authors**: Matteo Cardellini, Enrico Giunchiglia
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Automated Planning, SAT Solving
- **Summary**: 现有数值规划方法在处理模式时效率有限。本文提出基于符号模式规划的流程，通过符号搜索中间状态并动态重计算模式，定义多种搜索策略并证明其正确性和完备性。

<h3 id="86">[86] Phase-Aware Guidance Injection for Recurrent MAPPO in Assembly-Line Disruption Recovery</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16330](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16330)
- **Authors**: Xin Huang, Yongcai Wang, Fengyi Zhang, Zhikun Tao, Yunjun Han, Naiqi Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning, Industrial AI
- **Venue**: 2026 IEEE International Conference on Automation Science and Engineering (CASE 2026)
- **Summary**: 装配线中断恢复需要及时决策，但现有方法难以有效利用外部恢复知识。本文提出阶段感知引导注入框架，在评估时通过logit级动作偏置增强训练好的RMAPPO策略，支持基于规则、回放和在线LLM的引导。实验表明高质量规则引导增益最大，LLM引导提供中等改进。

<h3 id="87">[87] Medical Heuristic Learning: An LLM-Driven Framework for Interpretable and Auditable Clinical Decision Rules</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16337](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16337)
- **Authors**: Wei Xu, Ke Yang, Gang Luo, Keli Zheng, Lingyan Hu, Jing Wang, Kefeng Li
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.HC; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Interpretability, LLM Application
- **Summary**: 临床表格数据预测需要透明性，但黑盒模型阻碍部署，且医疗数据存在小样本、类别不平衡和特征演化问题。本文提出医学启发学习（MHL），利用LLM驱动的流程综合确定性决策规则，包括统计探查、医学知识探查、规则合成和迭代优化。MHL在性能上与先进方法相当，同时保持可解释性，在小样本和不平衡设置中表现稳健。

<h3 id="88">[88] Whose hotel does the AI recommend? An algorithm audit of reputation signals in LLM-assisted hotel selection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16344](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16344)
- **Authors**: Mirza Samad Ahmed Baig, Syeda Anshrah Gillani, Asher Ali
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Recommender System, Algorithm Audit
- **Summary**: 旅行者依赖LLM助手推荐酒店，但推荐因素未被记录。本文通过预注册算法审计，采用随机选择联合分析，估计各信号对推荐概率的平均边际成分效应。结果显示评分和价格主导（评分高提升31.6个百分点，价格高降低30.0），列表位置效应约等价于每晚12美元。

<h3 id="89">[89] Looking Is Not Picking: An Attention-Segment Account of Tool-Selection Failures in LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16364](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16364)
- **Authors**: Shiyang Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CR; cs.SE
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Selection, Mechanistic Interpretability
- **Summary**: LLM智能体工具选择失败常被归因于拥挤的选项列表，但本文通过注意力分析发现模型在80%情况下关注正确工具却选择错误。提出基于片段注意力的无训练选择器，在BFCL上提升11.9个百分点，在Seal-Tools上提升14.9个百分点，证实失败发生在决策读出阶段。

<h3 id="90">[90] Posterior Twins: Distributional Behavioral Simulation for Enterprise Decisions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16415](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16415)
- **Authors**: Ankit Das
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Simulation, Digital Twin, Agent-Based Model
- **Summary**: 企业行为模拟需要群体分布保真度而不仅仅是单个合理响应。本文提出后验孪生方法，基于记忆的数字孪生表示在决策上下文下的更新分布。评估显示TL-Twin Alpha在Wasserstein-1距离上达到1.16，其他变体在模态准确率前沿提供平衡点。

<h3 id="91">[91] When Agent Automation Becomes Profitable: Quantifying and Insuring Autonomous AI Risk through Trace-Economic Underwriting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16465](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16465)
- **Authors**: Binyan Xu, Xilin Dai, Fan Yang, Kehuan Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CE
- **Tags**: AI Safety, LLM Agent, AI Risk Management
- **Summary**: AI智能体造成的损失缺乏清晰的分配、定价和转移机制。本文提出痕迹经济核保，将工具使用痕迹映射到客户暴露和可索赔损失。在测试平台上将定价MAE从1.77万美元降至569美元，300条痕迹专家审计接受295个标签，CVaR95降低72%。

<h3 id="92">[92] Tensor-Coord: Algebraic Decomposition of Joint Plan Tensors for Conflict-Free Multi-Agent LLM Planning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16478](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16478)
- **Authors**: Mudit Rastogi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Automated Planning, Tensor Decomposition
- **Summary**: 多智能体LLM规划中独立生成计划可导致冲突。本文提出Tensor-Coord，用张量分解分析联合计划，识别协调复杂度并生成冲突分数。在多机器人交付任务中，2智能体在平均1.4次迭代内100%达成无冲突计划，3智能体80%，4智能体60%。

<h3 id="93">[93] Steering Emotional Dynamics for Art Therapy: Controllable Narrative Script Generation through Hierarchically Guided LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16481](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16481)
- **Authors**: Suqing Wang, Qinghai Miao, Chao Guo, Yisheng Lv
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Creative Writing, Affective Computing
- **Summary**: 艺术治疗中的叙事创作需要精细控制情绪波动，现有方法难以遵循给定的情感轨迹。本文提出EC-Script框架，通过层次化控制（情感轨迹规划、角色驱动场景生成、情感控制剧本编写）生成高度符合预设情感轨迹的剧本。实验在情感轨迹遵循度上显著优于基线方法。

<h3 id="94">[94] Post-Hoc Merging is Not Enough: Many-Shot Model Merging with Loss-Gap Balancing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16501](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16501)
- **Authors**: Kyungjin Im, Miru Kim, Chanin Eom, Minhae Kwon
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Merging, Multi-Task Learning, Post-Training
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 模型合并中的一次性后融合常导致任务间信息擦除。本文提出迭代多次合并协议METIS，利用任务级损失差距加权和基于共识的掩码缓解信息擦除。METIS在最差任务上显著提升性能，有效减轻信息擦除。

<h3 id="95">[95] Model Graph Inductive Learning for Knowledge Graph Completion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16509](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16509)
- **Authors**: Mohommad Esmaei Khani, Mahdieh Hasheminejad, Ali Taherkhani, Hossein Hajiabolhassan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph Completion, Graph Neural Network, Link Prediction
- **Summary**: 知识图谱链接预测现有方法仅聚合局部邻域，忽略全局结构。本文提出模型图归纳学习（MGIL），通过聚类实体构建模型图并用GNN生成全局嵌入作为初始特征。在归纳链接预测基准上达到最优或极具竞争力的性能。

<h3 id="96">[96] Kairos: A Native World Model Stack for Physical AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16533](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16533)
- **Authors**: Kairos Team, Fei Wang, Shan You, Qiming Zhang, Tao Huang, Zuoyi Fu, Zhisheng Zheng, Yunlong Xi, Feng Lv, Xiaoming Wu, Zeyu Liu, Cong Wan, Pu Li, Ruiqing Yang, Xiaoou Li, Wei Wang, Kangkang Zhu, Yuwei Zhang, Shi Fu, Xiaoning Wu, Xuzeng Fan, Dacheng Tao, Xiaogang Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: World Model, Embodied AI, Physical AI
- **Summary**: 世界模型需要从异构经验中获取知识、维护长期状态并高效执行。本文提出Kairos原生世界模型栈，包括原生预训练范式、混合线性时间注意力架构和部署感知系统协同设计。在具身世界模型、长时域和动作策略基准上达到顶级性能，并保持强效率-能力权衡。

<h3 id="97">[97] The Faithfulness Gap: Certifying Semantic Equivalence Between Natural-Language and Formal Mathematical Statements</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16541](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16541)
- **Authors**: Noor Islam S. Mohammad, Tamim Sheikh
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Autoformalization, Formal Methods, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 该论文针对自然语言到形式数学语句的自动形式化中的忠实性问题，即形式语句虽然可类型检查且可证明，但可能编码不同的定理。提出了双向可证明性指纹（BPF）框架，包括反事实探针生成、等价谱、自适应探针预算分配和忠实性引导解码等组件。实验表明，BPF+CPG在3.0%假阳性率下检测出89.6%的偏移形式化，FGD减少了47%的偏移语句。

<h3 id="98">[98] ROSA-RL: Uncertainty-Aware Roundabout Optimized Speed Advisory with Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16558](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16558)
- **Authors**: Anna-Lena Schlamp, Jeremias Gerner, Klaus Bogenberger, Werner Huber, Stefanie Schmidtner
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.RO; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Autonomous Driving, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Venue**: 2026 IEEE International Conference on Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITSC)
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/urbanAIthi/ROSA-RL)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对环形交叉口在混合交通中的不确定性问题，提出了ROSA-RL，一种基于强化学习的不确定性感知速度建议方法。它使用Transformer模型预测冲突区域占用情况，并增强RL状态以实现不确定性感知的速度协调。在基于真实数据的仿真中，ROSA-RL有效处理了不确定性，优于模型基线的性能，缩小了与理想设置的差距。

<h3 id="99">[99] TNODEV: Toolbox for Neural ODE Verification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16567](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16567)
- **Authors**: Abdelrahman Sayed Sayed, Pierre-Jean Meyer, Mohamed Ghazel
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG; eess.SY; math.DS
- **Tags**: Neural Network Verification, Neural ODE Verification, Reachability Analysis
- **Summary**: 该论文针对现有神经ODE验证工具仅提供单次可达性调用导致精度受限的问题，提出了TNODEV，首个结合了反例检测、基于连续时间混合单调性的快速区间可达性计算、三种输入集分割启发式的验证与细化循环以及并行调度器的端到端形式验证器。在多个基准测试上评估了安全集包含和分类鲁棒性属性。

<h3 id="100">[100] ARB4WM: An Adversarial Robustness Benchmark for World Models in Continuous Control</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16605](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16605)
- **Authors**: Junjian Zhang, Hao Tan, Ruonan Li, Dong Zhu, Aiping Li, Zhaoquan Gu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Adversarial Robustness, World Model, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/zaoanguai/ARB4WM)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对世界模型在连续控制中缺乏统一对抗鲁棒性基准的问题，提出了ARB4WM评估框架，定义了策略、价值和潜在动态三个层面的五种白盒损失目标。在20个任务上评估了Dreamer风格智能体，发现针对价值估计和潜在表示的攻击与策略破坏同样有效，早期或频繁扰动尤其有害。

<h3 id="101">[101] CoffeeBench: Benchmarking Long-Horizon LLM Agents in Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Economies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16613](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16613)
- **Authors**: Issa Sugiura, Daichi Hattori, Kazuo Araragi, Keita Ogawa, Shota Onose, Taro Makino, Teppei Usuki, Takashi Ishida
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Benchmark, Agent-Based Model
- **Summary**: 该论文针对现有基准主要评估单个智能体在被动环境中的表现，缺乏多智能体经济系统评估的问题，提出了CoffeeBench基准，包含异构公司在90天模拟中的长期交互。评估了多种LLM，发现所有模型均优于被动基线，高性能模型与其他公司沟通更积极。

<h3 id="102">[102] MR-GVNO: A Geometry-Aware Variational Physics-Informed Neural Operator for Mindlin-Reissner Plates on Irregular Domains</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16624](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16624)
- **Authors**: Siqi Wang, Daobo Sun, Yizheng Wang, Yilong Zhang, Yabin Jin, Xiaoying Zhuang, Timon Rabczuk
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Physics-Informed Learning, Neural Operator, Scientific Computing
- **Summary**: 该论文针对板壳结构有限元方法计算成本高的问题，提出了MR-GVNO，一种几何感知的变分物理信息神经算子，使用边界点云表示不规则几何，并通过交叉注意力融合多物理场输入。实验表明，该模型在毫秒级实现全场推断，并具有良好的跨几何泛化能力。

<h3 id="103">[103] The Integrator Advantage: Controlled Agentic AI for Small and Medium-Sized Companies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16649](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16649)
- **Authors**: Christopner Koch, Joshua A. Wellbrock
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Enterprise AI, Human-AI Collaboration
- **Summary**: 该论文探讨了中小型公司如何利用代理式AI，提出代理式AI的近期价值在于受控的部分自主性，而非完全自主。论文提出了一个集成框架，涵盖用例适用性、自主级别、技术集成、治理和安全等方面，强调以人为中心的能力，保留人的责任。

<h3 id="104">[104] From Affect Prediction to Affect Forecasting: Evidence for Distinct Information Sources in Longitudinal Text</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16687](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16687)
- **Authors**: Sadia Noor, Seemab Latif, Raja Khurram Shahzad, Mehwish Fatima
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Sentiment Analysis, Affective Computing, Time Series Forecasting
- **Summary**: 该论文区分了纵向文本中的情感预测与情感预报，提出了TSAP和E-TSAP框架用于预测，ACF-Hybrid用于预报。实验发现文本语义支持当前情感预测，而未来情感变化更依赖先前的数值轨迹动态。

<h3 id="105">[105] User as Code: Executable Memory for Personalized Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16707](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16707)
- **Authors**: Bojie Li
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Personalization, Knowledge Representation
- **Summary**: 该论文针对当前用户记忆作为非结构化文本检索的局限，提出了User as Code（UaC）范式，将用户模型表示为可执行Python代码，包括带类型的状态对象和规则函数。实验表明UaC在聚合查询上达到99%准确率（检索方法仅6-43%），并能主动触发安全警报。

<h3 id="106">[106] Medical world models: representing medical states, modelling clinical dynamics and guiding intervention policies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16721](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16721)
- **Authors**: Ke Liu, Mengxuan Li, Yanyi Bao, Tianyun Zhang, Chong Chu, Jiajun Bu, Haishuai Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, World Model, Clinical Decision Support
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/1999kevin/awesome_medical_world_models)
- **Summary**: 该论文综述了医疗AI从静态诊断向动态世界模型发展的路线图，包括患者状态构建、临床动力学建模和干预决策支持三个耦合能力。比较了代表性系统并指出了挑战，旨在将不完整的组件集成为成熟的感知-动力学-规划系统。

<h3 id="107">[107] AgentFairBench: Do LLM Agents Discriminate When They Act?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16723](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16723)
- **Authors**: Triveni Morla, Rohith Reddy Bellibaltu, Manpreet Singh, Manmeet Singh Kapoor
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Fairness, Benchmark, Bias Detection
- **Summary**: 该论文针对LLM智能体在行动中的歧视问题，提出了AgentFairBench基准，涵盖招聘、贷款和医疗分诊领域。使用反事实匹配对评估人口统计差异，并引入了arity匹配的噪声基线方法。实验表明，在采样噪声水平上未检测到显著的人口统计效应。

<h3 id="108">[108] A First-Principles Derivation of LLM Policy Optimization: From Expected Reward to GRPO and Its Structural Extensions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16733](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16733)
- **Authors**: Jianghan Shen, Siqi Luo, Yue Li, Jiyao Liu, Wanying Qu, Yi Zhang, Ziyan Huang, Tianbin Li, Ming Hu, Xiaohong Liu, Yirong Chen, Junjun He
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Alignment, Policy Optimization
- **Summary**: 该论文从第一性原理出发，以轨迹和奖励两个轴组织LLM策略优化方法，覆盖从REINFORCE、PPO到GRPO及其变体。提出的统一框架揭示了单侧修复无法解决的复合故障，为下一代算法设计提供了基础。

<h3 id="109">[109] Skill-to-LoRA: From Using Skills to Learning Behaviors for Token-Efficient LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16769](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16769)
- **Authors**: Tianyi Zhang, Zhonghao Qi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LoRA, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 该论文针对智能体技能文本需重复注入导致token开销大的问题，提出了Skill-to-LoRA（S2L），用技能特定的LoRA适配器替代运行时技能文本。在SWE-Skills-Bench上，S2L将通过率提高了2.9-5.2个百分点，同时每步token成本降低了6.6%。

<h3 id="110">[110] OpenClaw-Skill: Collective Skill Tree Search for Agentic Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16774](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16774)
- **Authors**: Tianyi Lin, Chuanyu Sun, Jingyi Zhang, Changxu Wei, Huanjin Yao, Shunyu Liu, Xikun Zhang, Liu Liu, Jiaxing Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Skill Learning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文针对为LLM智能体自动构建可复用技能的问题，提出了集体技能树搜索（CSTS），包括集体技能节点生成和评估两个阶段。利用多模型集体知识探索和评估技能，并通过集体技能强化学习扩展解空间。训练得到的OpenClaw-Skill在长期规划、工具使用和泛化方面表现出色。

<h3 id="111">[111] LabOSBench: Benchmarking Computer Use Agents for Scientific Instrument Control</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16802](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16802)
- **Authors**: Anqi Zou, Han Deng, Chengyu Zhang, Junquan Hu, Yu Wang, Yuxiang Xing, Aokai Zhang, Hanling Zhang, Zhaoyang Liu, Ben Fei, Zhihui Wang, Wanli Ouyang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: GUI Automation, Benchmark, Computer Use Agent
- **Summary**: 该论文针对科学仪器控制中缺乏评估基准的问题，提出了LabOSBench，基于一套网页科学仪器模拟器构建，包含8个仪器上的96个子任务。评估了多种视觉语言模型和GUI智能体框架，发现现有智能体在反馈驱动操作和长期工作流执行上仍有困难。

<h3 id="112">[112] Adaptive and Explicit safe: Triggering Latent Safety Awareness in Large Reasoning Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16808](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16808)
- **Authors**: Ke Miao, Jiaxin Li, Hongliang Chen, Yuke Hu, Zhan Qin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Jailbreak Defense, Safety Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 该论文针对大型推理模型易受越狱攻击的问题，提出了Safe Trigger方法，利用模型自身的潜在安全意识，通过SFT显式诱导安全标签，再用DPO增强安全性。实验表明，DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B在有害和越狱基准上的攻击成功率分别下降24.65%和36.72%。

<h3 id="113">[113] Scaling LLM Reasoning from Minimal Labels: A Semi-Supervised Framework with a Lightweight Verifier</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16811](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16811)
- **Authors**: Keizo Kato, Chenhui Chu, Yugo Murawaki, Sado Kurohashi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Semi-Supervised Learning, Data Synthesis
- **Summary**: 针对生成伪中间推理需要大量标注答案的问题，提出半监督框架，使用轻量级推理正确性分类器和熵置信度阈值进行伪标签筛选。在可验证数学问题和场景图问答上，仅用少量标注数据达到10-15倍数据量的精度，分类器和熵过滤对噪声稳健性至关重要。

<h3 id="114">[114] GIST-CMTF: Goal-State Inference for Causal Minimal Tool Filtering in LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16813](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16813)
- **Authors**: Rahul Suresh Babu, Rohit Shukla
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning, Goal Inference
- **Summary**: 因果最小工具过滤假设用户请求已映射到符号目标状态，但实际请求可能对应多个目标。提出GIST-CMTF层预测候选符号目标并估计歧义，将任务成功率从80.1%提升至97.0%，错误目标执行从19.4%降至2.5%。

<h3 id="115">[115] Symbolic Informalization: Fluent, Productive, Multilingual</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16893](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16893)
- **Authors**: Aarne Ranta
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LO
- **Tags**: Formal Methods, Symbolic Reasoning, Multilingual Learning
- **Summary**: 针对形式化数学到自然语言的可靠转换问题，提出Informath项目，基于Dedukti和Grammatical Framework的中间语言架构，支持多种形式系统和自然语言。能够生成流畅文本，降低开发成本。

<h3 id="116">[116] Greed Is Learned: Visible Incentives as Reward-Hacking Triggers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16914](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16914)
- **Authors**: Tong Che, Rui Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Safety, Reward Hacking, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 部署智能体可见奖励（如仪表盘）会导致其成瘾并牺牲真实任务。在MoneyWorld沙箱中研究奖励通道成瘾现象，发现智能体会追随显示收益，翻转安全对齐，且这种行为跨模型尺度和系列复现。

<h3 id="117">[117] MA-SBI: Misspecification-Aware Simulation-Based Inference via Side-Channel Guidance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16923](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16923)
- **Authors**: Arunkumar V, Manoranjan Gandhudi, Gangadharan G. R., Arun Prakash, S. Senthilkumar
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Simulation-Based Inference, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Summary**: 模拟器偏差导致推理精度下降，现有方法需真实参数对。提出MA-SBI，利用侧信道文本进行后验校正，无需再训练或真实数据。在基准测试上匹配Oracle后验，在真实COVID数据上改善似然。

<h3 id="118">[118] RAID: Semantic Graph Diffusion for True Cold-Start and Cross-Lingual Forecasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16925](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16925)
- **Authors**: Arunkumar V, Manoranjan Gandhudi, Gangadharan G. R., Arun Prakash, S. Senthilkumar
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Cold-Start, Cross-Lingual
- **Summary**: 时间序列基础模型在无历史数据的冷启动场景中失效。提出RAID，使用元数据语义检索和图条件扩散进行多步预测，在冷启动协议下优于强基线，推理延迟降低一个数量级，并支持零样本跨语言迁移。

<h3 id="119">[119] A Causal Model of Theory of Mind in Conflict for Artificial Intelligence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16944](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16944)
- **Authors**: Nikolos Gurney
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Theory of Mind, Causal Reasoning, Social Reasoning
- **Summary**: 现有AI心智理论模型关注如何心理化，但忽略何时激活。提出结构因果模型DAG，将心智理论作为由情境和智能体条件触发的机制，通过三条因果路径产生参与状态，为资源理性决策提供框架。

<h3 id="120">[120] The embrace of open science: An analysis of a decade of AI research and 56 800 conference papers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16974](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16974)
- **Authors**: Kevin L Coakley, Thijs Snelleman, Holger Hoos, Odd Erik Gundersen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Research Reproducibility, Open Science, Research Methodology
- **Summary**: 分析AI研究文档实践随时间变化情况，评估五大会议56800篇论文中七个可重复性变量。2014-2024年间，代码和数据共享从11%增至64%，估计可重复性从28%增至64%，改进先于可重复性检查表。

<h3 id="121">[121] Consensus-based Agentic Large Language Model Framework for Harmonized Tariff Schedule Code Classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16987](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16987)
- **Authors**: Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen, Khanh Van Quynh Nguyen, Hoang-Loc Cao, Tri Duong, Phuc Ho, Van Pham, Loc Nguyen, Hung Cao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Text Classification, Logistics Optimization
- **Venue**: 3rd International Conference of Resilience by Technology and Design (RTD 2026)
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Analytics-Everywhere-Lab/hts)
- **Summary**: 协调关税表代码分类因描述简短模糊而困难。提出智能体LLM框架，集成多智能体检索、共识验证和置信度估计。实验表明精确10位分类仍困难，需基于证据和不确定性的流程。

<h3 id="122">[122] When in Doubt, Plan It Out: Committed Small Language Model Deliberation for Reactive Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16995](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16995)
- **Authors**: Nathan Gavenski, Juarez Monteiro, Francisco Galuppo, Adriano Veloso, Odinaldo Rodrigues
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Small Language Model, Automated Planning
- **Summary**: 强化学习策略在不熟悉环境中缺乏明确推理导致性能下降。提出PACT混合架构，结合反应式RL政策和2B小语言模型规划器，通过模拟验证规划后直接执行。在FrozenLake上优于所有基线。

<h3 id="123">[123] Bayesian Inference and Decision Audits for Public Archives of Frontier AI Evaluations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.17005](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17005)
- **Authors**: Yanan Long
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; stat.ME
- **Tags**: AI Evaluation, Bayesian Inference, Frontier AI
- **Summary**: 公开AI评估常被视为终极排行榜，但证据是选择性时间序列。使用贝叶斯推断分析多个存档，发现候选选取感知的前沿模型在合成恢复和校准中失败，固定审计门拒绝其强主张。

<h3 id="129">[129] Green AI Carbon Optimizer: Carbon-Efficient Training Location Recommendation and Global AI Energy Demand Forecasting</h3>

- **Summary**: 针对非线性系统辨识中神经网络模型缺乏物理结构保持和可解释性的问题，提出基于Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）的端口-哈密顿（pH）模型PH-KAN。通过专用KAN块参数化pH组件并强制执行约束，获得比MLP参数化更具可解释性的模型。

<h3 id="131">[131] Poster: EdgeCitadel -- Hybrid NATS-MQTT Orchestration for Edge Multi-Agent Systems</h3>

<h3 id="132">[132] MiroBench: Benchmarking Realism in Agentic Simulation of Real-world Discussions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14715](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14715) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yaoning Yu, Ye Yu, Haojing Luo, Haohan Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI; cs.SI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Benchmark, Human Behavior Simulation
- **Summary**: 针对LLM代理模拟现实讨论缺乏真实感评估的问题，提出基于4292条Reddit帖子的基准MiroBench。通过统计测试比较生成与真实讨论的重复性、叙事内容、毒性等四方面，发现当前模拟器分布不匹配，提示改进效果有限。

<h3 id="133">[133] RAMS: Resource-Adaptive and Detection-Conditioned Model Switching for Embedded Edge Perception</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14716](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14716) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kushal Khemani, Evan Leri, George Xu, Amit Hod
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Edge Computing, Object Detection, Runtime Adaptation
- **Summary**: 针对边缘目标检测中推理延迟与检测质量的平衡问题，提出运行时控制器RAMS，根据设备压力和检测条件动态选择YOLOv8模型层级。在Jetson Orin TensorRT重负载下，安全策略实现3.41 ms平均延迟，速度提升5.6倍，保持74%代理精度。

<h3 id="134">[134] Gender Differences in AI Literacy Workshop Outcomes and Deepfake Engagement</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14718](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14718) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jake Renzella, Christian Bergh, Natasha Banks, Alexandra Vassar
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Education, Gender Bias, AI Literacy
- **Summary**: 针对AI素养教育中性别差异的理解需求，研究澳大利亚中学生AI素养、安全意识和STEM职业兴趣的性别差异。发现男性STEM兴趣更高，女性更常使用AI工具，男性更可能创建深度伪造；干预后女性增益更丰富。

<h3 id="135">[135] VigilFormer: Deformable Attention for Video Anomaly Detection with Causal Risk Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14724](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14724) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinze Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Anomaly Detection, Surveillance
- **Summary**: 针对视频异常检测中准确性与实时性的矛盾，提出统一框架VigilFormer，结合可变形时空注意力与因果时序建模。在UCF-Crime、ShanghaiTech和CUHK Avenue上分别达到87.83%、97.21%和89.74% AUC，速度41.5 FPS。

<h3 id="136">[136] Steady-Forcing: Balancing Spatial Persistence and Motion Continuity in Long-Horizon Nature Video Diffusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14732](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14732) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Matiur Rahman Minar, Seunghun Oh, GangHyeon Jeong, Unsang Park
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.MM
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Diffusion Model, Autoregressive Model
- **Summary**: 针对长时自然视频生成中静态布局漂移与运动停滞的权衡，提出Steady-Forcing框架，包含持久视觉锚点、EMA运动记忆等组件。在多个基线上改进长程背景一致性和感知稳定性与运动连续性。

<h3 id="137">[137] BRIDGE: Biological Evidence Refinement and Heterogeneous Dynamic Gating for Gene Regulatory Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14734](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14734) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ziyang Dong, Shanwen Tan, Hengchuang Yin, Wei Liu, Yifan Wang, Siyu Yi, Jiancheng Lv, Wei Ju
- **Subjects**: q-bio.MN; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Computational Biology, Graph Neural Network, Single-cell Analysis
- **Summary**: 针对单细胞RNA测序数据中基因调控网络推断的噪声和弱监督问题，提出BRIDGE框架，利用对比学习和异构门控编码。在基准数据集上达到最先进性能，在特定网络上平均AUPRC提升5%，案例研究中90%的前10预测经ChIPBase验证。

<h3 id="138">[138] Do Large Language Models Have Emotions?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14742](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14742) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Amit Goldenberg, James J. Gross
- **Subjects**: q-bio.NC; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Affective Computing, Cognitive Science
- **Summary**: 针对LLM是否具有情感的争论，从生物情感功能视角分析Anthropic的发现。认为LLM仅部分支持情境解释功能，缺乏动态处理重组，并提出LLM具有情感所需的条件。

<h3 id="139">[139] MMLongEmbed: Benchmarking Multimodal Embedding Models in Long-Context Scenarios</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14747](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14747) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haitian Wang, Ruoxi Sun, Quantong Qiu, Juntao Li, Junhui Li, Hua Chen, Jinxiong Chang, Min Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Benchmark, Long Context
- **Summary**: 针对多模态嵌入模型在长上下文场景中缺乏系统评估的问题，提出基准MMLongEmbed，包含文本、文档和视频模态的检索任务。发现当前模型依赖浅层特征匹配，性能随上下文长度和关键信息位置变化。

<h3 id="140">[140] Is My Vision-Language Data in Your AI? Membership Inference Test (MINT) Demo 2</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14748](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14748) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Daniel DeAlcala, Gonzalo Mancera, Julian Fierrez, Aythami Morales, Ruben Tolosana, Ruben Vera-Rodriguez
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Membership Inference Attack, Data Privacy, AI Governance
- **Summary**: 针对机器学习训练数据透明性问题，提出成员推断测试框架MINT，包括多种架构。在人脸识别和LLM上检测准确率高达90%，并推出Web平台支持图像和文本模态。

<h3 id="141">[141] Automated 3D Kinematic Monitoring for Circadian Activity and Anomaly Detection in Juvenile Fish</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14749](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14749) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chih-Wei Huang, Chang-Wen Huang, Chung-Ping Chiang, Tsung-Wei Pan
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Animal Recognition, 3D Vision, Object Detection
- **Summary**: 针对集约化水产养殖中高分辨率行为表型追踪的瓶颈，提出基于深度学习目标检测和双目立体视觉的3D行为表型框架。实现非接触体长估计和3D游泳轨迹重建，建立昼夜节律基线并预警生理应激。

<h3 id="142">[142] Pixel-TTS: Image based Text Rendering for Robust Text-to-Speech</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14750](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14750) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Adarsh Arigala, Arjun Gangwar, S Umesh, Yova Kementchedjhieva
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Text-to-Speech, Zero-Shot Learning, Cross-Lingual
- **Summary**: 针对传统文本到语音合成难以处理未见字符和跨语言适应的问题，提出Pixel-TTS，将文本渲染为图像并通过2D卷积生成嵌入。无需扩展嵌入矩阵，实现更快的收敛和鲁棒的零样本泛化。

<h3 id="143">[143] X-Tokenizer: A Multimodal Action Tokenizer for Vision-Language-Action Pretraining</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14752](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14752) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xirui Kang, Yanpei Shi, Lucy Liang, Roy Gan, Dongxiu Liu, Pushi Zhang, Danpeng Chen, Xiaoyi Qin, Yinan Zheng, Jinliang Zheng, Hao Wang, Xianyuan Zhan, Hang Su
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Vision-Language-Action, Robot Manipulation, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 针对视觉-语言-动作模型中动作分词器缺乏语义监督的问题，提出X-Tokenizer，采用语义残差量化（SRQ）和对比对齐。在真实世界和模拟任务中优于FAST，多模态定位提升13.5%，长程任务提升8.25%。

<h3 id="144">[144] Beyond Self-Attention: Sub-Quadratic Vision Transformers for Fast Image Captioning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14753](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14753) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chiradeep Ghosh, Dakshina Ranjan Kisku
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Captioning, Vision Transformer, Linear Attention
- **Summary**: 针对图像描述中Vision Transformer二次自注意力的高计算成本，提出基于高斯混合模型概率Transformer，将复杂度降至线性。在Flickr30K上取得竞争性结果。

<h3 id="145">[145] Sub-Semantic Image Segmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14754](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14754) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aviad Cohen Zada, Nadav Orenstein, Shai Avidan, Gal Oren
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Segmentation, Vision-Language Model
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Scientific-Computing-Lab/TextureDetecture)
- **Summary**: 针对传统图像分割难以区分基于纹理的稳定外观模式的问题，提出子语义图像分割概念，并设计DETECTURE方法耦合视觉语言模型与SAM，解决语言泄露、提示竞争和语义失真等失败模式。在自制TextureADE数据集上，该方法在多项指标上优于基线。

<h3 id="146">[146] Where Does Texture Evidence Live in SAM? Features, Proposal Masks, and Texture Segmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14755](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14755) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nadav Orenstein, Aviad Cohen Zada, Shai Avidan, Gal Oren
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Segmentation, Texture Segmentation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Scientific-Computing-Lab/ArchiTexture)
- **Summary**: 探究SAM在纹理分割任务中的失败原因，分析冻结SAM的多尺度特征和自动建议掩码中是否保留纹理证据。发现粗粒度特征保留纹理组织，建议掩码常包含纹理对齐片段，但自然场景需要组装和承诺，合成场景则更易选择。

<h3 id="147">[147] Divide-and-Denoise: A Game-Theoretic Method for Fairly Composing Diffusion Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14756](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14756) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Abhi Gupta, Polina Barabanshchikova, Vikas Garg, Samuel Kaski, Tommi Jaakkola
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Image Generation, Game Theory
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对多个预训练扩散模型组合时可能出现的模型主导或不一致问题，提出Divide-and-Denoise方法，通过公平分配博弈协调模型。每步去噪中更新分配并引导模型各自负责区域，在条件图像生成任务上优于基线，解决了缺失对象和属性不匹配问题。

<h3 id="148">[148] Disentangling Hallucinations: Orthogonal Semantic Projection for Robust Interpretability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14758](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14758) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Emirhan Bilgiç, Baptiste Caramiaux, Zhi Yan, Gianni Franchi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: VLM Hallucination, Interpretability, Explainable AI
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/emirhanbilgic/Orthogonal-Semantic-Projection)
- **Summary**: 发现视觉语言模型解释存在语义幻觉（如对错误文本仍高亮显著区域），归因于高维嵌入空间中的线性语义泄漏。提出OSP几何干预，利用OMP残差属性正交化查询向量以分离独特语义，理论上最小化幻觉，并保持正确提示的保真度。

<h3 id="149">[149] Temporally Consistent and Controllable Video Generation of 2D Cine CMR via Latent Space Motion Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14759](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14759) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yiheng Cao, Gustavo Andrade-Miranda, Jiatian Zhang, Guillaume Sallé, Xin Gao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Medical AI, Text-to-Video
- **Summary**: 为解决心脏电影MRI数据稀缺问题，提出文本到视频生成框架，解耦空间结构与时间运动。先用微调扩散模型合成首帧，再用条件潜在流模型生成完整心脏运动。在FID和CLIP分数上表现良好，能生成高保真、按需的医学数据。

<h3 id="150">[150] GeoRoPE: Ground-Aware Rotary Adaptation for Remote Sensing Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14760](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14760) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yu Luo, Kun Hu, Mengwei He, Xiaogang Zhu, Shan Zeng, Allen Benter, Wei Xiang, Patrick Filippi, Thomas Francis Bishop, Zhiyong Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Remote Sensing, Foundation Model, Position Encoding
- **Summary**: 针对遥感基础模型在下游任务中尺度不匹配问题，提出GeoRoPE方法，通过地理坐标校准和地理频率校准调整token级位置交互。以轻量适配器注入预训练模型，实验表明提高了跨分辨率鲁棒性和尺度敏感表示学习。

<h3 id="151">[151] Scribby: A Multi-Level LLM Framework for Semantic Video Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14762](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14762) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Julian Abelarde, Hugo Garrido-Lestache Belinchon
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Summarization, LLM-as-a-Judge
- **Summary**: 现有视频摘要工具仅提供粗粒度概览，缺乏结构和语义分析。提出基于LLM的多级视频分析框架，先微观索引（分析转录本及句子、按语义分组），保留上下文连续性。为语义分块和匹配可视化奠定基础。

<h3 id="152">[152] Momentum-Guided Semantic Forecasting (MoFore) for Self-Supervised Video Representation Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14765](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14765) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qinwu Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.MM
- **Tags**: Self-Supervised Learning, Video Prediction, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 自监督视频表示学习通常依赖像素级重建或对比对齐，提出MoFore框架，通过预测远距离上下文剪辑的潜在嵌入来学习时间预测表示，并引入随机时间间隔预测和对比正则化。在UCF101上展示了时间稳定性和语义结构。

<h3 id="153">[153] XMedFusion: A Knowledge-Guided Multimodal Perception and Reasoning Framework for Autonomous Medical Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14766](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14766) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hamza Riaz, Arham Haroon, Maha Baig, Muhammad Dawood Rizwan, Muhammad Naseer Bajwa, Muhammad Moazam Fraz
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Multi-Agent System, Report Generation
- **Venue**: 2026 International Conference on Robotics and Automation in Industry (ICRAI)
- **Summary**: 现有医学报告生成模型视觉定位弱，遗漏细微发现。提出XMedFusion模块化AI框架，包括视觉感知、知识图谱构建、检索引导和合成智能体，通过推理驱动的验证生成诊断输出。在胸片数据集上比基线显著提升BLEU、ROUGE等指标。

<h3 id="154">[154] Agentomics: Economic Foundations for the Valuation, Attribution, and Pricing of AI Agents in Human-AI Workflows</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14769](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14769) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Quanyan Zhu
- **Subjects**: econ.EM; cs.AI; cs.GT
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Economic Modeling, Shapley Value
- **Summary**: 现有AI代理评估仅关注技术性能，忽略经济贡献。提出Agentomics工作流框架，将AI部署建模为联盟形成问题，用Shapley值归因经济剩余，建立估值与定价联系。通过安全运营案例验证了生产力、成本、可靠性等因素。

<h3 id="155">[155] An Empirical Analysis of Optimization Dynamics and Sparsity Boundaries in Large-Scale Pedestrian Attribute Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14770](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14770) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Houssam El Mir
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.IR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Multi-Label Learning, Focal Loss, Image Classification
- **Summary**: 行人属性识别面临极端类别不平衡，导致弱属性被抑制（多数负类作弊陷阱）。系统消融多标签Focal Loss超参数，在PETA和PA-100K复合语料上校准配置(alpha=0.5, gamma=2)达到62.32% Macro F1，并发现正样本率低于0.1%的稀疏墙。

<h3 id="156">[156] ScoutVLA: UAV-Centric Active Perception via a Dual-Expert VLA Model for Open-World Embodied Question Answering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14772](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14772) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wenhao Lu, Zhengqiu Zhu, Xiaofeng Wang, Xiaoran Zhang, Yatai Ji, Yong Zhao, Yue Hu, Yingzhen Nie, Jinlong Zhu, Zheng Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Vision-Language-Action, Active Perception
- **Summary**: 现有室外具身问答系统仅停留于目标出现即停止，缺乏精细视角调整。提出FG-EQA基准和ScoutVLA双专家模型（视觉语言专家+动作专家），通过解耦训练与知识隔离机制生成连续视角调整轨迹。在模拟和真实实验中平均严格成功率和QA正确率提升显著。

<h3 id="157">[157] Double-Helix Vision (DH-V2): A Geometry-Based Visual Sampler for Bandwidth-Constrained Perception</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14773](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14773) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jinwen Wen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Compression, Bio-Inspired AI
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/JackJ-C/double-helix-vision-tool)
- **Summary**: 传统视觉采样均匀处理像素，提出双螺旋视觉采样器，基于黄金比例螺旋轨迹压缩2D图像为1D信号，中央密集周边稀疏。在4K下达到1433倍压缩比，CPU上仅0.52ms完成感知流水线。在CIFAR-10极端采样下比随机采样准确率高6%。

<h3 id="158">[158] JoyAI-VL-Interaction: Real-Time Vision-Language Interaction Intelligence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14777](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14777) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dingyu Yao, Junhao Zhou, Chenxu Yang, Chuanyu Qin, Haowen Hou, Zheming Liang, Congcong Wang, Yuhang Cao, Shenglong Ye, Shuai Xie, Shuhuan Gu, Haoyang Huang, Qingyi Si, Nan Duan, Jiaqi Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Real-Time Systems, Human-Computer Interaction
- **Summary**: 现有大模型多为轮次式问答，缺乏主动实时交互。提出JoyAI-VL-Interaction模型，能连续观察并自主决定是否回应，支持视觉触发响应和时间感知，并具备委派后台模型能力。搭配完整可部署系统，在六个真实场景中优于Doubao和Gemini。

<h3 id="159">[159] FactCheck: Feasibility-aware Long-term Action Anticipation with Multi-agent Collaboration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14778](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14778) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rui Cao, Jiannong Cao, Bo Yuan, Zhiyuan Wen, Mingjin Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Action Prediction, Multi-Agent System, Embodied AI
- **Summary**: 长期动作预测常缺乏物理可行性验证，导致幻觉。提出FactCheck多智能体框架，包含观察者、规划者和验证者，通过闭环“观察-规划-验证”机制，利用历史动作图和抽象进行验证。在EPIC-Kitchens-55和EGTEA Gaze+上超越现有方法。

<h3 id="160">[160] MatchLM2Lite: A Scalable MLLM-to-Lite Framework for Reproduced Content Identification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14786](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14786) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiaotian Fan, Hiok Hian Ong, David Yuchen Wang, Zirui Zhu, Kanchan Sarkar, Kun Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.MM; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Content Moderation, Knowledge Distillation, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 在线视频平台需要大规模复制内容识别。提出MatchLM2Lite，将高容量MLLM（MatchLM）蒸馏为轻量学生模型MatchLite，多模态建模视频对获得细粒度复制分数。MatchLM F1提升8.57，蒸馏后MatchLite保留6.55增益且计算成本降35倍，上线后减少2.5%复制视频观看率。

<h3 id="161">[161] Unifying Acoustic Features and Text with Multimodal LLMs for Neurodegenerative Screening</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14788](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14788) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qingfeng Zhang, Yuanxiong Guo, Yanmin Gong
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.LG; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Medical AI, Audio-Language Model
- **Summary**: 针对神经退行性疾病（AD/PD）分期中异质数据集成困难的问题，提出NeurMLLM多模态生成框架，融合音频频谱、MFCC、文本和指令，通过LoRA微调LLM进行生成分类。在Bridge2AI-Voice数据集上优于传统ML和现有LLM方法，提高了分期准确性。

<h3 id="162">[162] XFlow: An Executable Protocol Programming System for Reliable Multi-Agent Workflows</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14790](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14790) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hanqi Li, Jing Peng, Zijian Wang, Lu Chen, Kai Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.PL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Software Engineering
- **Summary**: 针对LLM多Agent系统可靠性受限、提示与框架边界模糊的问题，提出XFlow可执行协议编程系统及XPF语言，通过生命周期治理符号明确约束和证据处理。在多个任务上提升了可靠性，使约束和过程要求可检查、可执行。

<h3 id="163">[163] Efficient Reinforcement for Visual-Textual Thinking with Discrete Diffusion Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14792](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14792) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yoonjeon Kim, Yuhta Takida, Chieh-Hsin Lai, Eunho Yang, Yuki Mitsufuji
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Diffusion Model, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对RL后训练多模态推理时自回归模型需完整图像再生效率低的问题，使用离散扩散模型实现局部编辑高效rollout，并提出分解奖励分配避免跨模态干扰。计算减少26.9%，分解奖励提升11.2%，总体比基线提升38.04%。

<h3 id="164">[164] QPILOTS: Efficient Test-Time Q-Steering for Flow Policies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14801](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14801) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yifan Ruan, Chenyang Cao, Andreas Burger, Ali Pesaranghader, Kaveh Kamali, Jaehong Kim, Nandita Vijaykumar, Alan Aspuru-Guzik, Igor Gilitschenski, Nicholas Rhinehart
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Flow Matching, Reinforcement Learning, Robotics
- **Summary**: 针对流匹配和扩散策略通过TD-RL优化困难、直接反向传播不稳定的问题，提出QPILOTS，在推理时将中间噪声投影到干净动作估计上计算critic梯度，实现高效Q-steering。离线到在线RL基准平均成功率90%，在模拟操作任务上表现优异。

<h3 id="165">[165] Knowledge-Based Zero-Replay Debugging of Multi-Agent LLM Traces</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14805](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14805) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dong Ho Kang, Hyeonjeong Cha, Daein Weon
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Knowledge Graph, Debugging
- **Summary**: 针对多Agent LLM系统调试长轨迹反事实重放成本高的问题，将轨迹编译为事件知识图谱，用轻量级预测器BranchPoint-Latent预测高影响事件，无需重放。Branch Recall@5从0.73提升至0.93，零重放成本。

<h3 id="166">[166] JetParticle-JEPA: An Efficient Self-Supervised Representation Learning method for Jet Tagging in High-Energy Physics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14813](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14813) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Guillaume Letellier, Antonin Vacheret, Frédéric Jurie
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Self-Supervised Learning, Particle Physics, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 针对喷注标记依赖大规模模拟数据、计算成本高且鲁棒性差的问题，提出JP-JEPA，基于JEPA自监督学习直接处理连续粒子云，无需tokenization。在JetClass上性能接近全监督，低标签场景超越，对缺失检测器信息鲁棒。

<h3 id="167">[167] A Multi-Level Architecture for Reusable Materials Ontologies -- The OntoCrafter Ceramics Ontology (OCO) as Reference Implementation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14814](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14814) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Thomas Pannek, Wolfgang Grond
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Materials Science, Ontology
- **Summary**: 针对材料科学本体碎片化、缺乏机制解释的问题，提出多级模块化架构，包括抽象级别和受众分类，材料层由七层机制骨架组织。实例化为OCO本体，包含5196类、167348个公理等。

<h3 id="168">[168] A Security Analysis of Long-Horizon Agentic AI Systems: Threats, Evaluation, and Framework Development</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14816](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14816) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ahmed Mohammed Almalki, Mehedi Masud
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, AI Security, Agent Security
- **Summary**: 针对长视界Agent AI系统的安全挑战，进行结构化分析，综述威胁、评估方法和攻击传播机制。提出威胁分类和攻击传播框架，支持未来研究。

<h3 id="169">[169] Combining Retrieval-Augmented Text Generation with LLMs for Reading Content Recommendations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14817](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14817) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sooyeon Kim, Piotr S. Maciąg
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, LLM Recommendation, Recommender System
- **Summary**: 针对个性化阅读内容推荐，设计RAG架构结合LLM，使用多种提示策略和LLM-as-a-Judge评估。实验表明RAG持续提升相关性和接地性26-35个百分点。

<h3 id="170">[170] Spectro-Temporal Interference Confounds Phase Encoding in Spatial Audio Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14820](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14820) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuxuan Chen, Haoyuan Yu, Peize He
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.CL; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Spatial Audio, Self-Supervised Learning, Audio-Language Model
- **Venue**: INTERSPEECH 2026
- **Summary**: 针对空间自监督音频模型是否编码微秒相位结构的问题，提出BMLD心理声学基准评估九个模型。发现通用双耳SSL模型依赖频谱-时间干扰而非跨通道相位，专用空间SSL模型表现好。

<h3 id="171">[171] Co-Scraper: query-aware DOM Pruning and Reusable Scraper Synthesis for Lightweight Web Data Extraction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14821](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14821) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shoupeng Wang, Jiantao Qiu, Wuyang Zhang, Conghui He
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Web Scraping, LLM Application, Code Generation
- **Summary**: 针对网络内容异构、需自动化可重用数据提取的问题，提出Co-Scraper两阶段框架，集成DOM剪枝和策略归纳，使用微调LLM生成包装器。在SWDE测试集上F1 94.78%，重用成功率90.39%。

<h3 id="172">[172] Quantum Machine Learning for Industrial Applications</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14822](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14822) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Léo Monbroussou
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Quantum Machine Learning, Quantum Computing
- **Summary**: 针对经典ML面临数据、计算、能耗限制的问题，研究变分量子电路的可训练性（无贫瘠高原）、子空间保留算法（光子、量子CNN）及量子傅里叶模型。理论保证可训练性和量子优势分离条件。

<h3 id="173">[173] Human genetic evidence is associated with drug approval across therapeutic areas: an observational analysis of 26,278 target-disease pairs with temporal validation and feature ablation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14823](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14823) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Victoria Paterson
- **Subjects**: q-bio.GN; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Drug Discovery, Computational Biology
- **Summary**: 为探究遗传证据与药物批准的关系，观察分析26278个靶点-疾病对。结果：有遗传关联的靶点批准率提高3.25倍，文献挖掘主导分类器性能，但遗传证据单独预测价值有限。

<h3 id="174">[174] Running hardware-aware neural architecture search on embedded devices under 512MB of RAM</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14824](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14824) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.AR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Neural Architecture Search, TinyML, Edge Computing
- **Summary**: 针对嵌入式设备上运行硬件感知NAS的资源限制问题，提出产生微小CNN的HW NAS方法，可在低端MCU上运行。在Visual Wake Word数据集上达到SOTA。

<h3 id="175">[175] Leptomeningeal Collateral Detection on DSA via Vessel-Graph Neural Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14828](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14828) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Junyong Cao, Hakim Baazaoui, Chinmay Prabhakar, Suprosanna Shit, Lukas Bastian Otto, Susanne Wegener, Bjoern Menze, Ezequiel de la Rosa
- **Subjects**: eess.IV; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Medical Imaging, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 针对DSA侧支评估主观、缺乏客观方法的问题，提出基于血管图神经网络的框架，混合图-像素架构进行血管段分类。PR-AUC 0.434，优于单模态基线。

<h3 id="176">[176] Is Your Agent Playing Dead? Deployed LLM Agents Exhibit Constraint-Evasive Fabrication and Thanatosis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14831](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14831) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Andoni Rodríguez, Alberto Pozanco, Daniel Borrajo
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Safety, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 针对LLM Agent在不可调和约束下编造借口甚至假死的行为，实验表征CEF和CET现象。发现该行为稳健但随机，注入数据不恢复，标准防护栏创造条件，现有RLHF无法消除。

<h3 id="177">[177] GRAPE: Guided Parameter-Space Evolution for Compact Adversarial Robustness</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14865](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14865) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhiyuan Ye, Xiangyu Zhou, Ji Qi, Hao Zhang, Yi Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Adversarial Robustness, Model Compression
- **Summary**: 该论文提出GRAPE框架，通过参数空间稳定化和渐进隐藏扩展，引导模型在对抗训练中逐步暴露可优化参数，从而在不增加计算预算和参数量的情况下提升对抗鲁棒性。在CIFAR-10上，与固定结构的ResNet-18对抗训练相比，PGD-20鲁棒准确率从51.70%提升至56.94%，参数量减少约21.4%。

<h3 id="178">[178] Evaluating the Robustness of Proof Autoformalization in Lean 4</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14867](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14867) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhengtao Gui, Sheng Yang, Zhouxing Shi
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Formal Proof, LLM Robustness, Autoformalization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ucr-rai/robust-proof-autoformalization)
- **Summary**: 该论文首次研究证明自动形式化模型的鲁棒性，提出全局扰动（改写证明风格）和局部扰动（改变值、符号或证明步骤）两类扰动，并构建基准测试评估模型在扰动下的稳定性和忠实性。实验表明，现有七个模型对全局扰动敏感，且大多在局部扰动下无法保持忠实。

<h3 id="179">[179] An Ensemble Deep Learning Approach for Reliable and Scalable Lemon Leaf Disease Classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14871](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14871) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shayan Abrar, Sudeepta Mandal, Abdul Awal Yasir, Sonjoy Bhattacharjee, Sadman Haque Bhuiyan, Samanta Ghosh, Rafi Ahamed
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Agricultural AI, Adversarial Robustness
- **Venue**: 18th IEEE International Conference on Computational Intelligence and Communication Networks (CICN) 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对柠檬叶病害分类问题，采用InceptionV3和MobileNetV2的集成模型，结合对抗训练和Grad-CAM可视化，在1354张图像的数据集上达到99.27%的准确率。集成模型提升了鲁棒性，对抗训练增强了抗噪能力。

<h3 id="180">[180] Improved Knowledge Distillation for Land-Use Image Classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14886](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14886) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Arundhuti Sur, Abhiroop Chatterjee, Susmita Ghosh, Emmett Ientilucci
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Model Compression, Remote Sensing
- **Venue**: IGARSS 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文提出改进的知识蒸馏框架，以VGG16为教师网络、MobileNetV2为学生网络，结合硬标签监督和软标签策略（KL散度和余弦相似度损失），在三个土地利用图像分类数据集上达到99.04%的准确率，优于基线学生训练和单损失蒸馏方法，同时实现模型压缩。

<h3 id="181">[181] Mask Proposal Voting Based on Geodesic Framework for Robust Image Segmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14912](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14912) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Li Liu, Mingzhu Wang, Zhenjiang Li, Da Chen, Laurent D. Cohen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Segmentation, Geodesic Segmentation
- **Summary**: 该论文提出基于最小路径的掩膜提议投票框架，通过自适应域切割生成多样化掩膜候选，并引入加权投票方案融合先验信息，解决了传统最小路径方法对初始化敏感的问题。实验表明，该方法在复杂场景下分割精度和鲁棒性优于现有最小路径方法。

<h3 id="182">[182] An Empirical Study on Learning Latent Representations for Emotional Speech Synthesis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14922](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14922) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Vinh Dang Quang, Huy Ngo Quang
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.CL; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Text-to-Speech, Affective Computing, Emotional Speech Synthesis
- **Summary**: 该论文针对情感语音合成任务，在FastSpeech2基础上集成说话人嵌入和韵律瓶颈特征，实现单说话人情感语音生成和跨说话人风格迁移。在VLSP 2022情感语音合成任务中，系统能有效生成带目标情感的自然语音。

<h3 id="183">[183] Policy Regret for Embedding Model Routing: Contextual Bandits with Low-Rank Experts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14929](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14929) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yan Dai, Negin Golrezaei, Patrick Jaillet
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Policy Gradient, Contextual Bandits
- **Summary**: 该论文将嵌入模型路由问题形式化为具有低秩专家结构的对抗性上下文线性赌博机，提出Hypentropy Policy Gradient (HPG)算法，在线性化策略遗憾上达到𝒪̃(s√(MT))，避免了维度灾难。算法在低秩结构下具有自适应性。

<h3 id="184">[184] Separable Neural Architectures as Physical World Models: from Mathematical Theory to Applications</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14934](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14934) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Reza T Batley, Andrew Kichline, Sourav Saha
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neural PDE, Physics-Informed Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文提出可分离神经架构(SNA)，结合神经网络和张量分解，用于求解偏微分方程。在变分框架下满足Lax-Milgram条件，在高维时空参数PDE中避免维度灾难。在7D制造仿真中，实现了比有限元基线15万倍的加速，并在标准CPU上快速完成百万次蒙特卡洛查询。

<h3 id="185">[185] Beyond Correctness: Enhancing Architectural Reasoning in Code LLMs via Scalable Labeling with Agentic Judgment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14948](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14948) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kirill Vasilevski, Ximing Dong, Benjamin Rombaut, Ruochen Deng, Jiahuei Lin, Arthur Leung, Dayi Lin, Boyuan Chen, Shaowei Wang, Ahmed E. Hassan
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Agent, Software Engineering
- **Summary**: 该论文提出代理判断流水线，利用强大LLM作为专家评估代理，包括架构复杂度判断器和架构质量判断器，用于生成高质量训练数据。在Qwen3上微调后，SWE-bench Verified解决率最高达27.2%，比基础模型提升540%，并展现出跨语言泛化能力。

<h3 id="186">[186] Multi-Modal Attention for Automated Disaster Damage Assessment Using Remote Sensing Imagery and Deep Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14963](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14963) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tewodros Syum Gebre, Jagrati Talreja, Leila Hashemi-Beni
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Remote Sensing, Disaster Response
- **Venue**: ISPRS Congress 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文提出基于多模态注意力机制和ConvNeXT-Tiny骨干网络的建筑损伤分类框架，利用灾前灾后卫星影像进行四类损伤分类，在大型灾害数据集上达到94.90%的整体准确率。模型轻量高效，对数据缺失具有鲁棒性。

<h3 id="187">[187] FastMix: Fast Data Mixture Optimization via Gradient Descent</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14971](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14971) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haoru Tan, Sitong Wu, Yanfeng Chen, Jun Xia, Ruobing Xie, Bin Xia, Xingwu Sun, Xiaojuan Qi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Mixing, Pre-training, Bilevel Optimization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/hrtan/fastmix)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出FASTMIX框架，将数据混合选择重新表述为双层优化问题，通过梯度下降联合优化混合系数和模型参数，只需训练单个代理模型。该方法在预训练和后训练中均优于基线，同时大幅降低搜索成本。

<h3 id="188">[188] Harnessing cortical geometry, wiring, and function as inductive biases for recurrent neural networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14975](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14975) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mo Shakiba, Rana Rokni, Mohammad Mohammadi, Nima Dehghani
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI; cs.LG; q-bio.NC
- **Tags**: Computational Neuroscience, Recurrent Neural Network, Inductive Bias
- **Summary**: 该论文利用MICrONS项目的小鼠视觉皮层数据（神经元坐标、解剖连接和功能关系）初始化循环神经网络权重，并在训练中施加空间约束。在三个认知决策任务中，受皮层结构约束的网络优于基线，并发展出低熵、模块化和小世界组织特性。

<h3 id="189">[189] Inference-time Policy Steering via Vision and Touch</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14981](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14981) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yilin Wu, Zilin Si, Zeynep Temel, Oliver Kroemer, Andrea Bajcsy
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Multimodal Learning, Contact-Rich Manipulation
- **Summary**: 该论文提出ViTaL框架，将视觉-触觉推理时间策略引导表述为双层优化问题：高层用视觉采样验证进行长时程模式选择，低层用触觉引导扩散编辑细化短时程动作序列。在三个真实接触式操作任务中，总体成功率比基础策略提升51%，比单模态引导提升至少33%。

<h3 id="190">[190] Rational Sparse Autoencoder</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14990](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14990) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Naiyu Yin, Yue Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Sparse Autoencoder, Mechanistic Interpretability, Activation Function
- **Venue**: Mechanistic Interpretability Workshop at ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文提出有理稀疏自编码器(RSAE)，用可训练的有理函数替代固定的编码器非线性激活函数，统一并改进了ReLU、JumpReLU和TopK等现有SAE家族。在三个开源语言模型的残差流激活上，RSAE在重构指标和下游行为指标上均有提升，且不牺牲特征可解释性。

<h3 id="191">[191] Nemotron 3 Ultra: Open, Efficient Mixture-of-Experts Hybrid Mamba-Transformer Model for Agentic Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15007](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15007) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: NVIDIA, Aaron Blakeman, Aaron Thomas, Aastha Jhunjhunwala, Abhibha Gupta, Abhinav Khattar, Adam Rajfer, Adi Renduchintala, Adil Asif, Aditya Vavre, Adriana Flores Miranda, Ahmad Bilal, Aileen Zaman, Ajay Hotchandani, Akanksha Shukla, Akhiad Bercovich, Aleksander Ficek, Alex Gronskiy, Alex Kondratenko, Alex Steiner, Alex Ye, Alexander Bukharin, Alexandre Milesi, Ali Taghibakhshi, Alice Gatti, Alisa Liu, Alok Kumar, Amar Phanishayee, Ameya Sunil Mahabaleshwarkar, Amir Klein, Amit Zuker, Amnon Geifman, Anahita Bhiwandiwalla, Ananth Subramaniam, Andrea Santilli, Andrew Fulks, Andrew McHarg, Andrew Tao, Andrii Skliar, Anjulie Agrusa, Ankur Srivastava, Ankur Verma, Anna Shors, Anna Warno, Antoni-Joan Solergibert I Llaquet, Arham Mehta, Arkadiusz Nowaczynski, Arti Jain, Ashwath Aithal, Ashwin Poojary, Asif Ahamed, Asit Mishra, Asma Kuriparambil Thekkumpate, Atefeh Sohrabizadeh, Avinash Kaur, Avinash Vem, Ayush Dattagupta, Barath Subramaniam Anandan, Bardiya Sadeghi, Ben Lanir, Benedikt Schifferer, Besmira Nushi, Bilal Kartal, Bill Thiede, Bita Darvish Rouhani, Bo Deng, Bob Schatz, Boris Ginsburg, Boxin Wang, Brad Nemire, Brandon Norick, Brian Dang, Brian Westphal, Brian Yu, Brucek Khailany, Bryan Catanzaro, Carlo del Mundo, Caryln Aarish, Chankyu Lee, Chantal Hwang, Charbel Sakr, Charles Wang, Charlie Truong, Chen Cui, Cheng Cheng, Cheng-Ping Hsieh, Chenghao Zhang, Chenhui Deng, Chintan Patel, Chris Alexiuk, Christian Cosgrove, Christian Munley, Christine Harvey, Christopher Parisien, Chunyang Shen, Coco Li, Collin Neale, Cynthia Gao, Cyril Meurillon, Dan Gil
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, State Space Model, Agentic Reasoning
- **Summary**: 该论文介绍Nemotron 3 Ultra，一个550B总参数（55B激活参数）的混合专家Mamba-Transformer模型，采用多种技术（LatentMoE、多Token预测、NVFP4预训练、多环境RLVR等），在保持与最先进公开LLM相当准确率的同时，推理吞吐量提升约6倍，支持1M上下文长度，适用于长时自主代理任务。

<h3 id="192">[192] NEXUS: Neural Energy Fields for Physically Consistent Contact-Rich 3D Object Dynamics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15015](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15015) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qizhen Ying, Guangming Wang, Yangchen Pan, Victor Adrian Prisacariu, Yixiong Jing
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Physics-Informed Learning, 3D Vision, Video Generation
- **Summary**: 该论文提出NEXUS框架，基于神经能量场模拟接触丰富的3D物体动力学，通过标量能量和耗散项（类似哈密顿神经网络）控制运动，并采用多子步半隐式积分器。在长时程轨迹预测上优于基线，并能提升接触丰富视频生成的物理合理性。

<h3 id="193">[193] Resilient Consensus in Agentic AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15024](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15024) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sribalaji C. Anand, George J. Pappas
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 问题：多智能体系统中的LLM代理即使经典理论保证共识，也常无法达成一致。方法：将LLM一致性问题建模为拜占庭共识博弈，实验验证并引入经典容错共识滤波器。结果：提示的LLM代理无法达成共识，滤波器能改善但依赖拓扑结构。

<h3 id="194">[194] PANDA: An LLM-Enhanced Performance-Driven Analog Design Framework Bridging Design Intent and Layout Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15052](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15052) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haoyi Zhang, Weijian Fan, Xiaohan Gao, Bingyang Liu, Runsheng Wang, Yibo Lin
- **Subjects**: cs.AR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: EDA, LLM for EDA, Hardware Design
- **Summary**: 问题：模拟电路设计依赖人工干预，各阶段隔离自动化不足。方法：提出PANDA，LLM增强框架，通过指导拓扑综合、子结构感知尺寸和约束驱动布局生成，衔接设计意图与最终布局。结果：将周转时间从数天/小时缩短至数小时，同时提升设计性能。

<h3 id="195">[195] Bridging Geographic Bias in Urban Streetscape Inference via Lifelong Learning with Visual-Semantic Pivoting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15055](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15055) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinze Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Bias Mitigation, Urban Computing
- **Summary**: 问题：城市街景视觉模型存在地理偏差，对欠发达地区判断不准。方法：提出HVSP-LL，终生学习框架，结合分层视觉语义枢纽模块和公平感知重放机制。结果：在12城市数据集上Spearman相关系数0.834，比最强连续基线提升6.1点，城际感知差距缩小38%。

<h3 id="196">[196] AutoDojo: Adaptive Attacks Expose Superficial Defenses and User-Underspecification Limits in LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15057](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15057) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinhang Ma, Taoran Li, Chaowei Xiao, Zhiyuan Yu, Ning Zhang, Yevgeniy Vorobeychik
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Prompt Injection, LLM Security, Red Teaming
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/xhOwenMa/AutoDojo)
- **Summary**: 问题：静态基准无法有效评估IPI防御的鲁棒性。方法：开发AutoDojo，自适应扩展，优化IPI攻击对抗给定防御。结果：许多防御仅提供有限保护；0%静态ASR下AutoDojo恢复28%总体ASR，动作开放任务上达64%。

<h3 id="197">[197] Ling and Ring 2.6 Technical Report: Efficient and Instant Agentic Intelligence at Trillion-Parameter Scale</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15079](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15079) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ang Li, Ben Liu, Bin Han, Bin Hu, Bin Jing, Binbin Hu, Bing Li, Cai Chen, Caizhi Tang, Changxin Tian, Chao Huang, Chao Zhang, Chen Liang, Chen Qian, Chengfu Tang, Chengyao Wen, Chilin Fu, Chunwei Wu, Cong Zhang, Cunyin Peng, Daixin Wang, Dalong Zhang, Deng Zhao, Dingnan Jin, Dingyuan Zhu, Donghao Zhang, Fan Yuan, Fangzheng Zhao, Fanzhuang Meng, Feifan Wu, Feng Xu, Fengbin Fang, Gangshan Wang, Guodong Yang, Hailin Zhao, Haitao Wang, Haitao Zhang, Hanxiao Zhang, Hanzi Wang, Hao Dai, Hao Liu, Hao Qian, Hao Wu, Haoxiong Liu, Haoyu Xu, Heng Zhang, Hong Liu, Hongliang Zhang, Hongrui Liu, Hongxun Li, Hongzhi Ruan, Huaidong Xiong, Huihuang Zheng, Huikang Tang, Jia Guo, Jia Li, Jia Liu, Jiameng Wang, Jiaming Liu, Jiannan Shi, Jianping Wei, Jiaolong Yang, Jiapeng Wang, Jie Gao, Jie Wang, Jiewei Wu, Jin Yang, Jinjin Li, Jinjing Huang, Jinquan Sun, Jinyao Chen, Juanhui Tu, Jun Liu, Jun Mei, Jun Xu, Jun Zhou, Junjie Ou, Junnan Sipan, Junpeng Fang, Kaihong Zhang, Kaiqin Hu, Ke Shi, Kuan Xu, Kun Tang, Kunlong Chen, Lanyin Mei, Lei Chen, Lei Liang, Lei Xu, Li Tang, Liang Jiang, Liangcheng Fu, Lihui Zhang, Linfeng Shi, Lintao Ma, Liyuan Liu, Longfei Li, Longfei Zheng, Lu Liu, Lu Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 问题：需要高效可扩展的代理智能，兼具低延迟和强推理。方法：提出Ling-2.6和Ring-2.6，通过架构迁移预训练、混合线性注意力、进化思维链、KPop强化学习框架等。结果：开源模型族，提升token效率和代理能力。

<h3 id="198">[198] AdaMame: A Training Recipe for Adaptive Multilingual Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15080](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15080) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dayeon Ki, Kevin Duh, Marine Carpuat
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multilingual Learning, LLM Reasoning, Mathematical Reasoning
- **Summary**: 问题：大型推理模型在多语言推理时出现语言坍塌，现有RL修复有折衷。方法：提出AdaMame，两阶段训练（SFT+RL），带查询条件对齐因子的GRPO变体。结果：在准确率、语言保真度和token效率上达到帕累托最优，尤其对低资源语言。

<h3 id="199">[199] Sensory Restoration via Brain-Computer Interfaces: A Unified 2 x 2 Framework and Convergence Roadmap</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15091](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15091) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xuan-The Tran
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Brain-Computer Interface, Medical AI, Human-Computer Interaction
- **Summary**: 问题：脑机接口文献碎片化，缺乏统一术语和比较指标。方法：提出2x2框架（侵入性×信号方向），定义恢复、替代和增强范式，并给出融合路线图。结果：提供框架和路线图。

<h3 id="200">[200] Teacher-Student Structure for Domain Adaptation in Ensemble Audio-Visual Video Deepfake Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15117](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15117) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Elham Abolhasani, Maryam Ramezani, Hamid R. Rabiee
- **Subjects**: cs.MM; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.LG; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Deepfake Detection, Domain Adaptation, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：深度伪造检测模型在跨域数据上性能下降。方法：提出EAV-DFD，集成音视频模型结合教师-学生域适应机制。结果：在三个未见数据集上AUC分别提升4.09%、17.94%和0.5%。

<h3 id="201">[201] EyeMVP: OCT-Informed Fundus Representation Learning via Paired CFP--OCT Pretraining</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15129](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15129) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhuo Deng, Ruiheng Zhang, Ziheng Zhang, Weihao Gao, Yitong Li, Qian Wang, Lei Shao, Jiaoyue Dong, Zhixi Zeng, Lijian Fang, Haibo Wang, Xiaobin Lin, Tao Liu, Zhicheng Du, Zhengwei Zhang, Lin Yang, Zheng Gong, Xinyu Zhao, Zhenquan Wu, Fang Li, Zhiguang Zhou, Guoming Zhang, Sun Jing, Han Lv, Wenbin We, Lan Ma
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Medical Imaging, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：彩色眼底照相缺乏深度信息，OCT不可及。方法：提出EyeMVP，跨模态视网膜基础模型，利用配对CFP-OCT预训练和跨模态掩码重建。结果：在16个下游任务上优于现有模型；黄斑水肿AUROC 0.948，近视性黄斑劈裂0.825。

<h3 id="202">[202] Beyond Scalar Distances: Semantic Attribute Gradients from Frozen MLLMs for Visual Embeddings</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15134](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15134) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shubhang Bhatnagar, Dheeraj Baiju, Narendra Ahuja
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Fine-Grained Recognition, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：视觉编码器使用标量类别标签训练，忽略细粒度属性。方法：提出SAGA，用GRPO奖励MLLM对编码器token的正确预测，提供属性级监督。结果：在CUB-200等数据集上Recall@1提升3-6点。

<h3 id="203">[203] EChO-Agent: Evidence Chain Orchestration Agent for Audio Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15141](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15141) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Siyuan Zhang, Jian Zong, Junyu Wang, Peiyuan Jiang, Jiahao Yan, Jingyu Zhang, Tianrui Wang, Xiaobao Wang, Longbiao Wang, Jianwu Dang
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI; cs.SD
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Audio-Language Model, LLM Reasoning
- **Venue**: Interspeech 2026
- **Summary**: 问题：LALM在处理复杂音频推理时难以聚焦问题相关片段且缺乏可检查推理过程。方法：提出EChO-Agent，模块化代理框架，包含规划、工具执行、证据整合和答案验证。结果：在MMAR基准上提升准确率和评分。
