<h3 id="204">[204] PACUTE: Phonology-, Affix-, and Character-level Understanding of Tokens for Filipino</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15144](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15144) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jann Railey Montalan, David Demitri Africa, Jimson Paulo Layacan, Richell Isaiah Flores, Ivan Yuri De Leon, Lance Calvin Gamboa
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Linguistic Resource, Benchmark, Multilingual Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：LLM的subword分词掩盖词素结构，特别是非黏着语。方法：提出PACUTE，4600个任务诊断基准，评估菲律宾语的形态理解。结果：开源模型在词素分解上接近随机，前沿模型更好但仍低于字符级上限。

<h3 id="205">[205] MimicIK: Real-Time Generative Inverse Kinematics from Teleoperation with FK Consistency</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15148](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15148) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiahao Yang, Shenhao Yan, Fan Feng, Chengsi Yao, Ge Wang, Zhixin Mai, Yiming Zhao, Yatong Han
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robotics, Imitation Learning, Motion Planning
- **Summary**: 问题：逆运动学是机器人实时操控的瓶颈，经典方法有振荡，学习法难平衡精度和平滑性。方法：提出MimicIK，基于条件流匹配的生成式IK，含FK一致性损失。结果：平均位置误差4.65mm，成功率92.01%，延迟6.74ms，20Hz实时控制。

<h3 id="206">[206] PolyKV: Heterogeneous Retention and Allocation for KV Cache Compression</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15157](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15157) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chao Fei, Panos Kalnis
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: KV Cache, Model Compression, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 问题：KV缓存压缩通常对所有层使用单一策略和均匀预算，忽略层间差异。方法：提出PolyKV，逐层优化，选择压缩方法和非均匀预算分配。结果：在LLaMA-3.1-8B上恢复54.5%的FullKV差距，1.7%-6.4%提升。

<h3 id="207">[207] Enabling Real-Time Point-of-Care Ultrasound Segmentation: A GPU-Free Deployment in Resource-Limited Settings</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15176](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15176) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Weihao Gao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Medical Imaging, Image Segmentation
- **Summary**: 问题：AI超声部署依赖GPU，成本高于设备本身。方法：提出UltraSeg超轻量架构（130K/500K参数），适配点超声。结果：CPU上89.7 FPS，移动设备34.8 FPS；Dice性能匹配31M参数UNet，零样本泛化更强。

<h3 id="208">[208] FreeSonic: Training-Free Temporal-Aware Decoupled Attention for Precise Audio Editing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15186](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15186) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuxuan Jiang, Mingyang Han, Yusheng Dai, Andong Wang, Tianhong Zhou, Jiaxin Ye, Dongxiao Wang, Haoxiang Shi, Boyu Li, Jun Song, Cheng Yu, Bo Zheng, Weibei Dou, Zehua Chen, Jun Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Text-to-Audio, Audio Editing, Diffusion Model
- **Venue**: Interspeech 2026
- **Summary**: 问题：音频编辑难以平衡时间一致性和背景保留。方法：提出FreeSonic，基于Rectified Flow和TangoFlux的无训练框架，利用优化反演和计划注意力解耦。结果：实现高保真、高效的音频编辑。

<h3 id="209">[209] StarOR: Synergizing Tree Search and Test-Time Reinforcement Learning for Optimization Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15197](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15197) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiajun Li, Yu Ding, Shisi Guan, Ran Hou, Wanyuan Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Monte Carlo Tree Search, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 现有优化建模方法依赖大规模标注数据或固定策略，难以适应新分布且早期错误易传播。提出StarOR框架，将建模过程分解为四阶段，通过GRPO更新LoRA适配器，利用MCTS生成的兄弟节点作为局部比较集，并设计无监督多面奖励。在5个优化基准上，4B骨干模型达到SOTA，超越前沿LLM。

<h3 id="210">[210] AI Contagion in Social Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15206](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15206) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Olivier Bos, Stefano Bosi
- **Subjects**: econ.TH; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Safety, Social Network Analysis, Complex Systems
- **Summary**: 研究AI与社交网络相互作用对集体知识稳定性的影响，构建包含AI传染渠道和社会扭曲乘数的模型。通过分析高维系统的二维表示，表征了稳定性所需的最小过滤监管边界，并揭示网络拓扑对系统信息风险的影响。

<h3 id="211">[211] Controlled Dynamics Attractor Transformer</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15207](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15207) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Cheng Zhang, Minnan Luo, Zesheng Yang, Ming Li, Yong-Jin Liu, Qinghua Zheng
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.NE
- **Tags**: Graph Anomaly Detection, Graph Classification, Energy-Based Model
- **Summary**: 传统Transformer缺乏生物合理性的吸引子动力学，提出CDAT，结合混合von Mises-Fisher注意力能量和Hopfield精炼能量，并加入CANN激发的兴奋-抑制调制。在图异常检测和图分类多个基准上达到SOTA。

<h3 id="212">[212] Spokes: Optimizing for Diverse Pretraining Data Selection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15216](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15216) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Clarence Lee, Yejin Choi, Luke Zettlemoyer, Pang Wei Koh, Hai Leong Chieu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Selection, Data Mixing, Pre-training
- **Summary**: 数据预选中的多样性难以直接优化，现有方法依赖近似。提出基于G-Vendi分数的概率多样化框架，通过指数梯度下降直接优化多样性。在FineWeb和DCLM上，联合优化质量和多样性比随机采样提升1.5和1.4个点。

<h3 id="213">[213] Edu-Theater: A Data-Efficient Agent Framework for Scalable Learner Behavior Simulation through Staging Roll-Call</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15225](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15225) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Weibo Gao, Qi Liu, Linan Yue, Zheng Zhang, Yichao Du, Fangzhou Yao, Ao Yu, Zhenya Huang, Shijin Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Education Technology, Data Synthesis
- **Summary**: 现有学习者模拟器依赖个体级密集历史，数据与计算开销大。提出队列感知的点名模拟范式，通过教师智能体和回顾性点名模拟，使用少量诊断查询。在真实数据集上实现更高模拟精度，降低LLM调用次数。

<h3 id="214">[214] Benign in Isolation, Harmful in Composition: Security Risks in Agent Skill Ecosystems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15242](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15242) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yi Xie, Jiawei Du, Yu Cheng, Jiuan Zhou, Zhaoxia Yin
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, AI Safety, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/saint-viperx/SCR_Bench)
- **Summary**: LLM智能体技能在组合使用时可能产生安全风险，而孤立评估无法发现。引入SCR-Bench，包含三个子基准评估技能组合风险。结果显示组合路径下攻击成功率大幅提升，如SCR-CapFlow达到33.6%，而孤立基线接近零。

<h3 id="215">[215] Provenance-Enhanced Statements in Knowledge Graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15246](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15246) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Fabio Vitali, Valentina Pasqual
- **Subjects**: cs.LO; cs.AI; cs.DL
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Knowledge Representation, Cognitive Science
- **Summary**: 知识图谱中出处语句的认知立场不明确，导致推理混淆。提出DEC框架，将出处谓词解释为认知立场，分组为认知世界，基于认知模态逻辑推理。在RDF数据集上实现受控事实化、分歧和错觉检测。

<h3 id="216">[216] Exploring Starts Are Not Enough: Counterexamples and a Fix for Monte Carlo Exploring Starts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15247](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15247) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Octave Oliviers, Glenn Vinnicombe
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Exploration, Monte Carlo Methods
- **Summary**: 蒙特卡洛探索开始（MCES）的收敛性长期未解。通过构建反例显示初始访问和首次访问MCES可能收敛到次优解。提出修正方法：按状态缩放学习率至更新频率倒数，保证收敛到最优。

<h3 id="217">[217] Landmark-free Assessment of Lower-limb Alignment with Implicit Neural Shape Functions from Knee Radiographs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15250](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15250) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhisen Hu, Antti Kemppainen, David Johnson, Egor Panfilov, Huy Hoang Nguyen, Timothy Cootes, Claudia Lindner, Aleksei Tiulpin
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Implicit Neural Representation
- **Venue**: MICCAI 2026
- **Summary**: 传统下肢对齐评估依赖人工定位关键点，耗时且不灵活。提出利用隐式神经形状函数编码解剖结构，直接回归临床测量。在内部和外部数据集上性能与基于关键点的SOTA方法相当。

<h3 id="218">[218] Driving, Fast or Slow? Neuro-Symbolic Guidance for Motion Prediction in Multi-Modal Ground Mobility</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15251](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15251) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Simon Kohaut, Felix Divo, Julius Hahnewald, Benedict Flade, Julian Eggert, Kristian Kersting, Devendra Singh Dhami
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Trajectory Prediction, Neurosymbolic AI, Autonomous Driving
- **Summary**: 现有运动预测模型缺乏对交通规则的显式编码。提出TraCS神经符号框架，通过代码生成和反应式推理引擎将自然语言规则转化为概率逻辑约束，并加入置信度调节。在Argoverse 2上一致提升SOTA预测骨干。

<h3 id="219">[219] Trust-Region Diffusion Policies for Massively Parallel On-Policy RL</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15260](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15260) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Huy Le, Onur Celik, Denis Blessing, Tai Hoang, Claas A Voelcker, Axel Brunnbauer, Felix Richter, Michael Volpp, Gerhard Neumann
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Reinforcement Learning, Policy Optimization
- **Summary**: 在线策略RL中扩散策略训练不稳定。提出TruDi，将扩散模型与信任区域优化结合，对完整扩散轨迹施加KL散度约束。在73个任务上一致优于基线，尤其是人形机器人控制任务。

<h3 id="220">[220] Guiding Federated Graph Recommendation with LLM-encoded knowledge</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15277](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15277) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Thi Minh Chau Nguyen, Hien Trang Nguyen, Duc Anh Nguyen, Van Ho-Long, Thanh Trung Huynh, Zhao Ren
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.DB; cs.ET; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Graph Neural Network, Recommender System
- **Summary**: 联邦图推荐中非IID客户端的图表示难以对齐。提出利用冻结LLM编码语义向量，引导服务器选择性聚合结构表示。实验证明语义引导的结构对齐持续提升推荐准确性。

<h3 id="221">[221] RECTOR: Masked Region-Channel-Temporal Modeling for Affective and Cognitive Representation Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15278](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15278) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jinhan Liu, Mahsa Shoaran
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: EEG Analysis, Self-Supervised Learning, Affective Computing
- **Summary**: 情感和认知障碍的EEG/sEEG表示学习受限于固定解剖先验。提出RECTOR自监督框架，通过自适应功能分区和掩码拓扑学习联合优化区域-通道-时间表示。在情感识别和任务分类上达到SOTA，且对缺失通道鲁棒。

<h3 id="222">[222] CAP: Towards PPG Universal Representation Learning with Patient-level Supervision</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15284](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15284) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chenyang He, Xinyi Shao, Shun Huang, Bosong Huang, Daoqiang Zhang, Ming Jing, Cheng Ding
- **Subjects**: eess.SP; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Representation Learning, Multimodal Learning
- **Venue**: KDD 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/gody123gody/CAP)
- **Summary**: PPG通用表示学习忽略患者级健康上下文。构建大规模PPG-EHR多模态数据集，提出CAP通过跨模态对比对齐预训练。在四个下游任务上平均相对提升26.7%，呼吸率预测提升87.6%。

<h3 id="223">[223] Hybrid NARX-LLM for Greenland Iceberg Discharge: Prompt-Driven Residual Correction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15288](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15288) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yiquan Gao, Duohui Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, LLM Application, Physics-Informed Learning
- **Summary**: 格陵兰冰山排放预测受限于非线性动力学和有限可观测性。提出混合NARX-LLM框架，用物理知识提示引导LLM修正残差。实验表明该框架有效捕捉极端事件，提升预测准确性。

<h3 id="224">[224] Discovering Lattice Reduction Strategies via Self-Play</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15301](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15301) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohamed Malhou, Kristin Lauter, Ludovic Perret
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Monte Carlo Tree Search, Combinatorial Optimization
- **Summary**: LLL算法在维度升高时输出基远离最优。将格约简建模为MDP，使用AlphaZero风格自对弈和自适应视野MCTS训练策略DeltaStar。仅在8维训练，零样本泛化到32维，且所需行操作少于LLL。

<h3 id="225">[225] LatentGym: A Testbed For Cross-Task Experiential Learning With Controllable Latent Structure</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15306](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15306) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Daksh Mittal, Tommaso Castellani, Thomson Yen, Naimeng Ye, Fangyu Wu, Minghui Chen, Tiffany Cai, Emmanouil Koukoumidis, William Zeng, Hongseok Namkoong
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 论文针对智能体在跨任务学习中无法有效利用共享隐含结构进行改进的问题，提出了LatentGym可控测试平台，每个环境围绕真实潜在变量组织，提供分离探索和利用的指标。实验发现前沿模型在跨任务适应上存在不足，后训练可提升跨任务适应，并揭示了设计选择对训练动态和泛化的影响。

<h3 id="226">[226] Adapting Reinforcement Learning with Chain-of-Thought Supervision for Explainable Detection of Hateful and Propagandistic Memes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15307](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15307) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohamed Bayan Kmainasi, Mucahid Kutlu, Ali Ezzat Shahroor, Abul Hasnat, Firoj Alam
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, LLM Reasoning, RLHF
- **Summary**: 针对恶意模因检测中多模态大语言模型应用不足的问题，提出基于强化学习的后训练方法，利用GRPO优化分类和解释质量，并扩展数据集添加弱监督思维链原理。在Hateful Memes和ArMeme基准上，FHM准确率提升至82.0%，ArMeme宏F1提升至0.612，同时生成自然语言解释。

<h3 id="227">[227] LLMs on Tabular Data with Limited Semantics: Evidence from Industrial Car Retrofit Prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15314](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15314) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aina Vila Pons, Ioannis Tzachristas, Constantinos Antoniou
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Tabular Learning, LLM Evaluation, LLM Application
- **Summary**: 论文研究在不含语义的工业表格数据上使用LLM的效果，比较了嵌入、直接提示和混合堆叠三种策略。结果表明经典树集成模型仍是最强的单独模型，LLM嵌入有用但直接提示在去除语义后崩溃，混合堆叠获得最佳多类模型。在隐私受限的工业表格上，LLM更适合作为补充组件。

<h3 id="228">[228] HoloRec: Holistic Encoding and Interleaved Reasoning for Generative Recommendation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15331](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15331) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shuqi Zhao, Jingsong Su, Xiang Liu, Xingzhi Yao, Yiming Qiu, Huimu Wang, Liang Lin, Pengbo Mo, Mingming Li, Jiao Dai, Jizhong Han, Songlin Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Generative Recommendation, LLM Reasoning, Recommender System
- **Summary**: 针对生成式推荐模型缺乏层次化表示和外部思维链注释的问题，提出HoloRec内生思维链推荐机制，通过多层次嵌套残差量化构建层次语义编码矩阵，支持非思考和思考两种推理模式。在多个公共推荐数据集上持续优于基线，稀疏场景下提升显著。

<h3 id="229">[229] Privacy-Preserving Text Sanitization for Distributed Agents Collaboration via Disentangled Representations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15335](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15335) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xuan Liu, Hefeng Zhou, Sicheng Chen, Chao Yang, Xingcheng Xu, Jingjing Qu, Jiong Lou, Jie LI, Xia Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Privacy, Multi-Agent System, Federated Learning
- **Summary**: 针对分布式智能体协作中文本隐私泄露不仅来自显式标识符还来自分布特征的问题，提出DiSan框架，使用双流编码器将文本分解为角色子空间和风格子空间，通过联邦原型对齐和对抗正则化训练。实验表明，DiSan将PII暴露降低20倍，同时保持83%的答案忠实度，并将恩隆风格归因降低70%以上。

<h3 id="230">[230] Intrinsic Computational Functionalism and Simulated Consciousness</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15348](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15348) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ryota Kanai, Shuqin Ma
- **Subjects**: q-bio.NC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Consciousness, Cognitive Science
- **Summary**: 论文从内在计算功能主义（ICF）角度反驳模拟意识不可能的观点，定义了机制增强的规范结构和内在因果-计算实现（ICCR），证明如果意识属性是内在因果-计算组织的不变量，那么满足ICCR的系统实现相同的意识相关属性。讨论了生物学自然主义和整合信息理论等反对意见。

<h3 id="231">[231] LearnOpt: Recovering the Latent Cognitive Structure of Standardized Examinations via Knowledge Graphs and Constrained Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15349](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15349) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Joy Bose, Om Thomas
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Education Technology, Optimization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/joyboseroy/learnopt)
- **Summary**: 论文认为标准化考试具有稳定的潜在认知结构并偏离官方大纲，提出LearnOpt从历史试卷恢复该结构并生成个性化学习计划。应用于NEET九年的试题，发现潜在技能分布在教学大纲制度内稳定，在制度变化时显著转移，且考试层级比科目和年份对结构影响更大。

<h3 id="232">[232] Cognitive Trajectory Modeling: Quantifying Human-AI Co-Creation through Cognitively Grounded Interaction Trajectories</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15358](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15358) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nicholas Davis
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Human-Computer Interaction, Cognitive Science, AI Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对共创造AI中交互动态难以捕捉高阶动力学的问题，提出认知轨迹建模（CTM），基于认知理论将交互视为随时间展开的认知轨迹。CTM提供了超越特定编码方案的通用框架，将认知轨迹与交互痕迹区分。论证理解共创造系统需要建模认知和交互动态随时间展开的方式。

<h3 id="233">[233] CoAgent: Concurrency Control for Multi-Agent Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15376](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15376) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hongtao Lyu, Dingyan Zhang, Mingyu Wu, Xingda Wei, Haibo Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Concurrency Control
- **Summary**: 针对多智能体LLM系统并发访问共享状态时经典并发控制机制不适配的问题，提出MTPO协议，利用LLM自身的判断能力进行通告性控制：运行时通知，智能体修复。在十个争用工作负载上，CoAgent在接近串行正确性的同时实现1.4倍加速和接近串行代币成本，优于2PL和OCC。

<h3 id="234">[234] Learning Earthquake Wave Arrival Time Picking from Labels with Inaccuracies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15377](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15377) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sen Li, Xu Yang, S. Mostafa Mousavi, Anye Cao, Keting Fan, Yaoqi Liu, Changbin Wang, Qiang Niu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Label Noise, Earthquake Detection
- **Summary**: 针对地震波到达时拾取任务中存在标签噪声的问题，提出LaNCoR方法，通过对齐输入波形特征和标签表示分布来纠正误标。在真实微地震数据P波拾取任务上，使用两个基线模型，LaNCoR将性能提升高达28.8%。

<h3 id="235">[235] Not All Skills Help: Measuring and Repairing Agent Knowledge</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15390](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15390) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yixuan Wang, Yiyang Zhou, Yiming Liang, Congyu Zhang, Fuxiao Liu, Jiawei Zhou, Huaxiu Yao
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Evaluation, Skill Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/aiming-lab/assay)
- **Summary**: 针对LLM智能体累积自然语言技能但部分技能有害的问题，提出ASSAY框架，通过随机掩码测量技能因果贡献，离线重组库，抑制负技能。在AppWorld和tau-bench上，DeepSeek-V3取得69.3%任务完成率（相对提升47.4%），GPT-4.1相对提升8.7%。消融实验显示主要增益来自每任务掩码。

<h3 id="236">[236] CHILLGuard: Towards Fine-Grained Chinese LLM Safety Guardrail with Scalable Data Construction and Model-aware Preference Alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15396](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15396) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wenbo Yu, Bohua Wang, Hao Fang, Kuofeng Gao, Jingru Zeng, Xiaochen Yang, Tianyi Zhang, Xiaoxiao Ma, Jiawei Kong, Hao Wu, Bin Chen, Shu-Tao Xia, Min Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Chinese LLM
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/cswbyu/CHILLGuard)
- **Summary**: 针对缺乏适应中文语境的细粒度安全护栏的问题，提出5宏31微细粒度风险分类法，构建CHILLGuard护栏。通过多阶段数据构建管道生成大规模训练集，并采用模型感知直接偏好优化进行训练。在基准上F1分数比Qwen3Guard-8B-Strict提升15.92%。

<h3 id="237">[237] T-Mem: Memory That Anticipates, Not Archives</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15405](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15405) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Weidong Guo, Dakai Wang, Zixuan Wang, Hui Liu, Yu Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Memory Architecture, Dialogue System, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 针对当前LLM长期对话记忆仅依赖描述性检索而忽略关联性检索的问题，提出T-Mem架构，通过写入时的预演（triggers）实现两种粒度的描述性和关联性回忆。在LoCoMo和LoCoMo-Plus上达到最先进水平。

<h3 id="238">[238] Few-Shot Biomedical Relation Extraction with Large Language Models: A Viable Alternative to Supervised Learning?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15412](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15412) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jakob Mraz, Tomaž Curk, Blaž Zupan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Few-Shot Learning, Information Extraction, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 针对生物医学关系抽取中监督模型依赖昂贵标注数据的问题，探究少样本设置下基于提示的LLM方法，比较了逐对分类和联合生成两种形式。最佳模型微F1为0.44，优于先前少样本结果但低于监督基线，但在宏F1上超过监督基线，尤其在罕见关系类型上。

<h3 id="239">[239] Let LLMs Judge Each Other: Multi-Agent Peer-Reviewed Reasoning for Medical Question Answering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15419](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15419) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zaifu Zhan, Shuang Zhou, Rui Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Reasoning, Medical AI
- **Venue**: Journal of the American Medical Informatics Association
- **Summary**: 为提高医疗问答中LLM的准确性、可解释性和鲁棒性，提出多智能体同行评审推理方法：多个LLM独立生成思维链推理，然后互相评估，选择最高评分推理链。在HeadQA、MedQA-USMLE和PubMedQA上，最佳模型组合平均准确率0.820，超过单独模型和多数投票。

<h3 id="240">[240] Constitutional Value Potentials: reading and steering internal priority margins in language models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15420](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15420) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tong Che, Rui Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Interpretability, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 针对宪法是否真正被模型遵循难以从输出判断的问题，提出宪法价值势（CVP），从隐藏状态学习标量势，表示模型内部保护某种价值的压力，势差即为优先级边界。监视器预测冲突违规的AUROC高达0.95，信号可在回答开始时读取并用于检测对抗性攻击。

<h3 id="241">[241] Post-Launch Capability Expansion of Vision-Language Models via Prompting for On-Orbit Spacecraft Inspection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15427](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15427) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nicholas A. Welsh, Lennon J. Shikhman, Monty Nehru Attazs, Seemanthini K. Putane, Van Minh Nguyen, Ryan T. White
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Zero-Shot Learning
- **Venue**: CVPR2026 Workshop on AI4Space
- **Summary**: 针对航天器在轨检测模型发射后无法更新的问题，提出利用基于提示的视觉语言模型（SAM3）通过自然语言提示进行零样本实例分割，无需修改权重。在129张未见过卫星图像上，对于大型结构如航天器主体（AP@0.50=0.639）和太阳翼（0.598）性能可靠，而天线（0.221）和推进器（0.081）等小部件困难，结构化提示最高提升82%。模型可在嵌入式GPU内存和计算范围内运行，为在轨语义扩展提供机制。

<h3 id="242">[242] Beyond Classification: A Cough Regression Benchmark for Respiratory Acoustic Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15436](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15436) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mayur Sanap, Prasanna Desikan, Edgar Lobaton
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Benchmark, Audio Processing
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 Workshop on Structured Data for Health
- **Summary**: 针对呼吸声学基础模型在连续健康量回归方面探索不足的问题，引入多模型、多目标的咳嗽回归基准，比较五种基础模型在六个目标上的性能。HeAR在年龄回归上最佳（Coswara数据集MAE=9.12年），在少量样本（N=50）下即可接近满性能，而OPERA模型需要400样本。跨数据集迁移存在强不对称性，大而多样的数据可推广至小临床人群，反之则困难。

<h3 id="243">[243] Defending against Adaptive Prompt Injection Attacks via Reasoning-enabled Task Alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15441](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15441) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lipeng He, Yihan Wang, Jiawen Zhang, N. Asokan
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Adversarial Robustness, Red Teaming
- **Summary**: 现有间接提示注入防御在自适应攻击下失效，因为仅依赖模式识别或有限模板。提出RETA，基于任务对齐的防御方法，通过思维链验证动作与用户任务一致，并利用模拟攻击者和多样性奖励进行多目标强化学习训练。在六种黑盒自适应攻击下，每个攻击的成功率均低于10%，平均ASR为2.92%和3.75%，同时保持可用性。

<h3 id="244">[244] Understanding Diversity Collapse in RLVR via the Lens of Overtraining</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15455](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15455) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Suqin Yuan, Jinkun Chen, Jiyang Zheng, Muyang Li, Lei Feng, Dadong Wang, Tao Xiang, Tongliang Liu, Bo An
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR）导致多样性崩溃——Pass@1提升但高k的Pass@k下降。从过训练角度形式化该问题，并提出贝叶斯边界门控（BBG），仅对零成功问题的更新进行优化。BBG在困难基准上将Pass@256提升至超过基础模型，并表明RLVR仍能扩展推理能力。

<h3 id="245">[245] Bayesian 3D Steerable CNNs: Enabling Equivariance and Uncertainty Quantification Simultaneously</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15479](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15479) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Abhishek Keripale, Ponkrshnan Thiagarajan, Susanta Ghosh
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.PR
- **Tags**: 3D Vision, Uncertainty Estimation, Equivariant Learning
- **Summary**: 传统可转向CNN保证SE(3)等变性但缺乏不确定性量化。提出贝叶斯可转向CNN，在基系数上放置后验分布，通过变分推断实现不确定性的量化。模型在保持竞争性精度的同时，预期校准误差为0.0263，在加性高斯噪声下比确定性版本高出6.17%的准确率，且认知不确定性与预测误差显著负相关。

<h3 id="246">[246] The Perils of Agency: How Developers Perceive, Prioritize, and Address Risks in Agentic AI Products</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15485](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15485) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hao-Ping Lee, Jessica He, David Piorkowski, Thomas Serban von Davier, Jodi Forlizzi, Sauvik Das
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.HC; cs.LG; cs.SE
- **Tags**: AI Safety, LLM Agent, Human-AI Interaction
- **Summary**: 智能体AI系统的自主性、工具使用等特点带来了独特的风险。通过对35名行业开发者的定性研究，发现开发者优先考虑产品/业务风险而非社会风险，并且缺乏成熟的控制手段，往往通过约束智能体能力来缓解风险，造成了能力与风险控制之间的张力。

<h3 id="247">[247] LLM4RTL: Tool-Assisted LLM for RTL Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15500](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15500) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jing Jin, Robert Chu, Ning Yan, Masood S. Mortazavi
- **Subjects**: cs.AR; cs.AI; eess.SY
- **Tags**: RTL Generation, Code Generation, Hardware Design
- **Summary**: LLM进行RTL代码生成需要高质量训练样本。提出JRCRC流水线，利用层次化商业LLM过滤和精炼样本，并设计预处理工具辅助LLM从表格数据推断逻辑关系。在VerilogEval基准上，使用更小的LLM达到了与GPT-4O相当的性能，超越多种现有方法。

<h3 id="248">[248] AQ4SViT: An Automated Quantization Framework with Search Gating Policy for Compressing Spiking Vision Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15523](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15523) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rachmad Vidya Wicaksana Putra, Saad Iftikhar, Muhammad Shafique
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Quantization, Spiking Neural Network
- **Summary**: 脉冲视觉Transformer（SViT）模型庞大，手动量化不可扩展。提出自动化量化框架AQ4SViT，采用搜索门控策略，利用膜电位漂移作为性能代理，支持贪婪搜索和波束搜索变体。贪婪搜索实现高达6.6倍加速和82.5%内存节省，波束搜索实现90%内存节省（4.5倍搜索时间），精度损失在1.5%以内。

<h3 id="249">[249] Selective Synergistic Learning for Video Object-Centric Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15527](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15527) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: WonJun Moon, Jae-Pil Heo
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Object-Centric Learning, Video Understanding, Representation Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/wjun0830/SSync)
- **Summary**: 视频对象中心学习方法中，编码器和解码器空间图存在误差传播，密集对齐计算量大。提出选择性协同学习（SSync），通过选择性蒸馏：编码器用于边界细化，解码器用于内部去噪，具有线性复杂度。SSync提升了分解质量，作为即插即用模块对槽配置具有鲁棒性。

<h3 id="250">[250] MADAR: An Address-Free Processor</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15535](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15535) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohamed Amine Bergach
- **Subjects**: cs.PF; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Hardware Architecture, Energy Efficiency, Hardware Acceleration
- **Summary**: 现代处理器的大部分面积和能耗用于地址操作。提出无地址处理器MADAR，所有状态在环中循环，指令和数据共同传输，编译时调度计算。在RTL实现和能耗模型中，对于AI加速，乘累加操作的能耗不随归约增加而增加，内存层次通过旋转实现标签式功能。

<h3 id="251">[251] AP-GRPO: Anchor-Gated Phonetic Alignment with Policy Optimization for Pathological Speech Reconstruction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15540](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15540) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pengfei Zhang, Hoang H Nguyen, Yutong Song, Wenjun Huang, Tahmid Imtiaz Imu, Henry Peng Zou, Jiang Wu, Honghui Xu, Amir M. Rahmani
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.MM; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Speech Processing, Medical AI, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 神经退行性疾病患者的病理语音扭曲且不完整，部分可靠单词可作为线索。提出AP-GRPO，使用锚点门控和音素对齐奖励，通过策略优化迫使语音语言模型保留可靠锚点并恢复中间内容。在四种疾病条件下均提升重建质量，并自动适应疾病特征。

<h3 id="252">[252] EcoBin: A Two-Stage Deep Convolutional Neural Network for Contamination-Aware Waste Classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15547](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15547) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Raghav Senthil Kumar
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Computer Vision, Image Classification, Data Synthesis
- **Summary**: 现有垃圾分类模型忽略回收物污染，且缺乏污染回收物数据集。提出EcoBin，两阶段深度CNN：第一阶段基于EfficientNetV2-S分类，第二阶段污染检测器（利用U2-Net分割和合成污染纹理）。第一阶段准确率87.42%，污染检测ROC-AUC 0.99，完整流水线在25个测试样本中正确分类24个，而基础分类器仅正确1个。

<h3 id="253">[253] CmdNeedle: Measuring the Incompleteness of Command Denylists for AI Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15549](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15549) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chuyang Chen, Zhiqiang Lin
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Security, Cybersecurity
- **Summary**: 终端AI代理的命令黑名单存在脆弱性，易被绕过。形式化黑名单脆弱性问题，提出LLM驱动的流水线CmdNeedle，通过提议和迭代修复旁路来检测脆弱性。在GitHub上收集的13,332条黑名单规则中，69.0-98.6%的黑名单存在脆弱性，且跨项目和代理一致。

<h3 id="254">[254] Distilling Drifting Transformers with Representation Autoencoders</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15553](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15553) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiawei Zhang, Mengfei Xia, Gen Li, Yuantao Gu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Diffusion Model, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 表征自编码器（RAE）的潜在空间各向异性严重，导致蒸馏不稳定。提出Drift-RAE，在RAE潜在空间中使用漂移模型蒸馏预训练流模型，并引入改进稳定训练。在ImageNet 256上取得1.77 FID（仅需10k蒸馏步），超越现有RAE蒸馏方法，与原始漂移模型竞争力相当。

<h3 id="255">[255] Service-Induced Congestion in Memory-Constrained LLM Serving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15555](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15555) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ruicheng Ao, Jing Dong, Gan Luo, David Simchi-Levi
- **Subjects**: math.OC; cs.AI; cs.LG; stat.ML
- **Tags**: LLM Serving, Performance Modeling, Memory Efficiency
- **Summary**: LLM服务中KV缓存内存增长导致服务引起的拥塞，高并发下内存压力内生性增加。建立离散时间动态模型，分析准入、内存增长和驱逐。同构负载下，无驱逐均衡不稳定，系统收敛至最坏情况极限周期，吞吐损失达50%。同质解码长度可能导致不稳定，而互质长度稳定。

<h3 id="256">[256] LLM-Assisted Stance Detection in Scientific Discourse: A Test Case in Bayesian Cognitive Science</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15566](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15566) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Eyup Engin Kucuk, Tarik Kelestemur, Ömer Dağlar Tanrikulu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Computational Social Science, Cognitive Science, Prompt Engineering
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/EyupEK/autoresearch_bayes)
- **Summary**: 社会科学中的定性编码难以规模化，特别是理论驱动的概念识别。结合理论代码本、专家注释和诊断门控提示优化，使用三个前沿LLM进行零样本立场检测。最终提示达到0.76的可靠性得分，引文层面ICC=0.80，文章层面秩相关r=0.96-0.97。语料库以弱现实主义为主，低层感知文章比高层认知文章现实主义得分高8.8分。

<h3 id="257">[257] Localizing Credit at the Divergence: Path-Conditioned Self-Distillation for LLM Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15576](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15576) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yu Li, Shu Hong, Tian Lan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Knowledge Distillation
- **Summary**: 强化学习从可验证奖励中仅获得单个标量，导致长推理轨迹中的令牌级信用分配不明确。本文提出Hindsight Self-Distillation (HSD)，通过将教师条件化在当前训练组中的一个成功同伴轨迹上，聚焦于失败轨迹与成功同伴之间的分歧位置提供密集信用信号。在数学和代码基准测试上，HSD在Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-32B上取得最佳结果，尤其在简洁答案任务（如AIME）上提升最大。

<h3 id="258">[258] Is Code Better Than Language for Algorithmic Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15589](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15589) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Terry Tong, Yu Feng, Surbhi Goel, Dan Roth
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Code Generation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/TerryTong-Git/ToolProj)
- **Summary**: 自然语言推理与代码执行管道之间的比较混淆了中间表示和执行机制。本文通过中间干预分离两者：模型用可执行代码表达推理，然后语言模型模拟代码生成答案。在40个可验证算法任务上，确定性代码执行比自然语言推理高出31.6个百分点，而中间表示本身的改变并无显著差异，表明可靠的外部执行是性能提升的关键。

<h3 id="259">[259] Pixels to Proofs: Probabilistically-Safe Latent World Model Control via Parallel Conformal Robust MPC</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15594](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15594) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Devesh Nath, Anutam Srinivasan, Haoran Yin, Ruitong Jiang, Jeffrey Fang, Glen Chou
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.LG; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Model-Based RL, Conformal Prediction, Safe RL
- **Summary**: 基于像素的反馈运动规划需要安全约束，但潜在世界模型预测不完美。本文提出SLS^2框架，训练动作条件联合嵌入世界模型，结合GPU加速的鲁棒模型预测控制（MPC）与共形预测来校准潜在误差边界，并学习共形化的潜在约束检查器。在视觉控制任务上，该方法在目标达成和安全性能上均优于基线。

<h3 id="260">[260] SCAN: A Decision-Making Framework for Effective Task Allocation with Generative AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15601](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15601) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Fendi Tsim, Alina Gutoreva
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Human-AI Collaboration, Education Technology, Cognitive Science
- **Summary**: 需要一个人本主义的决策框架来有效分配生成式AI任务。本文提出SCAN框架，基于维果茨基的最近发展区和元认知，定义了四个子区域：替代、补充、辅助和不可协商。该框架应用于知识工作者和学生，讨论认知负荷、认知卸载、谄媚等，旨在实现混合智能。

<h3 id="261">[261] FragFuse: Bypassing Access Control of Large Language Model Agents via Memory-Based Query Fragmentation and Fusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15609](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15609) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zixin Rao, Wentian Zhu, Chan Aristella Lu, Zhaorun Chen, Wei Niu, Le Guan, Bo Li, Zhen Xiang
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Agent Security, Jailbreak Attack
- **Venue**: USENIX Security 2026
- **Summary**: 大型语言模型代理的长程记忆带来了新的攻击面：禁止内容可通过碎片化交互存储并在后续重建来绕过访问控制。本文提出FragFuse攻击，通过黑盒自适应查询识别拒绝响应片段，注入记忆，然后重构。在三种访问控制机制上，平均绕过成功率为86.3%，端到端有害任务成功率为41.1%。

<h3 id="262">[262] LLM Judges Have Dark Current: A Psychometric Datasheet for LLM-as-a-Judge Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15610](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15610) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hiroyasu Usami, Keisuke Hara, Ayato Tsuboi, Naohiko Matsuda
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; astro-ph.IM; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, LLM-as-a-Judge, Psychometrics
- **Summary**: LLM-as-a-judge系统通常报告为标量准确率，但需要作为测量仪器来表征。本文引入Judge Datasheet协议，测量暗电流、交叉敏感性、位置偏差等。对三个开源模型进行案例研究，结果显示提示调整的是决策标准而非分辨率。

<h3 id="263">[263] Mutual Distillation of Dual-Foundation Models for Semi-Supervised PET/CT Segmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15611](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15611) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Fuyou Mao, Beining Wu, Yanfeng Jiang, Bohan Xu, Lixin Lin, Naye Ji, Hao Zhang, Yan Tang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Semi-Supervised Learning, Knowledge Distillation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Wu-beining/MuDuo)
- **Summary**: PET/CT器官分割标注成本高，半监督学习是实用方案。本文提出MuDuo互蒸馏框架，协同利用SAM-Med3D（CT）和SegAnyPET（PET）作为模态专用基础模型，将知识蒸馏到轻量学生网络。在AutoPET数据集上仅用5个标注样本即达到最先进性能。

<h3 id="264">[264] Surprise-Guided MergeSort: Budget-Efficient Human-in-the-Loop Ranking via Adaptive Comparison Scheduling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15623](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15623) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yujin Park, Haejun Chung, Ikbeom Jang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Human-AI Collaboration, Active Learning, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 成对比较需要大量人工标注，排序方法仍需每对比较。本文提出Surprise-Guided MergeSort (SGS)，利用视觉语言模型作为问题优先级排序器，结合归并排序调度器、复合惊讶评分器和自适应预算分配器，将高惊讶对分配给人类。在六个基准上，SGS有效跳过非信息性比较，在相同预算下Kendall's τ比Active Elo提高6-12点。

<h3 id="265">[265] Retrieve, Don't Retrain: Extending Vision Language Action Models to New Tasks at Test Time</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15631](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15631) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jeongeun Park, Juhan Park, Taekyung Kim, Sungjoon Choi, Dongyoon Han, Sangdoo Yun
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robotics, Vision-Language Model, Few-Shot Learning
- **Summary**: 将视觉-语言-动作策略扩展到新任务通常需要特定任务演示和微调。本文提出检索增强策略，仅需一次训练，新任务通过将池侧演示添加到检索池即可在部署时添加，无需更新参数。在PushT和RoboTwin2.0上，该方法优于跨实体基线，并已在真实机器人上验证。

<h3 id="266">[266] CIWI-CKT: Chaos-Informed Wave Interference Feature Fusion and Cross-City Knowledge Transfer for Traffic Flow Forecasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15642](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15642) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Abdul Joseph Fofanah, Lian Wen, David Chen, Shaoyang Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Meta-Learning, Spatiotemporal Forecasting
- **Summary**: 跨城市数据稀缺场景下交通预测面临混沌动态和异质性挑战。本文提出CIWI-CKT框架，包含混沌信息波动生成、元干扰处理和混沌感知元学习。在四个真实数据集上显著优于现有方法，同时减少所需训练数据。

<h3 id="267">[267] AnonShield: Scalable On-Premise Pseudonymization for CSIRT Vulnerability Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15650](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15650) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Cristhian Kapelinski, Douglas Lautert, Beatriz Machado, Diego Kreutz, Isadora Garcia Ferrão
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.PF
- **Tags**: Cybersecurity, Data Privacy, Information Extraction
- **Summary**: 漏洞数据匿名化处理速度慢。本文提出AnonShield，结合GPU加速NER、流处理、缓存和模式感知配置。在550MB数据集上，处理时间从92小时以上降至10分钟以内（加速738倍），F1达94.2%，召回率96.7%。

<h3 id="268">[268] IoT-Zoo: A Container-Based Framework for Heterogeneous IoT Device Profiles and Reproducible Traffic Capture</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15653](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15653) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Vagner E. Quincozes, Diego Kreutz, Silvio E. Quincozes
- **Subjects**: cs.NI; cs.AI; cs.ET
- **Tags**: IoT, Research Reproducibility
- **Summary**: 现有IoT测试平台通过复制有限设备类型实现可扩展性，缺乏异构性。本文提出IoT-Zoo，基于Containernet的容器化测试平台，支持多域场景部署和真实应用协议（MQTT、RTSP），提供单命令环境配置和自动流量捕获，生成一致的流量基线。

<h3 id="269">[269] PO-PDDL: Learning Symbolic POMDPs from Visual Demonstrations for Robot Planning Under Uncertainty</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15654](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15654) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wenjing Tang, Xuanjin Jin, Yuan Liu, Renming Huang, Cewu Lu, Panpan Cai
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Automated Planning, Robotics, POMDP
- **Summary**: 构建部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）模型进行机器人规划非常耗时。本文提出PO-PDDL符号化公式，并开发基于演示的管道：从执行视频重建符号状态轨迹，识别部分可观测性，学习随机转移和观测模型。在真实长时操作任务上，优于现有PDDL和POMDP学习方法，且规划成本更低。

<h3 id="270">[270] Z-Plane Neural Networks: Bounded Geometric Activation Replaces ReLU and LayerNorm</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15669](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15669) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sungwoo Goo, Hwi-yeol Yun, Sangkeun Jung
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Activation Function, Deep Learning Theory
- **Summary**: ReLU和LayerNorm分别导致死神经元和破坏正交性。本文提出Z-Plane神经网络，将隐藏状态映射到超球面上的二维相量束，引入Radial Bounding激活函数限制能量幅度而保留相位。数学证明该激活保持1-Lipschitz连续且防止梯度消失。100层MLP在MNIST上达到98.34%准确率，无需ReLU和LayerNorm。

<h3 id="271">[271] The Reservoir Attention Network: Cross-Pass State in Pretrained Transformers via Content-Addressable Reservoir Injection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15678](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15678) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Emma Leonhart
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Transformer Architecture, Reservoir Computing
- **Summary**: 预训练变换器需要跨前向传递的状态。本文研究Reservoir Attention Network (RAN)，将固定随机初始化的储层注入中间层注意力，以携带跨前向传递状态。在GPT-2和Qwen2.5上进行可行性实验，使用最小探针任务，结果表明未经训练的循环动力学足以携带可用的跨传递状态。

<h3 id="272">[272] Imperfect Visual Verification for Code Edition : A Case Study on TikZ</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15693](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15693) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Charly Reux, Mathieu Acher, Djamel Eddine Khelladi, Clément Quinton, Olivier Barais
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Evaluation, Visual Verification
- **Summary**: 定制生成视觉代码（如TikZ）需要局部、语义保持的编辑，但视觉正确性难以形式化验证。本文通过迭代精炼框架研究不完美验证器的作用。大规模实验表明，不完美验证器可适度判断视觉指令是否应用（F1达0.815），反馈对弱模型改进显著（+11-20个完美定制），但需提供准确可操作的指导。

<h3 id="273">[273] MAF: Multimodal Adaptive Few-shot Prompting for Sentiment Analysis with MLLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15694](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15694) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hangling Xie
- **Subjects**: cs.MM; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Sentiment Analysis, Multimodal Learning, Prompt Engineering
- **Summary**: 问题：静态提示对多模态情感分析任务中的细微特征不敏感；方法：提出多模态自适应小样本提示框架(MAF)，动态检索与查询相关的示例，编码面部表情、场景上下文和文本语义，并通过轻量级系数网络进行加权融合；结果：在多个基准数据集上，MAF显著提升了性能，与强基线模型竞争。

<h3 id="274">[274] When Generator Replay Degrades: Projected Rehearsal Orchestration for Heterogeneous Federated Class-Incremental Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15695](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15695) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Thinh T. H. Nguyen, Khoa D. Doan, Binh T. Nguyen, Danh Le-Phuoc, Kok-Seng Wong
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Continual Learning, Memory Architecture
- **Summary**: 问题：异构联邦类增量学习(FCIL)中客户端标签子集不同、任务阶段不对齐导致性能退化；方法：提出PRO框架，用投影排练编排替代合成输入回放，在服务器维护类别级压缩投影记忆，客户端进行平衡伪多任务训练；结果：在图像、文本和图基准上，PRO和PRO-MAX在异构流下提升了保留和最终效用，在均匀流下也具有竞争力。

<h3 id="275">[275] Odds Law: The Decomposition Algebra On How Intelligence Organizes Itself to Solve Difficult Problems Reliably</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15712](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15712) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hidayet Aksu
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Probabilistic Reasoning, Ensemble Learning, Formal Methods
- **Summary**: 问题：如何组织不可靠的基本问题求解器以可靠地解决难题；方法：提出分解代数，包含四种组合子（顺序组合、并行集成、验证门控、递归约简），导出验证赔率定律和可靠性放大定理；结果：证明了自我组织是最小固定点，多样性对无界放大是必要的，存在信息上限。

<h3 id="276">[276] The algebra of Krom logic programs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15719](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15719) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Christian Antić
- **Subjects**: cs.LO; cs.AI; math.LO
- **Tags**: Logic Programming, Graph Theory, Formal Methods
- **Summary**: 问题：探究Krom逻辑程序的代数结构；方法：证明顺序组合赋予Krom程序自然幺半群结构，并扩展到各种近环和半近环；结果：建立了与变换幺半群和有限状态自动机的联系，揭示了逻辑程序设计与代数自动机理论之间的新联系。

<h3 id="277">[277] InstantForget: Update-Free Backdoor Unlearning with Inference-Time Feature Reset</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15730](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15730) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhenyu Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Backdoor Attack, Model Security, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: 问题：在无更新的推理时设置中移除后门触发器，现有投影假设失败；方法：提出InstantForget，使用干净校准的门控重置，通过马氏距离标记异常特征并移向中性非目标表示；结果：在CIFAR-10上平均ASR降至0.071，检测AUROC达0.981，并迁移到六个以上骨干网络。

<h3 id="278">[278] Vernier: Probing Representational Misalignment Behind Lexical Gaps in Causal Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15733](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15733) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhenyu Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Causal Reasoning, Interpretability, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：指令微调语言模型在替换变量名后对因果推理问题的回答不同，但答案不变；方法：Vernier使用配对视图权重更新作为工具，探查表示是否对齐；结果：证据支持表示未对齐，激活修补表明决策标记表示可以在视图间传递答案身份。

<h3 id="279">[279] Retrievable Gradients: Continual Post-Training Without Cumulative Weight Drift</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15734](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15734) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Weihang Su, Jiacheng Kang, Jingyan Xu, Qingyao Ai, Jianming Long, Hanwen Zhang, Bangde Du, Xinyuan Cao, Min Zhang, Yiqun Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.IR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, RAG, Meta-Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：持续后训练导致权重漂移和灾难性遗忘，RAG避免漂移但缺乏参数化深度；方法：ReGrad将梯度视为可检索的知识单元，离线预计算文档梯度并存储到梯度银行，推理时检索相关梯度进行临时权重适应；结果：在通用和领域特定设置中优于CPT和RAG基线，实现了可扩展且可逆的参数化知识注入。

<h3 id="280">[280] EHRNote-ChatQA: A Benchmark for Evidence-Grounded Multi-Turn Clinical Question Answering over Longitudinal Discharge Summaries</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15735](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15735) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiyoun Kim, Muhan Yeo, Eunhye Jang, Jeewon Yang, Hangyul Yoon, Su Ji Lee, Hee Jo Han, Hee-Jae Jung, Doyun Kwon, Jun young Lee, Jaehun Lee, Jung-Oh Lee, Sunjun Kweon, Jong Hak Moon, Daseul Kim, Minjae Cho, Edward Choi
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Question Answering, Medical AI, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 问题：现有临床问答基准缺乏对多轮、带证据溯源的评估；方法：构建EHRNote-ChatQA基准，包含967个病人级多轮样本和1.6万QA对，由11位医学专家审核；结果：22个大模型测试表明，模型在证据溯源上比内容回答更困难，多轮错误会累积。

<h3 id="281">[281] A Self Consistency Based Reranking for Narrative Question Answering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15741](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15741) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Molham Mohamed, Ali Hamdi
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Question Answering, Self-Consistency, Ensemble Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：叙述性问答依赖单次解码，易产生不完整或不一致的回答；方法：提出基于自一致性的重排序框架，生成多个候选答案，根据语义一致性选择最终答案；结果：在NarrativeQA上，FLAN-T5-Base从82.32%提升至86.66%，Pegasus-Large从72.50%提升至87.07%。

<h3 id="282">[282] OmniTraffic: A Controllable Generation Pipeline and Benchmark for Spatio-Temporal Traffic Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15749](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15749) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Maonan Wang, Zhengyan Huang, Kemou Jiang, Yuhang Fu, Jiayue Zhu, Yuxin Cai, Xingchen Zou, Qiaosheng Zhang, Yi Yu, Ding Wang, Xi Chen, Ben M. Chen, Yuxuan Liang, Zhiyong Cui, Man On Pun, Yirong Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Multimodal Learning, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 问题：现有交通场景基准主要关注被动视觉识别，缺乏对结构化交通推理的支持；方法：提出OmniTraffic可控生成管道，包含12个真实路口，生成800万VQA样本，定义三级任务层次；结果：11个前沿MLLM评估显示人类-模型差距大，在拓扑和时空推理任务上表现最差。

<h3 id="283">[283] From Correlation to Causation in Lane Change Prediction for Automated Driving: A Causal Explanation Framework</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15756](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15756) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohamed Manzour, Aditya Kumar, Augusto Luis Ballardini, Miguel Ángel Sotelo
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Autonomous Driving, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 问题：换道预测方法主要学习统计关联，忽略变量间的因果依赖，缺乏可解释性；方法：提出基于因果推断的框架，结合因果发现、深度结构因果建模和干预效应分析；结果：在线路交叉前3秒内平均F1分数超过95%，并生成因果链解释。

<h3 id="284">[284] Snyk VulnBench JS 1.0: Can LLMs Find the Same Bugs Twice?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15762](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15762) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Liran Tal, Johannes Kloos, Arsenii Rudich, Stephen Thoemmes, Manoj Nair
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Cybersecurity, Static Analysis
- **Summary**: 问题：评估智能体大模型安全审查的可重复性；方法：对相同JavaScript代码进行300次重复漏洞扫描；结果：引用匹配的发现稳定，但额外发现变化很大；与确定性SAST有互补性。

<h3 id="285">[285] Visualizing Uncertainty: Spatial Maps of Missing and Conflicting Evidence in Deep Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15767](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15767) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dong Hyun Jeong, Feng Chen, Jin-Hee Cho, Lance M. Kaplan, Audun Jøsang, Soo-Yeon Ji
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Uncertainty Estimation, Interpretability, Evidential Deep Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：现有不确定性量化方法只提供标量置信度，无法展示空间区域对不确定性类型的贡献；方法：提出不确定性激活图(UAM)，结合证据深度学习(EDL)和FullGrad，生成空间不确定性激活图，区分欠缺和冲突证据；结果：在多个基准数据集上提供了直观的视觉反馈。

<h3 id="286">[286] LaWAM: Latent World Action Models for Efficient Dynamics-Aware Robot Policies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15768](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15768) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jialei Chen, Kai Wang, Kang Chen, Shuaihang Chen, Feng Gao, Wenhao Tang, Zhiyuan Li, Weilin Liu, Zhuyu Yao, Boxun Li, Yuanbo Xu, Chao Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robotics, Vision-Language Model, World Model
- **Summary**: 问题：视觉-语言-动作模型(VLA)缺乏显式预测，世界-动作模型(WAM)使用昂贵的视频生成；方法：提出LaWAM，通过潜在世界模型(LaWM)在预训练视觉基础模型的潜在空间中预测潜在视觉子目标，并以此为条件生成动作；结果：在LIBERO和RoboTwin上达到最先进成功率，延迟低至187ms，比像素级WAM快24倍。

<h3 id="287">[287] DYNA : Dynamic Episodic Memory Networks for Augmenting Large Language Models with Temporal Knowledge Graphs in Continuous Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15778](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15778) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ali Sarabadani, Mahtab Tajvidiyan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.SI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Temporal Reasoning, Memory Architecture
- **Summary**: 问题：大模型难以在不遗忘或重新训练的情况下吸收新知识；方法：DYNA用时间知识图作为外部可更新记忆，查询时通过随机游走和中心性度量检索相关节点增强回答；结果：相比微调减少约7%灾难性遗忘，相比标准RAG提升约5%时序排序。

<h3 id="288">[288] Domain-Guided Prompting of the Segment Anything Model for Seismic Interpretation: The Role of Attributes, Visualization, and Hybrid Prompts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15786](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15786) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aniq Ahmad, Heather Bedle, Ahmad Mustafa
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Zero-Shot Learning, Image Segmentation, Segment Anything Model
- **Summary**: 问题：SAM在地震解释中需要微调，依赖大量标注数据且损失泛化能力；方法：提出零样本框架，结合地震属性与可视化选择，以及混合提示（用户点提示与SAM内部特征掩码提示）；结果：联合应用时达到有竞争力的分割性能，无需重训练。

<h3 id="289">[289] GAS-Leak-LLM: Genetic Algorithm-Based Suffix Optimization for Black-Box LLM Jailbreaking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15788](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15788) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aman Anifer, Vignesh Kumar Kembu, Vishnu M, Antonino Nocera, Vinod P., Amal Murali PK, Akshay S Rajan
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Jailbreak Attack, Prompt Injection
- **Summary**: 针对大语言模型在严格黑盒场景下仍易受到越狱攻击的问题，提出基于遗传算法的对抗后缀优化方法GAS-Leak-LLM，通过选择、变异和交叉启发式系统搜索离散提示空间。实验证明该方法能有效绕过安全约束，揭示现有防御机制的缺陷。

<h3 id="290">[290] Proximal Policy Optimization for Amortized Discrete Sampling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15793](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15793) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Anna Zykova-Myzina, Timofei Gritsaev, Daniil Tiapkin, Nikita Morozov
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Generative Flow Networks, Proximal Policy Optimization, Molecular Generation
- **Summary**: 针对生成流网络（GFlowNet）训练效率问题，首次推导并成功应用近端策略优化（PPO）算法，与GFlowNet框架结合。在合成能量和分子图生成等基准上，PPO相比标准GFlowNet训练目标收敛更快且数据效率更高。

<h3 id="291">[291] DifFRACT: Diffusion Feature Reconstruction and Attribution for Circuit Tracing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15796](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15796) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Artyom Mazur, Nina Konovalova, Aibek Alanov
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, Diffusion Model, Vision-Language Model
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Artalmaz31/DifFRACT)
- **Summary**: 针对多模态扩散Transformer（如FLUX）的机制可解释性缺乏有效工具的问题，扩展transcoder电路追踪方法，训练时间步条件transcoder近似MLP子层。在FLUX.1[schnell]上，transcoder在稀疏-忠实权衡上匹配或优于SAE，并揭示属性绑定和跨流语义传播机制，电路引导干预更精确有效。

<h3 id="292">[292] Continuous Cross-Domain Traffic State Prediction via Memory-Augmented Graph Liquid Time-Constant Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15807](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15807) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jinrong Xiang, Ming Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Traffic Forecasting, Graph Neural Network, Time Series Forecasting
- **Summary**: 针对跨域交通状态预测中源-目标适应粗粒度、难以建模连续动态等问题，提出记忆增强图液体时间常数网络（MA-GLTC），包含时空单元进行细粒度知识对齐、图液体时间常数网络建模连续时间演化，以及记忆迁移存储机制。在五个公共数据集上，平均预测误差相比第二名方法降低3.02%~10.09%。

<h3 id="293">[293] Let Them Steal: Trapping Large Language Model Extraction Attacks with Knowledge Honeypot</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15810](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15810) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuyang Dai, Yushun Dong
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Knowledge Graph, Model Extraction Attack
- **Summary**: 针对LLM模型提取攻击缺乏有效且不影响合法用户的防御问题，提出Knowledge Trap防御方法，通过蜜罐知识图和面包屑引导探索消耗攻击者查询预算。在医疗和金融领域，替代模型Agreement平均降低6.2%，且不降低合法用户准确率。

<h3 id="294">[294] SACE: Concept Erasure at the Semantic Singularity in Visual Autoregressive Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15819](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15819) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Siya Yang, Nanxiang Jiang, Zhaoxin Fan, Yunfeng Diao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Generation, Text-to-Image, AI Safety
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/limerenceysy/SACE)
- **Summary**: 针对视觉自回归模型中概念擦除导致语义崩溃和伪影的问题，提出语义奇点公理并验证，首次设计尺度感知的概念擦除框架SACE，仅在第一个尺度进行熵正则化擦除和恢复性保真损失。实验表明该方法在多个领域实现精确擦除且训练开销小。

<h3 id="295">[295] The Truth Stays in the Family: Enhancing Contextual Grounding via Inherited Truthful Heads in Model Lineages</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15821](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15821) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Miso Choi, Seonga Choi, Mincheol Kwon, Woosung Joung, Jinkyu Kim, Jungbeom Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, Multimodal Learning, LLM Evaluation
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/miso-choi/TruthProbe)
- **Summary**: 针对基础LLM与多模态变体之间关于上下文真实性行为联系不明的问题，量化头级真实性分数并发现其在模型家族内强保持。提出TruthProbe软门控策略放大真实头贡献，在HaluEval、POPE和CHAIR上减少幻觉，且基础LLM真实性分数有效迁移到微调模型。

<h3 id="296">[296] Wasserstein Convergence of ODE-Based Samplers in Decentralized Diffusion Model via Velocity Field Decomposition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15835](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15835) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chencheng Tang, Xuanyu Xue, Fangyikang Wang, Chao Zhang, Hubery Yin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Convergence Analysis
- **Summary**: 针对分散式扩散模型ODE采样缺乏收敛理论的问题，通过速度场分解建立Wasserstein-2距离下的收敛保证，速率为O(N^{-1/2}+ε)。这是首个分散式扩散模型ODE采样在W_2距离下的收敛结果。

<h3 id="297">[297] Free Energy Heuristics: Fast-And-Frugal Cognition as Active Inference Under Uncertain Precision</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15877](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15877) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alex Bogdan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Chain-of-Thought, LLM Reasoning, Active Inference
- **Summary**: 针对Chain-of-Thought在规划、伦理等任务上增加推理反而降低性能的异常现象，提出元不确定性概念，证明在重型尾精度先验下最小化期望自由能的策略与take-the-best启发式等价。在FEH-79基准上验证，高元不确定性项目CoT延长导致准确率下降17.3点。

<h3 id="298">[298] Deep Residual Injection for Full-Spectrum Forensic Signal Perception in Multimodal Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15880](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15880) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kaiqing Lin, Zhiyuan Yan, Ruoxin Chen, Ke-Yue Zhang, Yue Zhou, Caiyong Piao, Bin Li, Taiping Yao, Bo Wang, Youchang Xiao, Shouhong Ding
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Image Forensics, Deepfake Detection
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/KQL11/Deep-VRM)
- **Summary**: 针对多模态大模型能否同时捕获低级生成器伪影而不损失语义知识的问题，通过层分析揭示语义信息形成位置，提出Deep-VRM框架，在中间层注入伪影残差信号联合建模。在多个取证基准上达到最先进，实现鲁棒且泛化的检测性能。

<h3 id="299">[299] Koshur Diacritizer: A Byte-Level Sequence-to-Sequence Model for Kashmiri Diacritic Restoration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15883](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15883) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haq Nawaz Malik, Nahfid Nissar, Faizan Iqbal
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Low-Resource NLP, Diacritic Restoration
- **Summary**: 针对克什米尔语数字文本变音符号缺失导致的歧义问题，提出Koshur Diacritizer，基于ByT5-small的字节级序列到序列模型，结合脚本感知归一化和骨架保留推理。在测试集上DERm为0.2012，WER为0.2159，母语专家平均准确率77.5%。

<h3 id="300">[300] Intelligence Is Not the Bottleneck: Validating an LLM First-Pass Manuscript Score Against Peer-Review Outcomes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15887](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15887) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Costa Georgantas
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Peer Review, LLM Application
- **Summary**: 针对LLM辅助同行评审中数值评分有效性缺乏验证的问题，实证评估AIPR系统，仅通过提示对ICLR提交打分得到五个维度和总体评分。整体评分区分接受与拒绝的AUROC为0.82，与平均评审评分一致，最低五分之一被拒绝率远高于基线。

<h3 id="301">[301] NVMOS: Non-Verbal Vocalization Quality Assessment in Speech</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15888](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15888) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jialong Mai, Jinxin Ji, Xiaofen Xing, Wencui Liu, Xiangmin Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Speech Evaluation, Audio Processing, Multimodal Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/yongaifadian1/NVMOS)
- **Summary**: 针对非语言发声（如笑声、叹息）的感知质量评估未被充分探索的问题，构建NV-MOS数据集并分析多模态LLM与专家评分的不一致性，提出NVMOS模型，包含局部NV事件聚焦模块。实验表明NVMOS达到专家级或更强的人机评分一致性。

<h3 id="302">[302] Topological Flow Matching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15897](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15897) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kacper Wyrwal, İsmail İlkan Ceylan, Alexander Tong
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Flow Matching, Topological Data Analysis, Graph Neural Network
- **Venue**: ICLR 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/KacperWyrwal/topological-flow-matching)
- **Summary**: 针对标准Flow Matching忽略结构化数据拓扑特征的问题，提出拓扑Flow Matching，将参考过程增加拉普拉斯导出漂移以注入拓扑信息，保持原有稳定性和确定性。在脑fMRI、洋流、地震和交通流量数据集上验证有效性。

<h3 id="303">[303] SkillVetBench: LLM-as-Judge for Multi-Dimensional Security Risk Evaluation in Open-Source LLM Agent Skills</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15899](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15899) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ismail Hossain, Sai Puppala, Md Jahangir Alam, Tanzim Ahad, Sajedul Talukder
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.HC; cs.LG; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Security, LLM Agent, LLM-as-a-Judge
- **Summary**: 针对开源LLM代理技能安全性缺乏审查、现有代码层扫描器无法检测指令层风险的问题，提出SKILLVETBENCH，采用LLM-as-Judge进行多维风险评估，引入Skill Agentic Risk Score五维指标。在78个恶意技能上零假阴性，静态基线漏检15%；指令层类别（如提示注入）常规工具漏检89%-100%。

<h3 id="304">[304] Control-Plane Placement Shapes Forgetting: An Architectural Study of Agent Memory Across Thirteen System Configurations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15903](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15903) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dongxu Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/deeplethe/lethe)
- **Summary**: 针对LLM代理记忆流水线中控制平面位置对遗忘失败模式影响不明的问题，在13种系统配置上比较385案例，提出ForgetEval基准（1000案例模板+385对抗层）和六方法适配器协议。发现突变时钩子恢复意图感知删除78-85%，整体准确率91.7-93.2%，成本低。

<h3 id="305">[305] MAGE-RAG: Multigranular Adaptive Graph Evidence for Agentic Multimodal RAG in Long-Document QA</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15906](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15906) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yilong Zuo, Xunkai Li, Jing Yuan, Qiangqiang Dai, Hongchao Qin, Ronghua Li
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.DB; cs.MM
- **Tags**: RAG, Vision-Language Model, Question Answering
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/laonuo2004/MAGE-RAG.git)
- **Summary**: 现有的长文档多模态问答系统在证据检索中面临固定Top-K检索导致视觉信息丢失或噪声过多的问题。本文提出MAGE-RAG，一种多粒度自适应图证据框架，通过离线构建包含页面和元素的证据图，并在查询时利用在线证据控制器进行迭代激活、扩展、搜索和剪枝，生成紧凑的多模态阅读器输入。在LongDocURL和MMLongBench-Doc上，MAGE-RAG分别达到52.75和53.26的准确率，并平衡了分散证据覆盖和上下文噪声控制。

<h3 id="306">[306] On-Policy Distillation with Curriculum Turn-level Guidance for Multi-turn Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15912](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15912) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gengsheng Li, Mao Zheng, Mingyang Song, Ruiqi Liu, Tianyu Yang, Jie Sun, Qiyong Zhong, Haiyun Guo, Junfeng Fang, Dan Zhang, Jinqiao Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, LLM Agent, Curriculum Learning
- **Summary**: 多轮智能体的策略蒸馏中，小模型错误会随回合累积，导致教师监督失效。本文提出Guided-OPD，通过混合教师和学生生成的回合，并按课程衰减教师干预概率，逐步过渡到纯在策略蒸馏。在ALFWorld等任务上，从Qwen3-30B-A3B蒸馏Qwen3学生，Guided-OPD相比普通OPD将Score提升21.1%，成功率提升25.5%。

<h3 id="307">[307] Runtime Analysis of Cartesian Genetic Programming in Evolving Boolean Functions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15923](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15923) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Duc-Cuong Dang, Roman Kalkreuth, Andre Opris
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Cartesian Genetic Programming, Evolutionary Algorithm
- **Venue**: PPSN 2026
- **Summary**: 笛卡尔遗传编程（CGP）的理论分析尚不完善。本文首次对CGP演化布尔函数进行运行时分析，证明了使用严格选择时生成n输入合取式的期望适应度评估次数为O(n D^5)，非严格选择时改进为O(n D^4)，并证明演化异或需要指数时间。实验验证了理论结果，并发现不完整训练集可减少评估次数。

<h3 id="308">[308] ControlMap: Controllable High-Definition Map Generation for Traffic Scenario Simulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15930](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15930) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Marwan Farag, Steffen Wäldele, Yu Yao
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Diffusion Model
- **Summary**: 自动驾驶仿真因高清地图创建成本高昂而场景多样性不足。本文提出ControlMap，一种基于潜在扩散和ControlNet的可控高清地图生成管道，支持空间条件控制、无分类器引导和城市风格迁移。实验表明生成的HD地图能忠实遵循输入道路拓扑并保留城市细节，引入新指标评估控制信号保真度。

<h3 id="309">[309] DeepRoot: A KG-Coordinated Multi-Agent System for Therapeutic Reasoning over Historical Medical Texts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15931](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15931) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zijian Carl Ma, Sean J. Wang, Sijbren Kramer, Li Erran Li
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, LLM Agent, Drug Discovery
- **Summary**: 历史医学文献因非标准化术语无法用于现代药物发现，现有LLM系统无法规模化转化为可验证线索。本文提出DeepRoot，一个多智能体LLM系统，联合构建并利用验证知识图谱进行治疗推理。在神农本草经上，DeepRoot恢复10/21个候补药物-疾病对（R@20 47.6% vs 4.8%），幻觉率从87%降至7-10%，在推理质量上优于基线。

<h3 id="310">[310] Graphical-Probabilistic Modeling of Generative Flows in LLM-Native Software Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15943](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15943) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Víctor A. Braberman, Flavia Bonomo-Braberman
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Software Engineering, LLM Application, Probabilistic Inference
- **Venue**: 2026 IEEE/ACM 5th International Conference on AI Engineering - Software Engineering for AI (CAIN '26)
- **Summary**: 原生LLM软件工程缺乏有原则的结构来支持设计级推理。本文提出Generation Networks，一种基于图概率模型的框架，用于记录生成流和陈述LLM系统属性。该框架为生成交互和系统级属性提供了可推理的基础，旨在将传统软件工程的模块化和抽象引入LLM原生开发。

<h3 id="311">[311] Green SARC: Predictive Cost and Carbon Governance for Agentic AI Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15954](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15954) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gaston Besanson
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.DC; cs.LG
- **Tags**: AI Sustainability, LLM Agent, Cost Optimization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/besanson/Greensarc)
- **Summary**: 智能体AI系统缺乏预测性的成本和碳治理机制。本文提出Green SARC，应用SARC治理框架于FinOps和GreenOps，提供理论并实证分析。结果显示无约束的State Snowball复杂度为Θ(n^2)，分裂共形校准覆盖率达95.2%，架构门控超预算率为0%，而软罚则达91.5%。端到端Token/美元/碳排放节省47-55%。

<h3 id="312">[312] You Don't Need Strong Assumptions: Visual Representation Learning via Temporal Differences</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15956](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15956) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ninad Daithankar, Alexi Gladstone, Yann LeCun, Heng Ji
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Self-Supervised Learning, Video Understanding, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 现有自监督学习方法依赖数据增强等强归纳偏置，且随数据规模增大这些偏置成为瓶颈。本文提出TDV，一种基于因果假设（过去导致未来）的视频自监督学习范式，联合训练图像编码器和运动编码器，使当前帧表示加运动编码等于下一帧表示。在密集空间任务上匹配了最先进方法，无需强归纳偏置。

<h3 id="313">[313] Quantifying the Impact of Lossy Compression on Neural Generative Surrogate Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15959](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15959) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhimin Li, Harshitha Menon, Charles Jekel, Valerio Pascucci, Peter Lindstrom
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Scientific Computing, Generative Model
- **Summary**: 高保真生成式代理模型需要大量训练数据，造成存储和I/O挑战。本文提出一种方法，通过表征神经网络训练的不确定性来估计代理模型对压缩误差的容忍度。在两种应用中，有损压缩将数据存储节省23.7倍和39倍，训练时间减少最多3倍，而对模型质量影响可忽略。

<h3 id="314">[314] PreLort: Prefix-Nested LoRA for Federated Fine-Tuning under Rank Heterogeneity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15963](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15963) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Muhammad Waseem, Nurbek Tastan, Andrej Jovanovic, Nicholas D. Lane, Nils Lukas, Karthik Nandakumar, Samuel Horvath
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, LoRA, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 联邦微调中异构LoRA秩的问题导致无法直接聚合，现有方法未能控制信息如何分布。本文提出PreLort，一种嵌套低秩公式，通过前缀层级组织适配器维度，并引入分段聚合和前缀嵌套训练策略。实验表明PreLort在多个基模型上优于先前方法，准确率和ROUGE-L有提升，困惑度更低或相当。

<h3 id="315">[315] Formalize Once, Edit the Rest: Efficient Lean-Based Answer Selection for Math Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15972](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15972) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ji Feng, Zhouxing Shi
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Mathematical Reasoning, Formal Methods
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ucr-rai/base-and-edit)
- **Summary**: 数学推理中利用Lean验证答案选择时，对每个候选答案单独形式化成本高昂。本文提出BASE管道，先形式化一个基础候选，再通过编辑其余候选的答案表达式来重用。训练的LEANSCRIBE重写器可定位答案并生成可重用编辑函数。在12个配置上，BASE同时提高了选择精度并减少形式化调用约5倍（K=8），且随K增大效果更佳。

<h3 id="316">[316] Do Safety Monitors Stay Reliable After an Update? Benchmarking and Predicting Activation-Monitor Staleness</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15980](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15980) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Evan Duan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: AI Safety, Safety Evaluation, Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 激活监控器在模型更新（量化、微调等）后是否仍可靠尚不清楚。本文首次系统测试了多种更新对监控器的影响，发现量化类更新基本保持性能，而微调常导致监控器失效，尤其QLoRA损害严重。退化可从部署前特征预测，从而优先重新验证最可能失败的监控器。

<h3 id="317">[317] Task-guided cross-subject latent alignment: a multi-encoder-decoder VAE</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15989](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15989) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Angeliki Papathanasiou, Jascha Achterberg, Thomas E. Nichols, Rui Ponte Costa
- **Subjects**: q-bio.NC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neuroscience, Representation Learning, Variational Autoencoder
- **Summary**: 传统跨被试神经活动对齐需要共享刺激，限制其应用。本文提出MED-VAE，通过锚定到预训练ANN表示实现无共享刺激的对齐。在Natural Scenes Dataset上，MED-VAE创建了具有卓越语义组织的公共潜在空间，对齐度优于常见方法，且保持了刺激驱动信号，支持跨被试图像解码。

<h3 id="318">[318] Entity Labels Are Not Entity Signals: A Framework for Observable Relevance in Document Re-Ranking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15998](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15998) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Utshab Kumar Ghosh, Shubham Chatterjee
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Information Retrieval, Entity Linking, Reranking
- **Summary**: 实体感知检索假设语义相关实体也是有用检索信号，但该假设因链接器缺陷而不充分。本文形式化了概念实体相关性（CER）和可观察实体相关性（OER）的区分，证明CER是系统性异常值。基于OER的监督将无关文档剪枝提高最多10倍，开放世界MAP提升0.051。

<h3 id="319">[319] Theorem-Grounded Execution Ontologies for Interpretable Machine Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16010](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16010) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Raghu Anantharangachar
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Formal Methods, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: LLM的推理过程是隐式的，难以解释和验证。本文提出TGEO框架，将推理建模为可执行状态转移过程，包含定理优先、执行本体、算子中介转移等组件。生成的执行图提供可解释、可回放、可审计的推理表示，在定理密集型任务上展示了可执行推理表示的价值。

<h3 id="320">[320] Orchestrated Reality: From Role-Play to Living, Playable Game Worlds -- LLM-Driven World Simulation as a Parameterized-Action POMDP</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16014](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16014) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuhang Huang, Chenmiao Li, Chaowei Fang
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Game AI, LLM Agent, POMDP
- **Summary**: 游戏叙事与模拟世界的融合代价高昂，LLM驱动世界缺乏持久状态。本文提出编排现实框架，采用单一编排智能体（类似游戏主持人），将世界状态规范化为JSON实体树，并形式化为参数化动作POMDP。框架包含Plan-Diff-Validate-Apply管道，确保状态变更经过验证，为全自主游戏引擎奠定基础。

<h3 id="321">[321] Open-SWE-Traces: Advancing Dual-Mode Multilingual Distillation for Software Engineering Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16038](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16038) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wasi Uddin Ahmad, Nikolai Ludwig, Somshubra Majumdar, Boris Ginsburg
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Code Generation, Agent Trajectory
- **Summary**: 该论文针对软件工程智能体大规模轨迹数据缺乏的问题，构建了Open-SWE-Traces数据集，包含20万条多语言轨迹，通过混合推理合成并过滤，微调Qwen3-30B-A3B模型。在SWE-bench Verified上达到61.7%的解决率，显著提升了智能体的软件工程能力。

<h3 id="322">[322] Leveraging Deep Learning for Object and Position Recognition of Load Carriers for Autonomous Logistics Vehicles</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16042](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16042) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Christoph Legat, Tobias Miller, Marco Riess
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Object Detection, Pose Estimation, Robotics
- **Venue**: IFAC World Congress2026
- **Summary**: 针对自主物流车辆中负载载体检测和位姿估计的需求，本文设计了一个深度神经网络，从RGBD数据中识别预定义地标并计算位姿。实验验证了该方法在工业环境中的准确性，满足了自动化拾取的可靠性要求。

<h3 id="323">[323] ALCL: An Adaptive Log-Correntropy Loss for Robust Learning under Non-Gaussian Noise</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16050](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16050) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mainak Kundu, Ria Kanjilal, Ismail Uysal
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robust Loss Function, Image Classification, Image Reconstruction
- **Summary**: 为解决传统损失函数对异常值敏感及相关熵损失需固定核参数的问题，提出自适应对数相关熵损失(ALCL)，通过可微分重参数化联合学习形状和尺度参数。在高噪声下，该方法在灰度图和RGB数据集上中位精度分别提升4.75%和4.51%，优于静态相关熵损失。

<h3 id="324">[324] How to Detect and Measure the AI Dangers to Democracy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16054](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16054) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Giulia Sandri, Claudio Novelli
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Governance, AI Risk Management, AI Ethics
- **Summary**: 本文针对AI对民主构成的系统性风险缺乏评估框架的问题，运用委托-代理理论和NIST AI风险管理框架，构建了可衡量的指标体系。该框架强调制度可评估性，但指出危害严重性评估仍是未解决的规范性判断。

<h3 id="325">[325] Mojo: A Promising Tool for Scalable Financial AI Efficiency</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16059](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16059) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Henry Han
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Financial AI, High Performance Computing, Programming Language
- **Summary**: 为解决量化金融中Python到C++的两语言税及GPU非确定性漂移问题，本文介绍Mojo语言，它结合Python易用性与C++性能，通过MLIR编译支持多目标执行。在Apple Silicon上实现20-180倍加速，并提供了开源确定性内核库。

<h3 id="326">[326] MASCOT-Android: A Curated Dataset and Automated Collection Pipeline for Android Malware Source Code Specimens</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16072](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16072) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bojing Li, Duo Zhong, Prajna Bhandary, Raguvir S, Charles Maxa, Robert J Joyce, Charles Nicholas
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Cybersecurity, Malware Detection, Android Security
- **Summary**: 鉴于恶意软件源码数据稀缺且人工审查成本高，本文提出MASCOT-Android数据集和自动收集框架，利用README文档的TF-IDF特征训练LinearSVC分类器。该模型准确率达96.28%，FPR仅1.06%，可大规模发现Android恶意软件源码。

<h3 id="327">[327] PVminerLLM2: Improving Structured Extraction of Patient Voice via Preference Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16074](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16074) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Samah Fodeh, Linhai Ma, Ganesh Puthiaraju, Srivani Talakokkul, Afshan Khan, Elyas Irankhah, Sreeraj Ramachandran, Ashley Hagaman, Sarah Lowe, Aimee Roundtree
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Information Extraction, Preference Optimization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Data-Mining-Lab-Yale/PVminerLLM2)
- **Summary**: 为解决监督微调难以处理患者文本中罕见细粒度错误的问题，本文提出PVminerLLM2，采用偏好优化，包括代币级门控稳定项、混淆感知偏好对构建等。在结构化提取任务上，Code、Sub-code和Span指标分别提升4.43%、3.50%和1.55%。

<h3 id="328">[328] Phys-JEPA: Physics-Informed Latent World Models for Multivariate Time-Series Forecasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16076](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16076) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Weizhi Nie, Weichao Liu, Honglin Guo, Yuting Su
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.GT
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Physics-Informed Learning, World Model
- **Summary**: 针对物理系统多变量预测中物理约束仅在解码层施加的不足，提出Phys-JEPA，在潜在状态和转换中引入物理一致性，分解为物理和残差分量。在Jena气候数据集上温度MSE从0.01892降至0.01831，在Traffic和Electricity数据集上也有所提升。

<h3 id="329">[329] Tool-IQA: Augmenting Image Quality Assessment with Simple Tools</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16082](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16082) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Guanyi Qin, Junjie Zhang, Chunming He, Yibing Fu, Jie Liang, Tianhe Wu, Lei Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Quality Assessment, Vision-Language Model, Tool Learning
- **Summary**: 为解决VLM图像质量评估中静态单次打分忽略局部细节的问题，提出Tool-IQA，为VLM配备放大镜和伽马校正工具，通过结构化管道和批量感知训练策略提升评估。在CLIVE数据集上PLCC达0.854，超越现有方法。

<h3 id="330">[330] VinQA: Visual Elements Interleaved Long-form Answer Generation for Real-World Multimodal Document QA</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16092](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16092) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Young Rok Jang, Hyesoo Kong, Kyunghwan An, Jae Sub Huh, Gyeonghun Kim, Stanley Jungkyu Choi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Document Understanding, Question Answering, Multimodal Learning
- **Venue**: CVPR 2026
- **Summary**: 针对文档QA中视觉元素利用不足的问题，提出VinQA数据集，支持视觉元素与文字交织的长答案生成，并研究了Page Encoding和Modality Encoding两种编码方法。微调Qwen2.5-VL后性能显著提升，Modality Encoding在复杂文档中更鲁棒。

<h3 id="331">[331] Long-Context Modeling via GSS-Transformer Hybrid Architecture with Learnable Mixing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16093](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16093) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kuzey Torlak, Hüseyin Arda Arslan, Anıl Dervişoğlu, Beyza Nur Deniz, Onur Boyar
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Long Context, Transformer Architecture, State Space Model
- **Summary**: 为平衡Transformer二次复杂度与SSM线性复杂度但存在检索瓶颈的问题，提出并行混合架构PHA，独立运行GSS、GQA和FFN分支并通过可学习混合融合。在WikiText-103上达到16.51 PPL，同时吞吐量提高24%，内存降低40%。

<h3 id="332">[332] Scaling Adaptive Depth with Norm-Agnostic Residual Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16112](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16112) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tomás Figliolia, Beren Millidge
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Depths, Layer Pruning, Residual Networks
- **Summary**: 针对残差网络深层更新贡献被残差范数增长抑制的问题，提出范数无关残差架构NAG，分离方向与幅度，并实现可解释的Mixture-of-Depths机制。在等计算量训练下，约20-25%的层跳过率可匹配全深度性能，同时大幅减少FLOPs。

<h3 id="333">[333] AuAu: A Benchmark for Auditing Authoritarian Alignment in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16127](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16127) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Andreas Einwiller, Max Klabunde, Florian Lemmerich
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, AI Ethics, Authoritarian Alignment
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/andreaseinwiller/AuAu)
- **Summary**: 为评估LLM生成威权倾向内容的风险，提出AuAu基准，融合心理测量、情境小插图及真实用户提示三个维度。对17个模型测试发现，心理测量中威权响应率普遍较高，且系统提示可轻易操纵模型输出，凸显了持续审计的必要性。

<h3 id="334">[334] InvDesMobility: a reliability-gated first-principles feedback framework for closed-loop materials discovery</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16133](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16133) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wen-Kao Li, Ze-Feng Gao, Peng-Jie Guo, Wei Ji, Zhong-Yi Lu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Materials Science, Inverse Materials Design, DFT
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/DreamLufei/invDesMobility)
- **Summary**: 针对逆材料设计中昂贵计算结果的验证与反馈不足，提出InvDesMobility框架，集成多智能体自动DFT、证据分层、生成结构提议和可审计发布。在筛选240万结构后，提交102个候选，保留86个可靠性门控通道，实现了闭环发现。

<h3 id="335">[335] XAI-Grounded Explanation Generation for Speech Deepfake Detection with Training-Free Multimodal Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16137](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16137) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yupei Li, Qiyang Sun, Xiaoliang Wu, Chenxi Wang, Berrak Sisman, Björn W. Schuller
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Deepfake Detection, Explainable AI, Multimodal LLM
- **Venue**: Interspeech 2026
- **Summary**: 为解决语音深度伪造检测中XAI低层可理解性差与LLM解释缺乏依据的问题，提出将XAI证据与多模态LLM集成的免训练框架。在PartialSpoof数据集上，使用XAI的方法内部准确率提升超过45%，生成更具体、有依据的解释。

<h3 id="336">[336] A Comprehensive Survey of Medical Image Segmentation: Challenges, Benchmarks, and Beyond</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16153](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16153) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pengyu Zhu, Xiaojing Zhang, Kunbo Zhang, Chunyan Zhang, Zhenyu Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Image Segmentation, Survey, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/andrew-pengyu/Awsome_MedSeg/tree/main)
- **Summary**: 本文综述了医学图像分割的发展，覆盖U-Net、Transformer和SAM等方法，统一分析数据集、评价指标和挑战。旨在指导未来研究并推动临床转化，相关资源开源。

<h3 id="337">[337] A comparative and critical study of EEGNet for fNIRS-driven cognitive load classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16160](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16160) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mehshan Ahmed Khan, Houshyar Asadi, Li Zhang, Mohammad reza Chalak Qazani, Ghazal Bargshady, Stefanos gkikas, Christian arzate, Sam Oladazimi, Zoran Najdovsk, Lei Wei, Chee Peng Lim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: fNIRS Classification, Cognitive Load Classification
- **Summary**: 针对fNIRS认知负荷分类中时间变异性和个体差异导致的泛化难题，系统评估了EEGNet模型，考察了时间分割策略、窗口长度、特征提取方法、学习率配置和评估协议的影响。随机分割下重叠窗口和小固定学习率（0.01-0.001）准确率最高，但独立于主体（SI）评估中准确率大幅下降，非重叠窗口在SI下表现更好，最佳准确率56.11%（PCA，20秒窗口，lr=0.1）。结果表明分割策略和学习率选择对泛化至关重要。

<h3 id="338">[338] LLM-Powered Virtual Population for Demand Simulation and Pricing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16183](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16183) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chengpiao Huang, Kaizheng Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Demand Simulation, Pricing Optimization
- **Summary**: 针对产品定价决策中需要非结构化信息需求预测和不确定性估计的问题，提出基于LLM的虚拟人口模型，通过有限混合客户角色和LLM输出的购买概率聚合得到需求分布。在H&M时尚数据集上，校准后的LLM模拟器预测性能最佳，支持样本高效定价，并生成完整预测分布以支持风险感知决策。

<h3 id="339">[339] Embedded Arena: Iterative Optimization via Hardware Feedback</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16190](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16190) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhihan Zhang, Alexander Le Metzger, Jiuyang Lyu, Chun-Cheng Chang, Jiayi Shao, Yujia Liu, Emmanuel Azuh Mensah, Edward Wang, Kurtis Heimerl, Gregory D. Abowd, Shwetak Patel, Natasha Jaques, Vikram Iyer
- **Subjects**: cs.AR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Hardware-in-the-Loop, Model Compression
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ubicomplab/embedded-arena)
- **Summary**: 针对嵌入式设备上模型优化需同时满足内存、功耗和精度等多维硬约束的挑战，提出硬件在环的agent框架，让LLM agent根据真实硬件反馈迭代优化模型和固件。无硬件反馈时部署成功率为0%，而硬件在环方法3次迭代内首次成功部署，7次超越人类专家，实现视觉模型250倍压缩（精度损失\<3.3%）和音频模型400倍压缩（FER\<6%），并成功部署于太阳能驱动的麋鹿检测相机和儿童语音转录可穿戴设备。

<h3 id="340">[340] Cascaded Sparse Autoencoders Learn Multi-Level Visual Concepts in Multimodal LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16193](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16193) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yusong Zhao, Hengyi Wang, Tanuja Ganu, Akshay Nambi, Hao Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Sparse Autoencoder, Multimodal LLM, Mechanistic Interpretability
- **Summary**: 针对多模态大模型视觉表示缺乏层次结构可解释性的问题，提出级联稀疏自编码器（CSAEs），通过在第一个SAE解码器权重上训练二级SAE来学习高层概念。在Qwen3-VL、Gemma-3和LLaVA上实验表明，CSAEs相比现有SAE方法在层次概念连贯性上有显著提升，并且学到的概念组支持有效的组级干预。

<h3 id="341">[341] EgoPhys: Learning Generalizable Physics Models of Deformable Objects from Egocentric Video</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16202](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16202) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hyunjin Kim, Ri-Zhao Qiu, Guangqi Jiang, Xiaolong Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Deformable Object Manipulation, Egocentric Vision, Sim-to-Real
- **Summary**: 针对从第一人称视频预测可变形物体动力学难以泛化的问题，提出EgoPhys框架，从RGB视频构建可变形物理数字孪生，通过将每物体逆物理解蒸馏到码本实现零样本泛化。在xArm6机器人上，单个人类播放视频初始化的数字孪生可作为世界模型辅助可变形物体规划，优于基线方法。

<h3 id="342">[342] LUCID: Learned Undersampling-Adaptive Consistency-Guided Inference with Deterministic Flow Matching for Sparse-View CT Reconstruction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16212](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16212) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jigang Duan, Jiayi Wang, Heran Wang, Ping Yang, Genwei Ma, Xing Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, CT Reconstruction, Flow Matching
- **Summary**: 针对稀疏视角CT重建中角度欠采样导致严重病态和幻觉的问题，提出基于Flow Matching的稀疏自适应一致性引导重建框架Lucid。仅用高质量CT训练，推理时根据采样密度调整生成轨迹并引入投影域数据一致性。实验表明Lucid在不同采样密度下稳定提升图像质量，减少幻觉结构。

<h3 id="343">[343] Calibrated Sampling-Free Uncertainty Estimation in Bayesian Deep Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16214](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16214) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tobias Jan Wieczorek, Leon de Andrade, Thomas Möllenhoff, Marcus Rohrbach
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Uncertainty Estimation, Bayesian Deep Learning, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 针对贝叶斯深度学习测试时多次采样成本高昂的问题，提出校准方差传播（CVP），通过新归一化层传播方法和轻量校准实现单次前向传播的不确定性估计。在BEiT-3视觉推理和ViLT VQA任务上，CVP将0.5%风险覆盖率分别从8.2%提升至14.6%和从2.6%提升至10.8%，且计算成本远低于MC采样。

<h3 id="344">[344] PACT: Privileged Trace Co-Training for Multi-Turn Tool-Use Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16215](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16215) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhenbang Du, Jun Luo, Zhiwei Zheng, Xiangchi Yuan, Kejing Xia, Dachuan Shi, Qirui Jin, Qijia He, Shaofeng Zou, Yingbin Liang, Wenke Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对多轮工具使用agent后训练中RL稀疏奖励和SFT过约束的问题，提出PACT框架，利用专家轨迹作为训练时优化信号，通过轨迹条件RL和组件感知SFT损失结合提示锚定指导优化。在FTRL、BFCL和ToolHop上实验表明，PACT一致优于强SFT和RL基线。

<h3 id="345">[345] From Tokens to Regions: CUDA-Sensitive Instruction Tuning for GPU Kernel Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16231](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16231) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wentao Chen, Jiace Zhu, Xing Zhe Chai, Zeng Qu, Qiaoling Xiao, Liucheng Duan, An Zou
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, CUDA Kernel Generation, Instruction Tuning
- **Summary**: 针对大模型生成CUDA内核困难且现有方法成本高或未显式建模CUDA敏感性的问题，提出CuSeT后训练方法，结合自适应令牌掩码和区域感知样本重加权。在不同模型和规模上实验表明，CuSeT一致提升功能正确性，优于标准SFT并达到与前沿模型竞争的性能。

<h3 id="346">[346] Propagating Structural Guidance: Synthesizing Fluorescein Angiography from Fundus Images and Sparse OCT Scans</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16234](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16234) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tengfei Ma, Ruiqi Wu, Chenran Zhang, Ye Geng, Na Su, Xiangyuan Duanmu, Tao Zhou, Yi Zhou, Wen Fan
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Image Synthesis, Multimodal Fusion
- **Venue**: MICCAI 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/while-plus/OCT-guide-FFA-Syn)
- **Summary**: 针对眼底图像合成荧光素血管造影（FFA）缺乏功能血管信息的问题，提出利用OCT结构引导的多模态合成框架，构建首个眼底、FFA和OCT三模态配准数据集。通过空间对齐跨模态融合和令牌级对齐对比学习，方法在合成质量上超越现有方法，且下游疾病诊断性能显著提升。

<h3 id="347">[347] SPARK: Security Knowledge Priming and Representation-Guided Knowledge Activation for LLM-based Secure Code Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16244](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16244) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiaoyun Xu, Lichao Wu, Jona te Lintelo, Siyu Zhang, Stjepan Picek
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Security, Prompt Engineering
- **Summary**: 针对大模型生成代码具有安全漏洞的问题，提出SPARK推理时安全挂接方法，无需再训练：检索CWE条目并附加提示（组件I）以及添加预计算令牌偏置（组件II）。在9个开源模型和7个黑盒模型上，SPARK匹配或超越微调和RAG基线，同时保持HumanEval准确率。

<h3 id="348">[348] Data Augmentations for Data-Constrained Language Model Pretraining</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16246](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16246) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Michael K. Chen, Xikun Zhang, Zhen Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Pre-training, Data Augmentation, Language Modeling
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/michaelchen-lab/data-augmentations-for-pretraining)
- **Summary**: 针对数据受限下语言模型预训练多轮训练过拟合的问题，研究了三种数据增强正则化：令牌噪声、序列排列和目标偏移预测。消融实验表明随机令牌替换效果最佳，组合增强进一步降低验证损失，有效缓解过拟合。

<h3 id="349">[349] Learned Image Compression for Vision-Language-Action Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16253](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16253) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hyeonjun Kim, Jegwang Ryu, Sangbeom Ha, Junhyeok Lee, Jun-Hyuk Kim, Hyemin Ahn, Jaeho Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Compression, Robotics, Vision-Language-Action
- **Summary**: 针对VLA模型中多相机视觉通信成为实时控制瓶颈的问题，提出SPARC自适应比特率学习图像压缩框架，根据任务相关性分配latent比特率并引入倾斜率损失。在RoboCasa365等机器人基准上，SPARC在相同比特率下控制性能优于传统和近期方法，在真实远程控制中也改善了比特率-成功率权衡。

<h3 id="350">[350] Variance Reduction for Non-Log-Concave Sampling with Applications to Inverse Problems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16257](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16257) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: M. Berk Sahin, Ahmet Ege Tanriverdi, Behzad Sharif, Abolfazl Hashemi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Sampling, Variance Reduction, Inverse Problem
- **Venue**: Uncertainty in Artificial Intelligence (UAI) 2026
- **Summary**: 针对非对数凹分布采样中随机梯度方差大的问题，首次统一分析方差缩减技术（STORM等）用于采样，建立了Fisher信息量和总变差距离下的非渐近收敛率并推广到逆问题。实验在图像逆问题中验证了方差缩减一致提升样本质量。

<h3 id="351">[351] UXBench: Measuring the Actionability of LLM-Generated UX Critiques</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16262](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16262) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wenjie Wang, Yue Huang, Zipeng Ling, Han Bao, Hang hua, Xiaonan Luo, Yu Jiang, Shiyi Du, Yuexing Hao, Xiaomin Li, Yuchen Ma, Dianzhuo Wang, Yanfang Ye, Xiangliang Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, LLM-as-a-Judge, Usability Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对缺乏可靠基准评估LLM生成的UX批评可操作性的问题，提出UXBench，包含10个产品系列的可运行Web fixture和覆盖门控浏览器探索，通过下游修复agent的改进衡量报告质量。评估8个前沿模型显示UX评判未饱和，模型间在可操作性、修复特征和表面类别上存在显著差异。

<h3 id="352">[352] RealityBridge: Bridging Editable 3D Gaussian Splatting Driving Simulations and Real-World Videos</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16278](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16278) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhenhua Wu, Yun Pang, Mingkun Chang, Yuwei Ning, Liangzhi Wang, Yi Xiao, Guanbin Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, 3D Gaussian Splatting, Sim-to-Real
- **Summary**: 针对可编辑3DGS驾驶仿真视频存在模拟到现实差距（伪影、光照不一致、闪烁）的问题，提出RealityBridge结构保持的Sim-to-Real框架，利用多模态条件（渲染视频、掩码、边缘、语义）和轻量GateNet自适应分配，结合自回归长视频训练和奖励后训练。在内部和公开数据集上，方法在伪影去除、光照协调和长序列时间一致性上优于现有方法。

<h3 id="353">[353] Who Should Lead Decoding Now? Tracking Reliable Trajectories for Ensembling Masked Diffusion Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16281](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16281) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Heecheol Yun, Joonhyung Park, Joowon Kim, Eunho Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, LLM Reasoning, Ensemble Learning
- **Summary**: 针对掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）集成知识的问题，提出基于轨迹的迭代集成框架TIE，通过追踪置信度动态并在模型间传递部分去噪序列来融合知识。实验表明该方法在多种推理任务上表现优异。

<h3 id="354">[354] FlowMPC: Improving Flow Matching policies with World Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16286](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16286) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chandon Hamel
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Flow Matching, World Model, Robot Manipulation
- **Summary**: 针对流匹配（FM）行为克隆策略未直接最大化期望回报的问题，提出FlowMPC框架，将模仿学习的FM策略与学习的世界模型结合，在测试时通过MPPI规划改进策略。在ManiSkill操作任务中，该方法显著提升了末端成功率。

<h3 id="355">[355] An affordable hardware-aware neural architecture search for deploying convolutional neural networks on ultra-low-power computing platforms</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16290](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16290) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Andrea Mattia Garavagno, Edoardo Ragusa, Antonio Frisoli, Paolo Gastaldo
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AutoML, Edge Computing, TinyML
- **Summary**: 针对现有硬件感知神经架构搜索（HW-NAS）面向高性能微控制器、不适用于超低功耗传感节点的问题，提出轻量级HW-NAS方法生成适配超低功耗微控制器的微型CNN。在多个微型计算机视觉基准上，该方法在保持分类精度的同时实现了高效搜索。

<h3 id="356">[356] AI Supply Chain Galaxy: 3D Visual Analytics for License Compliance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16292](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16292) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Weiru Han, Xuetao Shi, Wenyi He, Wei Wang, Rui Zhao, Moming Duan
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
