- **Tags**: AI Governance, Data Visualization, Compliance Monitoring
- **Summary**: 针对传统合规工具难以处理大规模多跳依赖网络的问题，提出AI供应链银河（AISCG）交互式3D可视化系统，整合结构依赖与规则引擎进行模型溯源与合规审计。对Hugging Face上908,449个模型的分析显示，55.46%的模型存在合规风险，并揭示了许可漂移等模式。

<h3 id="357">[357] Is Your Trajectory Displacement Safe in Long-tail?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16313](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16313) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qiao Sun, Weicheng Zheng, Yixin Huang, Hang Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Safety Evaluation, Benchmark, Long-Tailed Learning
- **Summary**: 针对自动驾驶长尾场景评估缺乏安全感知与可解释性的问题，提出FluidTest评估管道，包含成对WebUI标注协议、32种语义威胁分类以及三智能体验证系统。实验发现，尽管规划器取得了高评分，但在65%的测试轨迹中仍存在额外安全威胁。

<h3 id="358">[358] RL-Index: Reinforcement Learning for Retrieval Index Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16316](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16316) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yongjia Lei, Nedim Lipka, Zhisheng Qi, Utkarsh Sahu, Koustava Goswami, Franck Dernoncourt, Ryan A. Rossi, Yu Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Information Retrieval, RAG, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对隐式推理任务中检索依赖表面语义匹配的问题，提出RL-Index智能索引框架，将检索索引推理转化为强化学习问题，通过GRPO优化文档的LLM生成原理。在BRIGHT基准上，该方法显著提升了检索与问答性能，并降低了在线延迟。

<h3 id="359">[359] Gaming-Resistant Insurance Contracts for Autonomous AI Agents: Strategy-Proof Toll Mechanism Design</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16326](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16326) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hao-Hsuan Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.GT; cs.AI; q-fin.RM
- **Tags**: AI Safety, Mechanism Design, Game Theory
- **Summary**: 针对自主AI代理保险合约的博弈抵抗性问题，刻画了五种攻击面并提出相应合约条款（包括共同控制聚合、接口违规处理、模型身份菜单），证明了联合激励兼容性。通过两参数保费族实现个体理性与弱预算平衡。

<h3 id="360">[360] ArtBoost: Synthetic Articulatory Data Augmentation for Acoustic-to-Articulatory Inversion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16327](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16327) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hyung Kyu Kim, Byungchan Hwang, Hak Gu Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Speech Processing, Data Augmentation
- **Venue**: Interspeech26
- **Summary**: 针对声-发音反演（AAI）依赖有限EMA数据的问题，提出ArtBoost数据增强策略，利用大规模语音-网格数据提取伪发音轨迹进行预训练。在多种AAI架构上，该方法一致提升了PCC和RMSE指标。

<h3 id="361">[361] SMEPilot: Characterizing and Optimizing LLM Inference with Scalable Matrix Extensions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16332](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16332) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Feiyang Chen, Haibo Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.PF
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Hardware Acceleration, Edge Computing
- **Summary**: 针对CPU矩阵扩展单元（SME）与核心共享内存带宽导致LLM推理效率低的问题，提出SMEPilot推理引擎，基于roofline模型为每个算子选择CPU独占、SME独占或协同执行。在多种平台上，端到端推理性能提升达3.94倍。

<h3 id="362">[362] Communication-Efficient Verifiable Attention for LLM Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16352](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16352) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ziqun Chen, Ming Wu, Michael Heinrich, Jason Zeng, Huiying Lan, Tianwei Zhang, Rui Tan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Verifiable Computing, Hardware Security
- **Summary**: 针对可验证LLM推理中TEE-GPU通信开销大的问题，提出VeriAttn方法，将注意力计算全部卸载至GPU，TEE仅执行验证，并采用两级流水线和分区策略。在Intel TDX平台上，预填和解码阶段分别实现2.60-3.38倍和3.86-5.42倍加速。

<h3 id="363">[363] What Should a Streaming Video Model Remember?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16353](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16353) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haonan Ge, Yiwei Wang, Hang Wu, Yujun Cai
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Memory Architecture, Visual Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对流式视频理解中不加区分注入历史信息会稀释当前场景感知的问题，提出SelectStream选择性潜在记忆框架，利用惊奇驱动自适应窗口、优先级保留合并和查询条件图推理。该方法在StreamingBench上达到82.67%，超越强基线。

<h3 id="364">[364] The Proxy Knows Too Much: Sealing LLM API Routers with Attested TEEs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16358](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16358) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sipeng Xie, Qianhong Wu, Hengrun Lu, Ziliang Sun, Qi Wu, Bo Qin, Qin Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.ET; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Hardware Security, Privacy
- **Summary**: 针对LLM API路由器作为中间人可读取/篡改明文交互的问题，提出AEGIS体系，使用TEE将明文处理限制在小型硬件飞地中，客户端验证飞地后释放明文。实验表明该方法能阻止所有恶意路由器攻击，单请求开销仅约6毫秒。

<h3 id="365">[365] Tyler: Typed Latent Reasoning for Language Models -- When to Think, What to Compute, and How Much to Allocate</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16360](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16360) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hanyu Lin, Min Cai, Jiawei Wen, Haodi Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Chain-of-Thought, Inference Optimization
- **Summary**: 针对现有潜在推理方法预定义何时调用和分配计算预算的问题，提出Tyler框架，学习策略在解码步骤中选择文本令牌或专门潜在计算模块，并动态分配预算。在多个LLM上，准确率比CoT提升最多14.49点。

<h3 id="366">[366] Input-Dependent Fisher Information for Local Sensitivity Analysis of Medical Image Classifiers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16362](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16362) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sourya Sengupta. Mark A. Anastasio
- **Subjects**: eess.IV; cs.AI; cs.IT
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Medical AI, Medical Imaging
- **Summary**: 针对医学图像分类器事后解释方法启发式且间接的问题，提出基于输入依赖Fisher信息矩阵（iFIM）的局部敏感性分析框架，通过格拉姆矩阵恢复iFIM特征空间。实验表明高敏感性成分与预测置信度变化更相关。

<h3 id="367">[367] LectūraAgents: A Multi-Agent Framework for Adaptive Personalized AI-Assisted Learning and Embodied Teaching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16428](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16428) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jaward Sesay, Yue Yu, Siwei Dong, Yemin Shi, Guangyao Chen, Börje F. Karlsson
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Education Technology, Multi-Agent System, Embodied AI, Personalized Learning
- **Summary**: 针对现有教育代理主要聚焦讲座内容自动化、缺乏适应性具身教学的问题，提出LectūraAgents多智能体框架，包含层级化架构、适应性具身教学机制和教学动作-语音对齐算法。在多个课程级别上，该方法在教学质量和个性化方面均优于现有方法。

<h3 id="368">[368] ACCORD: Action-Conditioned Contextual Grounding for Language Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16432](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16432) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lai Jiang, Cheng Qian, Zhenhailong Wang, Pan Lu, Heng Ji, Hao Peng
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Interactive Reasoning, Tool Learning
- **Summary**: 针对LLM代理无法从环境中挖掘隐式假设导致指令执行失败的问题，提出ACCORD框架，每次动作前主动探测缺失信息并整合轨迹上下文。在AppWorld上任务完成率提升最高20.6个百分点，且泛化到具身环境。

<h3 id="369">[369] Autonomous End-to-End SOH Prediction Services for Battery Systems via Temporal-Contrastive Representation Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16434](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16434) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Junting Wen, Dan Li, Qihao Quan, Xiwen Wang, Hang Yang, Zhaohong Meng, Zigui Jiang, Changlin Yang, Tianle Liu, Diego Muñoz-Carpintero, Jian Lou
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Battery SOH Estimation
- **Summary**: 该论文针对锂离子电池健康状态（SOH）估计中依赖人工特征工程和黑盒模型的问题，提出了TC-SOH模块化即插即用服务架构，通过时间对比机制和跨窗口预测任务直接从原始数据提取退化相关表示。在四个公开数据集上，TC-SOH将MAPE降低1.91倍，RMSE降低2.13倍，并优于多种物理信息与数据驱动基线。

<h3 id="370">[370] NeuronFabric: A Software Reference Architecture for On-Chip Transformer Training with Local Adam</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16440](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16440) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Evgeny Ukladchikov
- **Subjects**: cs.AR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Transformer Architecture, On-Device Learning, Hardware Architecture
- **Summary**: 该论文提出NeuronFabric，一种用于芯片上Transformer训练的软件参考架构，支持本地Adam优化，旨在为FPGA和ASIC实现提供数值正确性和内存需求验证。334K参数模型在莎士比亚语料上训练，BF16W配置评估损失1.5426，略高于FP32的1.5224，但内存需求从4.0MB降至3.34MB。

<h3 id="371">[371] Training and Evaluating Diffusion Policies with Long Context Lengths</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16447](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16447) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Abhinav Agarwal, Adam Wei, Taylan Kargin, Michael Zeng, Cole Becker, Arif Kerem Dayi, Pablo Parrilo, Asuman Ozdaglar, Russ Tedrake
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Imitation Learning, Diffusion Model, Robotics
- **Summary**: 该论文系统研究了模仿学习中上下文长度的影响，发现在合适的条件方法和去噪骨干（UNet+交叉注意力）下，简单缩放上下文长度并不像先前声称的那般脆弱。提出联合训练多种上下文长度的算法以降低样本复杂度，并重新评估了先前提出的长上下文解决方案。

<h3 id="372">[372] SDS-LoRA: Overcoming Anisotropic Gradient Scaling in Low-Rank Adaptation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16454](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16454) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Junghun Oh, Sungyong Baik, Kyoung Mu Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LoRA, Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 该论文揭示了低秩适应（LoRA）中各向异性梯度缩放问题，该问题扭曲了全微调梯度并降低了有效秩。提出SDS-LoRA，通过结构解耦奇异值从反向传播中移除尺度影响，使收敛率独立于低秩矩阵的条件数。在自然语言和视觉任务上，SDS-LoRA缩小了与全微调的差距。

<h3 id="373">[373] SPRI: SVD-Partitioned Residual Initialization for Data-Constrained MoE Upcycling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16456](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16456) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Weiqiao Shan, Ruixiang Mao, Yuang Li, Yuhao Zhang, Yingfeng Luo, Tong Zheng, Chen Xu, Yucheng Qiao, Chunxiang Jin, Yi Yuan, Jingdong Chen, Tong Xiao, Jingbo Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, MoE Upcycling
- **Summary**: 该论文针对数据受限情况下混合专家（MoE）升级性能差的问题，提出SPRI方法，利用SVD分解的残差初始化路由专家，引入基于预训练频谱结构的受控多样性。在CoVoST2多语言语音翻译上，SPRI较完全微调稠密模型平均BLEU提升2.58，COMET提升3.32。

<h3 id="374">[374] Learning aligned EEG representations with subject-specific encoders</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16462](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16462) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bruna J. Lopes, Gabriel Schwartz, Sylvain Chevallier, Raphael Y. de Camargo, Bruno Aristimunha
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: EEG Classification, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文研究了跨被试EEG解码中分布偏移问题，提出使用被试特定编码器加共享分类器的混合模型。在四个运动想象数据集上，混合模型优于标准EEGNet等基线，且被试特定头增强了类别区分性，但新被试的头选择仍是瓶颈。

<h3 id="375">[375] AI systems out-persuade expert humans</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16475](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16475) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kobi Hackenburg, Caroline Wagner, Luke Hewitt, Ben M. Tappin, Ed Saunders, Hannah Rose Kirk, Helen Margetts, Christopher Summerfield
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Persuasion, Human-AI Interaction
- **Summary**: 该论文通过四项预注册实验（18978次对话）证明，AI系统在说服力上可靠地超过专家人类（包括世界辩论冠军），即使专家提前研究、结构化练习并有现金激励。AI的优势源于快速部署更多信息，且在实际捐赠中AI比专业募捐者高效近3倍。

<h3 id="376">[376] Uncertainty Quality of VGGT: An Analysis on the DTU Benchmark Dataset</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16479](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16479) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Markus Hillemann, Robert Langendörfer, Steven Landgraf, Markus Ulrich
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D Reconstruction, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Venue**: ISPRS Annals of the Photogrammetry, Remote Sensing and Spatial Information Sciences
- **Summary**: 该论文分析了VGGT（CVPR2025最佳论文）生成的不确定性质量，重点关注其深度和3D重建的置信度。在DTU基准上确定了有效置信度阈值，并展示了提升不确定性质量对改善3D重建精度的潜力。

<h3 id="377">[377] HOLO-MPPI: Multi-Scenario Motion Planning via Hierarchical Policy Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16480](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16480) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Youngjae Min, Jovin D&#39;sa, Faizan M. Tariq, David Isele, Navid Azizan, Sangjae Bae
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Motion Planning, Autonomous Driving, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文提出HOLO-MPPI框架，结合离线学习的高层策略与在线MPPI随机最优控制，实现多场景运动规划。在自动驾驶场景中，HOLO-MPPI优于MPPI和端到端RL基线，同时保持实时控制。

<h3 id="378">[378] Unified Multimodal Model for Brain MRI Imputation and Understanding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16484](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16484) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhiyun Song, Che Liu, Tian Xia, Avinash Kori, Wenjia Bai
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.MM
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Medical AI, Medical Image Imputation
- **Venue**: MICCAI 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文提出UniBrain，一种统一的脑MRI多模态模型，通过联合训练图像模态插补与图像理解，解决模态缺失问题。采用自对齐策略学习细粒度解剖特征，并引入动态隐藏状态机制缓解长上下文推理中的曝光偏差。在多疾病数据集上实现高精度诊断。

<h3 id="379">[379] Lost at the End: Primacy Bias in Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Question Answering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16494](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16494) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jieyuan Liu, Jianyang Gu, Shijie Chen, Jefferson Chen, Zhen Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Multimodal Retrieval, LLM Evaluation, Long Context
- **Summary**: 该论文研究了多模态KB-VQA中读者端的位置依赖性，发现其形状从纯文本的U型变为首因偏差（“末尾丢失”）。在三个VLM上，黄金首位比末位高16-26个百分点，且检索端修复无法消除该差距，表明需要读者端干预。

<h3 id="380">[380] daVinci-kernel: Co-Evolving Skill Selection, Summarization, and Utilization via RL for GPU Kernel Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16497](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16497) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dayuan Fu, Mohan Jiang, Tongyu Wang, Dian Yang, Jiarui Hu, Liming Liu, Jinlong Hou, Pengfei Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: GPU Computing, Code Generation, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文提出daVinci-kernel，一个强化学习框架，通过共演化的技能选择、总结和利用库优化GPU内核。三个代理共享同一LLM骨干，联合训练。在KernelBench上，14B模型在Level 1/2/3上分别达到37.2%、70.6%、32.2%的Fast1阈值，超越先前最强模型。

<h3 id="381">[381] Direction-Conditioned Policies via Compositional Subgoal Scoring for Online Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16515](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16515) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Swaminathan S K, Damiya Gondha, Theyanesh Eswaramoorthy Rajahkrishnan, Aritra Hazra
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Goal-Conditioned RL, Reinforcement Learning
- **Venue**: 2nd Workshop on Compositional Learning at ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文提出方向条件策略（DCP），用于在线目标条件RL，将目标达成分解为子目标评分和方向条件动作。理论证明了方向充分性和可控子空间特征，在九个环境中多数指标优于对比RL，并在操作和障碍交互任务上提升最大。

<h3 id="382">[382] Dual-Granularity Orthogonal Disentanglement for Generalizable Audio Deepfake Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16532](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16532) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhuodong Liu, Hugen Lv, Xiangyu Li, Chunhong Yuan
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Deepfake Detection, Disentanglement
- **Venue**: Interspeech 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文提出双粒度正交解缠框架用于音频深度伪造检测，在样本级和批次级强制执行特征独立性。采用课程解缠调度，无需辅助网络。在ASVspoof 2019 LA等数据集上分别达到1.35%、7.88%、21.58%的EER，跨数据集迁移优于梯度反转解缠。

<h3 id="383">[383] Fast When, Careful Who: Dual-Process Multiparty Turn-Taking with Diffusion Augmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16568](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16568) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rutherford A. Patamia, Ming Liu, Wei Luo, Favour Ekong, Akan Cosgun
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Dialogue System, Speech Processing, Turn-Taking
- **Summary**: 该论文研究多说话人轮换任务，提出两阶段音频管道：快速触发器提出候选转台时间，轻量验证器决定保持或转移。采用扩散增强背景音频混合。在VoxConverse数据集上，该方法改进了转移检测。

<h3 id="384">[384] Learning Interface Breakup: A Geometry-Conditioned Latent Surrogate for Spray Formation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16587](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16587) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Julius H Ramlau, Friedrich Hastedt, Tolga Birdal, Ehecatl-Antonio del Río Chanona, Nausheen S Basha, Omar K Matar
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Surrogate Model, Physics-Informed Learning, Two-Phase Flow
- **Venue**: ICML AI4Physics 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文提出几何条件潜在代理模型，用于喷雾形成中瞬态两相破碎预测。通过编码自适应网格细化的密度场重建瞬态演化，推断时间仅0.045秒，加速超过6×10^4倍，准确捕捉关键界面动力学。

<h3 id="385">[385] Infant Spontaneous Movement Noise Improves Exploration in Deep RL</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16590](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16590) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Francisco M. López, Markus R. Ernst, Francisco Cruz, Matej Hoffmann, and Jochen Triesch
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; q-bio.NC
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Exploration Noise
- **Venue**: IEEE ICDL 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/trieschlab/baby-noise-rl)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对深度强化学习中探索噪声通常是时间不相关的白噪声的问题，提出了一种受婴儿自发运动启发的噪声机制，该噪声的频谱指数随年龄增加。通过逐步增加训练过程中探索噪声的时间自相关性，该方法在多个RL环境中相较于传统探索策略提高了学习效率。

<h3 id="386">[386] ArtNet: A JEPA-Like Articulatory Predictive Framework for Robust Zero-Shot Phoneme Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16595](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16595) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zeqian Hu, Fuliang Weng, Shu Shang, Yaqian Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Phoneme Recognition, Self-Supervised Learning
- **Venue**: Interspeech 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对零样本跨语言音素识别中直接声学到符号映射易受语言变化影响的问题，提出了ArtNet框架，它采用类似JEPA的架构，通过发音特征预测和变分信息瓶颈来抑制语言特定变异。在7种未见语言上，该方法相比基线实现了20.56%的音素错误率相对降低和7.01%的发音特征错误率相对降低。

<h3 id="387">[387] VeriGraph: Towards Verifiable Data-Analytic Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16603](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16603) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiajie Jin, Zhao Yang, Wenle Liao, Yuyang Hu, Guanting Dong, Xiaoxi Li, Yutao Zhu, Zhicheng Dou
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Neurosymbolic AI
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ignorejjj/VeriGraph)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对LLM代理在数据分析任务中输出难以验证的问题，提出了VeriGraph框架，通过构建异质证据有向无环图来追踪推理过程，并设计了基于图的策略优化。实验表明VeriGraph-8B在多个基准上取得最高总体分数，且证据图的接地率达到87.61%。

<h3 id="388">[388] Sycophancy as Material Failure under Pushback Loading: A Multi-Axis Characterization Across Three Loading Cases and up to Seventeen Material Charges</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16617](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16617) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ferdinand M. Schessl
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Sycophancy, LLM Evaluation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/FerdinandSchessl/sycophancy-note-companion)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对LLM阿谀奉承概念边界模糊的问题，采用材料科学框架，通过多轴测量（速度、损伤累积等）在多种加载情况下表征阿谀奉承。结果发现辩论情境下材料特性主导，而错误预设和道德情境下话题主导，且跨判断者可靠性存在差异。

<h3 id="389">[389] Entropy-Gated Latent Recursion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16620](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16620) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Soham Bhattacharjee, Dushyant Singh Chauhan, Salem Lahlou, Martin Takac, Nils Lukas
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Inference-Time Scaling, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 该论文针对推理时扩展仅依赖随机采样的局限性，发现了第二个确定性轴：层跨度递归，并提出了熵门控潜在递归（EGLR）。通过联合L×T轴，在MATH-500上达到91.6%的准确率，比仅温度采样高8.2个百分点，证明了两轴互补性。

<h3 id="390">[390] Using AI in engineering education: a balancing act, driven by clear purpose</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16626](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16626) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Olya Kudina
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Education Technology, AI Literacy
- **Venue**: The Routledge Handbook of the Philosophy of Engineering
- **Summary**: 该论文通过100名工程学生的问卷和文献综述，探讨了学生在工程教育中使用和看待LLM的方式。学生重视写作支持、概念澄清等功能，但担心不准确和过度依赖，文章主张有目的性地整合AI，并强调批判性AI素养。

<h3 id="391">[391] DCP-Prune: Ultra-Low Token Pruning with Distribution Consistency Preservation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16633](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16633) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xifeng Xue, Xiaokang Wang, Zirui Li, Ming-Ming Cheng, Guolei Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Token Compression, Vision-Language Model
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/EMVision-NK/DCP-Prune)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对视觉令牌剪枝在超低预算下性能不稳定的问题，提出了DCP-Prune框架，包括锚点上下文图恢复和文本感知令牌簇选择，并引入分布一致性度量。在LLaVA-1.5-7B上仅用16个视觉令牌保留了92.1%的上界平均性能。

<h3 id="392">[392] Optimising Temporary Accommodation Placement Across London with AI-Powered SaaS in E-Governance Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16652](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16652) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hankun He, Jordan Richards, Gopalakrishnan Netuveli, Kumar Aniket, Ramya Pachatcharam, Binta Ade-olusile, Nathan Nagaiah, Matthew I Bellgard
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.SI
- **Tags**: E-Governance, LLM Application
- **Venue**: International Conference on AI and Sustainability Advances 2026 Companion Proceedings
- **Summary**: 该论文针对伦敦临时住宿安置效率低下问题，开发了DOMUS系统，结合规则过滤和LLM辅助搜索的AI决策支持系统。试运行表明搜索时间大幅减少，安置约束遵守度提高，员工满意度高。

<h3 id="393">[393] PATCH: Action-Chunk-Conditioned Latent Patch Innovation Monitoring for Robot Manipulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16690](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16690) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yanan Zhou, Ranpeng Qiu, Yincong Chen, Jiajie Cui, Weiming Zhi
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation
- **Summary**: 该论文针对机器人操作在意外场景动态下的脆弱性，提出了PATCH运行时监控器，通过预测动作块投影执行走廊内的潜在块演化并累积残差来触发干预。真实机器人实验表明其能产生更稳定且上下文相关的触发信号。

<h3 id="394">[394] Adaptive inference and function vectors in deep transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16694](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16694) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ravin Raj, Gautam Reddy
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; q-bio.NC
- **Tags**: In-Context Learning, Mechanistic Interpretability
- **Summary**: 该论文针对深度Transformer内部机制不明的问题，提出了一个平均场相互作用系统的理论，表明Transformer可利用函数向量在层间以越来越细的尺度推断潜在上下文。在上下文回归任务中验证了预测与非高斯层次结构及深度的关系。

<h3 id="395">[395] Attention is Just Another Name for Coupling?: A Fast-Slow ODE Perspective on Hierarchical Pretraining</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16730](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16730) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhengyuan Gao
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Transformer Architecture, Pre-training
- **Summary**: 该论文探讨了因果自注意力是否可以通过一个更慢的耦合（基于时间下采样）来补充，提出了快慢ODE形式化，并实例化为双路径网络。实验表明在50万token时耦合是中性的，主要贡献在于精确的映射关系。

<h3 id="396">[396] MuVAP: Multimodal Multiparty Voice Activity Projection for Turn-taking Prediction in the Wild</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16731](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16731) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haotian Qi, Gabriel Skantze
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Dialogue System, Turn-Taking
- **Summary**: 该论文针对多人口语轮次预测依赖复杂设备的问题，提出了MuVAP框架，通过将声学预测与面部轨迹结合，实现从单声道音频和单摄像头进行说话人感知的轮次预测。在双人和三人对话中超过了基线。

<h3 id="397">[397] Revealing Artifacts via Noise Amplification: A Novel Perspective for AI-Generated Video Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16742](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16742) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Renxi Cheng, Jie Gui, Hongsong Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI-Generated Video Detection
- **Summary**: 该论文针对AI生成视频检测难题，提出了基于位平面的噪声放大方法，从像素、区域和帧三个层面放大噪声信号用于分类。在GenVidBench和HardGVD基准上显著优于现有方法。

<h3 id="398">[398] Automated jailbreak attack targeting multiple defense strategies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16751](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16751) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qi Wang, Chengcheng Wan, Weijia He, Yanqing Li, Hanqi Sun, Xiaodong Gu, Jiangtao Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Jailbreak Attack, LLM Security
- **Summary**: 该论文针对LLM安全防御问题，提出了UNIATTACK黑盒攻击框架，通过提取攻击特征并优化生成灵活模板，实现对多道防线模型的高效攻击。攻击成功率提升64.63%-248.82%，成本仅为基线的0.03%-4.96%。

<h3 id="399">[399] P3B3: A Multi-Turn Conversational Benchmark for Measuring European and Brazilian Portuguese Variety Bias in LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16753](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16753) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rafael Ferreira, Inês Vieira, Inês Calvo, James Furtado, Iago Paulo, Diogo Tavares, Diogo Glória-Silva, David Semedo, João Magalhães
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Multilingual Learning, LLM Evaluation
- **Venue**: MeLLM Workshop at ACL 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对LLM中欧洲和巴西葡萄牙语变体偏见未被充分探索的问题，构建了P3B3多轮对话基准和评估框架。实验表明大多数LLM表现出对巴西葡萄牙语的强烈偏见。

<h3 id="400">[400] Gen-VCoT: Generative Visual Chain-of-Thought Reasoning via Diffusion-Based RGB Intermediate Representations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16783](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16783) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhiqiang Zhou, Junliang Dai, Xu ling
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Chain-of-Thought, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 该论文针对多模态大语言模型推理缺乏可解释视觉中间表示的问题，提出了Gen-VCoT框架，利用专家视觉模型生成RGB图像作为推理中间步骤。在空间和深度问题上分别提升25%和50%，但简单事实问题可能下降。

<h3 id="401">[401] Decision-Weighted Flow Matching for Contextual Stochastic Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16790](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16790) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jize Xie, Haomiao Wu, Qiang Chen, Xiu Su, Yi Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Flow Matching, Decision Theory
- **Summary**: 该论文针对条件生成模型用于随机优化时标准训练目标与下游决策不匹配的问题，提出了决策加权流匹配（DW-FM）框架，通过重新加权速度回归目标来对齐遗憾值。理论推导了遗憾值与路径速度不匹配的联系，并在三个CVaR基准任务上显示DW-FM显著改善了决策遗憾。

<h3 id="402">[402] Decoupling Semantics from Distortions: Multi-Scale Two-Stream Vision-Language Alignment for AI-Generated Image Quality Assessment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16799](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16799) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zijie Meng
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Quality Assessment, Vision-Language Model
- **Venue**: ICME2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/YMlinfeng/MST-CLIPIQA)
- **Summary**: 现有VLM的AIGIQA方法存在语义与失真维度冲突，本文提出MST-CLIPIQA多尺度双流框架，通过显式解耦表示实现层级视觉-语言对齐，利用双CLIP编码器捕捉全局语义和局部纹理，信息瓶颈融合机制自适应蒸馏。在五个基准上取得SOTA，质量SRCC提升1.11%，图文对应SRCC提升2.35%，仅需0.8M可训练参数。

<h3 id="403">[403] A Perception vs. Distortion Perspective on Score-Based Generative Channel Estimation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16815](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16815) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Marco Skocaj, Lukas Eller, Mate Boban
- **Subjects**: eess.SP; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Channel Estimation, Diffusion Model
- **Summary**: 本文针对无线通信中信道估计问题，从感知-失真权衡角度分析了分数匹配的优势与局限。通过将下游任务建模为信道估计的泛函，量化了标准失真最小化方法的超额风险，数值结果表明在高预测不确定性下分数估计可实现近贝叶斯最优预编码，而低不确定性下判别式方法更优。

<h3 id="404">[404] Tying the Loop -- Tied Expert Layers in Mixture-of-Experts Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16825](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16825) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Martin Jaggi
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, Model Compression
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/epfml/looped-moe)
- **Summary**: 针对MoE架构中专家参数占用大量内存的问题，本文提出Expert Tying，在连续Transformer层间共享专家参数，同时保持独立的逐层路由和注意力。预训练实验表明，该方法可在几乎不降低困惑度和下游质量的情况下将内存占用减少近2倍，实现有利的计算-内存权衡。

<h3 id="405">[405] ATOM-Bench: A Real-World Benchmark for Atomic Skills and Compositional Generalization in Manipulation Policies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16826](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16826) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zenan Wu, Bingqing Wei, Lu Liu, Zheqi He, Xi Wang, Jiakang Liu, Zehui Li, Guocai Yao, Jing-Shu Zheng, Xi Yang, Yongtao Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Benchmark, Compositional Generalization
- **Summary**: 现有的通用操作策略难以诊断真实场景中的泛化能力。本文提出ATOM-Bench基准，将桌面操作分解为原子技能和组合任务，包含30个原子任务和24个组合任务，并引入原子分数和组合失败率以区分失败原因。通过2700次物理滚动评估发现当前策略在精细运动原子和组合重用上仍有不足。

<h3 id="406">[406] Robust Spoofed Speech Detection via Temporal Pyramid Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16837](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16837) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mahtab Masoudi Nezhad, Nima Karimian
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Anti-Spoofing, Audio Processing, Self-Supervised Learning
- **Summary**: 针对欺骗语音检测中跨数据集泛化不足的问题，本文提出时间金字塔适配器，利用不同感受野的并行时间卷积捕获多尺度欺骗线索，并结合自监督XLS-R表示。在多个基准上表现优异，在PartialSpoof数据集上AUC达99.24%，EER 3.87%，显著优于基线。

<h3 id="407">[407] Beyond Models: Reflections on Engineering AI-enabled Systems in a Project-Based Course</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16842](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16842) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Amir Mashmool, Kishan Ravindra Sawant, Mojtaba Shahin, Nico Hochgeschwender, Rainer Koschke
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Education Technology, Software Engineering
- **Summary**: 针对软件工程教育中AI系统集成能力培养不足的问题，本文反思了一门项目式硕士课程的设计，学生开发电影推荐系统并做出架构决策。混合方法研究表明学生在早期架构决策、异构ML集成和需求演变方面存在困难，课程培养了系统级推理能力。

<h3 id="408">[408] Robust Dual-Signal Fusion: Hybrid Neuro-Symbolic Gating with Compressed Chain-of-Thought Refinement for Irony Detection in Social Media Texts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16845](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16845) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ankit Bhattacharjee, Krityapriya Bhaumik
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Figurative Language, Sentiment Analysis, Neurosymbolic AI
- **Summary**: LLM默认字面语义解释导致零样本讽刺检测困难。本文提出鲁棒双信号融合框架，压缩思维链推理轨迹，无需监督微调。在TweetEval上达到78.1%准确率和0.777宏F1，与微调BERTweet持平；在iSarcasm数据集上零样本宏F1达0.6726，超越多个监督集成。

<h3 id="409">[409] Deep Q-Learning on Hölder Spaces</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16846](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16846) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qian Qi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Continuous Control, Neural Operator
- **Summary**: 本文研究连续时间随机控制中Q学习的算子理论，分析了贝尔曼最优目标的正则性和逼近复杂度。在均匀椭圆性和Hölder正则系数下，证明贝尔曼更新将有界输入映射到各向异性正则类，并提出了张量积DeepONet架构，导出了显式的逼近和资源界限。

<h3 id="410">[410] Follow the Latent Roadmap: Navigating Revocable Decoding for Diffusion LLMs with Anchor Tokens</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16847](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16847) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yizhen Yao, Qinglin Zhu, Runcong Zhao, Xiangxiang Dai, Yanzheng Xiang, Yulan He, Lin Gui
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Language Model, Decoding Strategy, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 扩散LLM在并行生成中面临解码速度与质量的权衡。本文提出ASRD框架，在嵌入空间通过锚标记监督可撤销解码，解耦上下文为可信锚标记和不确定候选，引入锚引导生成和锚扰动验证机制。在数学和编程基准上准确率提升高达6.4%，推理吞吐量提升7.2倍。

<h3 id="411">[411] Federated Medical Image Segmentation under Real-World Label Noise: A Benchmark Suite for Noisy Label Learning Method Selection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16868](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16868) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Markus Bujotzek, Dimitrios Bounias, Stefan Denner, Ralf Floca, Maximilian Fischer, Peter Neher, Klaus Maier-Hein
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.DC
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Medical Imaging, Noisy Label Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/MIC-DKFZ/FedSegNoiseBench)
- **Summary**: 联邦学习中真实标签噪声（如轮廓不一致、结构缺失）影响医学图像分割性能。本文提出一个基准套件，结合真实噪声数据集、多种客户端噪声场景和噪声目标评估，为联邦噪声标签学习提供系统评估和方法选择框架。代码已开源。

<h3 id="412">[412] Upper Bounds on the Generalization Error of Deep Learning Models via Local Robustness and Stability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16883](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16883) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Abdul-Rauf Nuhu, Parham M. Kebria, Vahid Hemmati, Mahmoud N. Mahmoud, Edward Tunstel, Abdollah Homaifar
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Adversarial Robustness, Generalization Bound, Image Classification
- **Summary**: 现有基于鲁棒性的泛化界在实际中常过于宽松。本文提出基于局部鲁棒性和稳定性的泛化界，通过按子区域缩放鲁棒性项，从而得到更紧的上界。在ImageNet上的实验表明该界保持非空洞且比现有方法更紧密。

<h3 id="413">[413] Compositional Reasoning Depth Predicts Clinical AI Failure: Empirical Evidence Consistent with Transformer Compositionality Limits in Electronic Health Record Question Answering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16890](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16890) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sanjay Basu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Clinical NLP, LLM Evaluation, Compositional Reasoning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/sanjaybasu/compositional-depth-clinical-ehr)
- **Summary**: 聚合准确率基准掩盖了LLM在电子病历问答中错误的结构性规律。本文提出推理跳跃数分类法，通过预定义的跳跃数预测模型失败。在313个MedAlign问答对上的实验表明，三个模型（Claude, GPT-4o, GPT-5）均显示准确率随跳跃数单调下降，跳跃数是跨架构的强预测因子。

<h3 id="414">[414] Beyond Weights and Gradients: A Taxonomy of Federated Learning Messages</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16891](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16891) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alvaro Javier Vargas Guerrero, Xinguang Wang, Quang Manh Doan, Guy Nagels
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Privacy, Taxonomy
- **Summary**: 联邦学习已超越传统权重和梯度交换，但现有定义未涵盖合成数据等新载荷。本文提出联邦消息的数学定义，并将交换分为模型结构、统计摘要和数据条件表示三类，评估计算、通信和隐私权衡。对202篇论文的评审显示自2021年起消息范式日趋多样化。

<h3 id="415">[415] Semantic Flip: Synthetic OOD Generation for Robust Refusal in Embodied Question Answering and Spatial Localization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16898](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16898) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dongbin Na, Chanwoo Kim, Giyun Choi, Dooyoung Hong
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Out-of-Distribution Detection, Vision-Language Model
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ndb796/SemanticFlip)
- **Summary**: VLM在面对无法回答的查询时往往过度自信，本文提出Semantic Flip框架，通过独立变换查询和视频记忆合成辅助OOD样本，训练轻量拒绝模块，无需外部OOD标注。在空间定位基准上F1达0.9559，优于强提示基线。

<h3 id="416">[416] Binary Tracking for Spatial QA and Navigation with Open Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16902](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16902) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 针对服务机器人长路径的空间问答，本文提出BinTrack，一种完全开源的定位智能体，利用轨迹时间顺序进行二分搜索。在SpaceLocQA基准的全局类别上准确率提升22.8%，并与使用GPT-4o的闭源结果持平，推理加速1.5倍以上。同时发布了GangnamLoop户外多行程基准。

<h3 id="417">[417] IMPACTeen: Intentions, Manipulation, Persuasion, Annotations, and Consequences in Teen Communication Dataset</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16910](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16910) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Social Influence Detection, Dataset
- **Summary**: 该论文针对青少年社交情境中文本影响力检测数据集缺失的问题，通过受限LLM生成和人工编辑构建了IMPACTeen数据集，包含多视角标注（青少年、家长等）。该数据集支持社会影响力检测、标注者分歧和跨语言建模等研究。

<h3 id="418">[418] Demystifying Variance in Circuit Discovery of LLMs</h3>

- **Authors**: Frank Zhengqing Wu, Francesco Tonin, Volkan Cevher
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

<h3 id="419">[419] A Unified Causal-Origin Taxonomy of Distributional Shifts in Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16933](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16933) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 该论文针对强化学习分布偏移（ID/OOD和非平稳）关系不明的问题，提出基于POMDP的因果起源分类法，区分内部和外部偏移，统一了相关概念。该框架支持系统地分析鲁棒性。

<h3 id="420">[420] CrossMaps: Confidence-Aware Open-Vocabulary Semantic Mapping for Rover Navigation</h3>

<h3 id="421">[421] Scalable Circuit Learning for Interpreting Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16939](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16939) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="422">[422] Phantoms and Disclosures: a Causal Framework for Auditing Synthetic Data</h3>

<h3 id="423">[423] Probing Low Frame Rate Degradation in Neural Audio Codecs</h3>

<h3 id="424">[424] How Much Do Reviews Really Contribute? A Study on Text-Enriched Matrix Factorization for Recommendations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16973](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16973) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="425">[425] Stable Menus of Public Goods: AI-Enabled Progress</h3>

- **Summary**: 该论文以公共品稳定菜单问题为测试平台，探索AI辅助经济学研究的工作流。结果发现提供人类直觉和多轮交互有帮助，LLM略逊于博士生。

<h3 id="426">[426] ActiveSAM: Image-Conditional Class Pruning for Fast and Accurate Open-Vocabulary Segmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16996](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16996) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="427">[427] TuneJury: An Open Metric for Improving Music Generation Preference Alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.17006](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17006) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="428">[428] TokenPilot: Cache-Efficient Context Management for LLM Agents</h3>

<h3 id="429">[429] FusionRS: A Large-Scale RGB-Infrared Remote Sensing Dataset for Dual-Modal Vision-Language Foundation Models</h3>

<h3 id="430">[430] HAMON: Passive Optical Sequence Mixing for Long-Horizon Forecasting</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.AR

<h3 id="441">[441] JE-IRT: A Geometric Lens on LLM Abilities through Joint Embedding Item Response Theory</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.22888](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22888) (replaced)

<h3 id="442">[442] Dual-Uncertainty Guided Policy Learning for Multimodal Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.01444](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.01444) (replaced)

- **Summary**: 针对多模态推理忽略视觉感知不确定性，提出DUPL方法，量化感知和输出不确定性引导策略更新。在多模态基准上提升高达12.4%。

<h3 id="443">[443] PISA: A Pragmatic Psych-Inspired Unified Memory System for Enhanced AI Agency</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.15966](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.15966) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shian Jia, Ziyang Huang, Xinbo Wang, Haofei Zhang, Mingli Song
- **Subjects**: cs.AI

- **Summary**: 针对AI agent记忆缺乏适应性和建设性，提出PISA统一记忆系统，受皮亚杰理论启发，采用三模态适应机制和混合记忆访问。在多个基准上取得最先进结果。

<h3 id="444">[444] Sample from What You See: Visuomotor Policy Learning via Diffusion Bridge with Observation-Embedded Stochastic Differential Equation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.07212](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.07212) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhaoyang Liu, Mokai Pan, Zhongyi Wang, Kaizhen Zhu, Haotao Lu, Haipeng Zhang, Jingya Wang, Ye Shi

- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对模仿学习中扩散模型削弱感知-控制耦合，提出BridgePolicy，通过扩散桥接将观测集成到随机动力学中。引入语义对齐器处理异构数据，在仿真和真实任务上超越现有方法。

<h3 id="445">[445] Interpretation as Linear Transformation: A Cognitive-Geometric Model of Concepts and Meaning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.09831](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.09831) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chainarong Amornbunchornvej
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.MA; cs.SI

- **Summary**: 建立几何框架建模异构agent间概念、动机和影响，每个agent用价值空间表示，通过线性解释映射传递。提出无零空间领导条件，统一概念空间、社会认识论和AI价值对齐。

<h3 id="446">[446] Multi-Granular Node Pruning for Causal Circuit Discovery</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.10903](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.10903) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Muhammad Umair Haider, Hammad Rizwan, Hassan Sajjad, A.B. Siddique
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
