- **Summary**: 针对LLM电路发现中迭代剪枝计算昂贵且限于粗粒度，提出多粒度节点剪枝框架，从模块到神经元设置掩码。识别电路节点更少，内存占用减少5-10倍。

<h3 id="447">[447] MedAI: Evaluating TxAgent's Therapeutic Agentic Reasoning in the NeurIPS CURE-Bench Competition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.11682](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11682) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tim Cofala, Christian Kalfar, Jingge Xiao, Johanna Schrader, Michelle Tang, Wolfgang Nejdl
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical AI, LLM Agent, RAG
- **Summary**: 评估TxAgent在CURE-Bench治疗推理中的表现，基于Llama-3.1-8B微调并调用生物医学工具集。分析工具检索质量对性能影响，展示改进策略。

<h3 id="448">[448] Discovering Symmetry Groups with Flow Matching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.20043](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.20043) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuxuan Chen, Jung Yeon Park, Floor Eijkelboom, Jianke Yang, Jan-Willem van de Meent, Lawson L.S. Wong, Robin Walters
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Flow Matching, Symmetry in AI, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 针对自动发现数据中对称性，提出LieFlow框架，将对称性发现建模为李群上分布学习，通过流匹配学习对称群。在点云和ModelNet10上准确发现连续和离散对称性，优于LieGAN。

<h3 id="449">[449] DynaDebate: Breaking Homogeneity in Multi-Agent Debate with Dynamic Path Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.05746](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.05746) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhenghao Li, Zhi Zheng, Wei Chen, Jielun Zhao, Yong Chen, Tong Xu, Enhong Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Reasoning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/nwpuLee2021/brianstorm)
- **Summary**: 针对多智能体辩论中智能体因推理路径同质化而失效的问题，提出DynaDebate框架。通过动态路径生成、过程中心辩论和触发式验证机制，促进智能体多元化推理。实验表明该方法在多个基准上达到最优或极具竞争力的性能。

<h3 id="450">[450] E-mem: Multi-agent based Episodic Context Reconstruction for LLM Agent Memory</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.21714](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21714) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kaixiang Wang, Yidan Lin, Jiong Lou, Zhaojiacheng Zhou, Bunyod Suvonov, Jie Li
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture, Multi-Agent System
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/dog-last/E-mem)
- **Summary**: 现有记忆预处理方法破坏上下文完整性，导致长程推理能力下降。提出E-mem框架，采用异构层次架构，多个辅助智能体维护未压缩记忆，主智能体进行全局规划。在LoCoMo基准上F1达54%，超过GAM 7.75%，同时降低70%以上令牌成本。

<h3 id="451">[451] Edit Knowledge, Not Just Facts via Multi-Step Reasoning over Background Stories</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.02028](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02028) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ya Gao, Kalle Kujanpää, Pekka Marttinen, Harri Valpola, Alexander Ilin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Editing, LLM Reasoning, Knowledge Distillation
- **Summary**: 现有知识编辑仅关注原子事实，无法整合到推理框架中。提出基于背景故事和多步推理的训练策略，通过自生成多跳问题和知识蒸馏，使模型灵活运用新知识。实验表明模型能有效利用新知识进行推理，在多事实组合问题上表现优异。

<h3 id="452">[452] RaBiT: Residual-Aware Binarization Training for Accurate and Efficient LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.05367](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05367) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Youngcheon You, Banseok Lee, Minseop Choi, Seonyoung Kim, Hyochan Chong, Changdong Kim, Youngmin Kim, Dongkyu Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Quantization, LLM Inference
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/SamsungLabs/RaBiT)
- **Summary**: 残差二值化存在路径间特征共适应问题，限制了模型表达能力。提出RaBiT框架，通过算法强制残差层次结构，从单个共享全精度权重顺序推导二值路径。在2位精度下达到SOTA性能，媲美向量量化方法，并在RTX 4090上实现4.49倍加速。

<h3 id="453">[453] JADE: Expert-Grounded Dynamic Evaluation for Open-Ended Professional Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.06486](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06486) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lanbo Lin, Jiayao Liu, Tianyuan Yang, Li Cai, Yuanwu Xu, Lei Wei, Sicong Xie, Guannan Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Agent Evaluation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/smiling-world/JADE)
- **Summary**: 开放式专业任务评估面临严格性与灵活性两难。提出JADE双层评估框架：第一层编码专家知识作为稳定评估标准，第二层进行声明级评估并引入证据依赖门控。在BizBench上提升评估稳定性，发现LLM评估器遗漏的关键失败模式，并有效迁移至医疗等专业领域。

<h3 id="454">[454] ToolSelf: Unifying Task Execution and Self-Reconfiguration via Tool-Driven Emergent Adaptation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.07883](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.07883) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jingqi Zhou, Sheng Wang, Dezhao Deng, Junwen Lu, Junwei Su, Qintong Li, Jiahui Gao, Hao Wu, Jiyue Jiang, Lingpeng Kong, Dunhong Jin, Chuan Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/lian-tian-mo-zun/ToolSelf)
- **Summary**: LLM智能体静态配置导致专用性与泛化性无法兼得。提出ToolSelf范式，将配置更新抽象为标准工具接口，统一执行与自重构，并采用配置感知两阶段训练（CAT）。零样本下即匹配专用智能体，CAT训练后平均提升28.8点。

<h3 id="455">[455] An Attention Mechanism for Robust Multimodal Integration in a Global Workspace Architecture</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.08597](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.08597) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Roland Bertin-Johannet, Lara Scipio, Leopold Maytié, Rufin VanRullen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Attention Mechanism
- **Venue**: ICANN 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/RolandBERTINJOHANNET/GW_attention)
- **Summary**: 多模态系统在模态退化时鲁棒性不足。基于全局工作空间理论，在冻结的多模态工作空间上使用轻量级自上而下的模态选择器。在Simple Shapes和MM-IMDb上，该选择器用更少参数提升鲁棒性，且选择策略可迁移至不同下游任务和未见模态。

<h3 id="456">[456] AgentLeak: A Benchmark for Internal-Channel Privacy Leakage in Multi-Agent LLM Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.11510](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11510) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Faouzi El Yagoubi, Godwin Badu-Marfo, Ranwa Al Mallah
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Privacy, Multi-Agent System, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Privatris/AgentLeak)
- **Summary**: 多智能体LLM系统存在内部通道隐私泄露，现有基准仅测量最终输出。提出AgentLeak基准，覆盖7条隐私相关通道。评估发现，多智能体配置虽降低最终输出泄露（27.2% vs 单智能体43.2%），但内部通道总暴露率达68.9%，智能体间消息泄露高达68.8%。

<h3 id="457">[457] SkillsBench: Benchmarking How Well Agent Skills Work Across Diverse Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.12670](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12670) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xiangyi Li, Yimin Liu, Wenbo Chen, Bingran You, Zonglin Di, Yifeng He, Shenghan Zheng, Kyoung Whan Choe, Jiankai Sun, Shuyi Wang, Chujun Tao, Binxu Li, Xuandong Zhao, Hejia Geng, Xiaojun Wu, Junwei Zhou, Xiaokun Chen, Hanwen Xing, Yubo Li, Qunhong Zeng, Di Wang, Yuanli Wang, Roey Ben Chaim, Penghao Jiang, Haotian Shen, Luyang Kong, Xinyi Liu, Runhui Wang, Xuanqing Liu, Jiachen Li, Xin Lan, Yueqian Lin, Wengao Ye, Junwei He, Songlin Li, Yue Zhang, Yipeng Gao, Yijiang Li, Ze Ma, Liqiang Jing, Tianyu Wang, Kaixin Li, Yiqi Xue, Haoran Lyu, Yizhuo He, Yuchen Tian, Shutong Wu, Bowei Wang, Yixuan Gao, Bo Chen, Litong Liu, Sikai Cheng, Jiajun Bao, Shuaicheng Tong, Shuwen Xu, Terry Yue Zhuo, Tinghan Ye, Qi Qi, Miao Li, Longtai Liao, Zelin Tan, Chang Shi, Xilin Tang, Srinath Tankasala, Boqin Yuan, Yaoyao Qian, Jianhong Tu, Chenguang Wang, Yizhou Sun, Wei Wang, Aaron Taylor, Ziyue Yang, Changkun Guan, Zhikang Dong, Xinyu Zhang, Steven Dillmann, Han-chung Lee, Dawn Song
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Evaluation, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 智能体技能（Agent Skills）缺乏标准评估方法。提出SkillsBench基准，包含8领域87个任务及对应技能和确定性验证器。实验表明，精选技能将平均通过率从33.9%提升至50.5%（+16.6个百分点），且小型模型搭配技能可匹敌大型模型。

<h3 id="458">[458] LLM-WikiRace Benchmark: How Far Can LLMs Plan over Real-World Knowledge Graphs?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.16902](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16902) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Juliusz Ziomek, William Bankes, Lorenz Wolf, Shyam Sundhar Ramesh, Xiaohang Tang, Ilija Bogunovic
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Automated Planning, Knowledge Graph, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 评估LLM在真实知识图谱上的规划与推理能力。提出LLM-WikiRace基准，模型需从源页面逐步导航至目标页面。最强模型Gemini-3在困难模式下仅成功23%，轨迹分析显示模型在失败后难以重新规划，常陷入循环。

<h3 id="459">[459] WorkflowPerturb: Calibrated Stress Tests for Evaluating Multi-Agent Workflow Metrics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.17990](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.17990) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Madhav Kanda, Sharad Agarwal, Rodrigo Fonseca, Alok Gautam Kumbhare, Pedro Las-Casas
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Workflow Automation, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 多智能体工作流评估指标校准不足，数值变化无法反映退化严重性。提出WorkflowPerturb基准，对黄金工作流施加分级扰动（缺失步骤、压缩步骤、描述变更）并评估指标敏感性与校准度。结果揭示了不同指标族的系统差异。

<h3 id="460">[460] The Initial Exploration Problem in Knowledge Graph Exploration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.21066](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21066) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Claire McNamara, Lucy Hederman, Declan O&#39;Sullivan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Human-Computer Interaction, Information Retrieval
- **Summary**: 知识图谱初学者面临初始探索问题（IEP）：不了解可问问题、知识结构及如何开始。从信息行为理论出发，识别出三个相互依赖的障碍：范围不确定性、本体不透明性和查询能力不足。指出设计空间中缺乏范围揭示交互原语。

<h3 id="461">[461] A Model-Free Universal AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.23242](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23242) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yegon Kim, Juho Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Model-Free RL
- **Summary**: 通用强化学习中所有最优智能体均为基于模型的。提出AIQI，首个被证明在通用RL中渐近ε-最优的无模型智能体，通过对分布动作值函数进行通用归纳。证明其在真理粒度条件下渐近最优，并扩展了Self-AIXI的最优性证明。

<h3 id="462">[462] MemPO: Self-Memory Policy Optimization for Long-Horizon Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.00680](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00680) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ruoran Li, Xinghua Zhang, Haiyang Yu, Shitong Duan, Xiang Li, Wenxin Xiang, Chonghua Liao, Xudong Guo, Yongbin Li, Jinli Suo
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture, Reinforcement Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/TheNewBeeKing/MemPO)
- **Summary**: 长程智能体面临上下文增长导致性能下降的问题。提出记忆策略优化算法MemPO，使策略模型在交互中自主总结和管理记忆，通过基于记忆有效性的信用分配机制选择性保留关键信息。F1得分比基础模型提升25.98，比SOTA提升7.1，令牌使用减少67.58%和73.12%。

<h3 id="463">[463] SorryDB: Can AI Provers Complete Real-World Lean Theorems?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.02668](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02668) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Austin Letson, Leopoldo Sarra, Auguste Poiroux, Oliver Dressler, Paul Lezeau, Dhyan Aranha, Frederick Pu, Aaron Hill, Miguel Corredera Hidalgo, Julian Berman, George Tsoukalas, Lenny Taelman
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Automated Theorem Proving, Benchmark, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 现有定理证明基准多为竞赛题目，无法反映真实形式化需求。提出SorryDB，动态更新的Lean任务基准，源自78个GitHub项目。评估表明当前方法互补：基于Gemini Flash的智能体方法虽最有效，但并非严格优于其他LLM或专用证明器。

<h3 id="464">[464] Rescaling Confidence: What Scale Design Reveals About LLM Metacognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.09309](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09309) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuyang Dai, Yuxia Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Uncertainty Estimation, Metacognition
- **Summary**: LLM口头置信度通常采用0-100刻度，但刻度设计鲜受关注。系统操纵刻度粒度、边界和范围规律性后发现：0-20刻度持续改善元认知效率，边界压缩降低性能，且整数偏好在不规则范围下仍存在。表明刻度设计应作为LLM评估的第一类实验变量。

<h3 id="465">[465] Beyond Scalars: Evaluating and Understanding LLM Reasoning via Geometric Progress and Stability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.10384](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10384) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xinyan Jiang, Ninghao Liu, Di Wang, Lijie Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, LLM Hallucination, LLM Evaluation
- **Venue**: ICML2026
- **Summary**: 当前通过标量概率评估LLM可靠性的方法难以捕捉推理的结构动态。本文提出TRACED框架，利用几何运动学中的进度（位移）和稳定性（曲率）来评估推理质量。实验表明，正确推理表现为高进度、稳定的轨迹，而幻觉则呈现低进度、不稳定的模式，该方法在多个基准上取得了竞争性能且鲁棒性更优。

<h3 id="466">[466] Protein Design with Agent Rosetta: A Case Study for Specialized Scientific Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.15952](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15952) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jacopo Teneggi, S.M. Bargeen A. Turzo, Tanya Marwah, Alberto Bietti, P. Douglas Renfrew, Vikram Khipple Mulligan, Siavash Golkar
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Protein Engineering
- **Summary**: 蛋白质设计需要通用工具，现有机器学习方法局限于典型氨基酸和狭窄目标。本文提出Agent Rosetta，将LLM代理与Rosetta物理设计软件结合，支持非规范构建模块。在典型氨基酸设计上匹配专业模型和专家基线，在非规范残基上达到可比性能，表明环境设计对于LLM代理集成专业软件至关重要。

<h3 id="467">[467] EMS: Multi-Agent Voting via Efficient Majority-then-Stopping</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.02863](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02863) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yiqing Liu, Hantao Yao, Wu Liu, Yongdong Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Inference
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/fuyu66/EMS)
- **Summary**: 传统多数投票需要所有代理完成推理，造成大量计算开销。本文提出EMS，通过任务条件可靠性排序、自适应增量投票和可靠性历史更新，在保持精度的同时平均减少35%的调用代理数和44%的令牌消耗。

<h3 id="468">[468] Beyond Predefined Schemas: TRACE-KG for Context-Enriched Knowledge Graph Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.03496](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03496) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Mohammad Sadeq Abolhasani, Yang Ba, Yixuan He, Rong Pan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.IR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Information Extraction
- **Venue**: Graph Foundation Models at ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 知识图谱生成要么依赖预定义本体（成本高）要么无模式提取（图碎片化）。本文提出TRACE-KG，联合构建上下文增强的知识图谱和诱导模式，无需预定义本体。实验表明生成的结构连贯、可追溯的知识图，为两种传统方法提供了实用替代方案。

<h3 id="469">[469] When Do We Need LLMs? A Diagnostic for Language-Driven Bandits</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.05859](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05859) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Uljad Berdica, Fernando Acero, Anton Ipsen, Parisa Zehtabi, Michael Cashmore, Manuela Veloso
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Inference, Decision Making
- **Summary**: 在决策问题中每一步都使用LLM成本高且难以估计不确定性。本文提出LLMP-UCB算法从LLM推导不确定性，同时发现基于文本嵌入的轻量级数值bandit在成本极小的情况下匹配或超过LLM精度，并基于嵌入几何提供诊断何时使用LLM。

<h3 id="470">[470] The Missing Knowledge Layer in Cognitive Architectures for AI Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.11364](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11364) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Michaël Roynard
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Memory Architecture, Knowledge Representation, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 现有认知架构（CoALA、JEPA）缺乏具有独立持久化语义的知识层。本文提出四层分解（知识、记忆、智慧、智能），每层具有不同的持久化语义。在Python和Rust中的实现验证了架构分离的可行性。

<h3 id="471">[471] Emergent Strategic Reasoning Risks in AI: A Taxonomy-Driven Evaluation Framework</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.22119](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22119) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tharindu Kumarage, Lisa Bauer, Yao Ma, Dan Rosen, Yashasvi Raghavendra Guduri, Anna Rumshisky, Kai-Wei Chang, Aram Galstyan, Rahul Gupta, Charith Peris
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Safety, LLM Evaluation, LLM Security
- **Summary**: LLM在推理能力和部署范围增长时可能产生策略性风险（如欺骗、评估博弈、奖励黑客）。本文提出ESRRSim，一个基于分类法的自动化行为风险评估框架。在11个推理LLM上的评估显示检测率从14.45%到72.72%不等，且代际提升显著。

<h3 id="472">[472] Virtual Speech Therapist: A Clinician-in-the-Loop AI Speech Therapy Agent for Personalized and Supervised Therapy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.01101](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01101) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shakeel Sheikh, Patrick Marmaroli, MD Sahidullah, Slim Ouni, Fabrice Hirsch, Goncalo Leal, Bjorn W Schuller
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.SD; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Medical AI, LLM Agent, Speech Processing
- **Summary**: 口吃评估和治疗计划需要自动化和个性化。本文开发VST平台，结合深度学习分类和多代理LLM推理生成个性化的治疗计划，并保持临床医生在回路中。专家评估证实其生成高质量、基于证据的建议。

<h3 id="473">[473] The Model Knows, the Decoder Finds: Future Value Guided Particle Power Sampling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02427](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02427) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tu Nguyen, Matthieu Zimmer, Rasul Tutunov, Xiaotong Ji, Haitham Bou Ammar
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Inference-Time Scaling, Decoding Strategy
- **Summary**: 基础LLM已将非平凡概率质量分配给正确多步解，但瓶颈在于推理时高效定位这些模式。本文提出APPS，一个分块粒子算法，通过未来值引导的选择重新分配计算到竞争前缀。实验表明改善了无训练解码的精度-运行时间权衡。

<h3 id="474">[474] FORTIS: Benchmarking Over-Privilege in Agent Skills</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09163](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09163) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shawn Li, Chenxiao Yu, Han Wang, Wei Yang, Ryan Rossi, Franck Dernoncourt, Xiyang Hu, Philip Yu, Chaowei Xiao, Huan Zhang, Yue Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, AI Safety, LLM Security
- **Summary**: 代理技能层被视为组织抽象，但实际上是一个权限边界，模型经常越界。本文提出FORTIS基准，评估技能选择和执行中的过度权限。在十个前沿模型和三个领域中发现过度权限行为是常态，在非对抗条件下失败率仍很高。

<h3 id="475">[475] LLM Jaggedness Unlocks Scientific Creativity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10574](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10574) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shray Mathur, J. Anibal Boscoboinik, Esther H. R. Tsai, Kevin G. Yager
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Scientific Reasoning, Creative Writing
- **Summary**: AI能力增长不均匀（锯齿状）。本文引入SciAidanBench基准衡量LLM的科学创造力。发现锯齿状跨模型和模型内存在，并可通过推理时计算、知识池化和头脑风暴构建元模型集成，超过任何单一模型。

<h3 id="476">[476] MBABench: Evaluating LLM Agents on End-to-End Spreadsheet Tasks in Finance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.22664](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22664) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Thomson Yen, Julian Poeltl, Harshith Srinivas Gear, Yilin Meng, Joshua Fan, Adam Shen, Yili Liu, Ali Bauyrzhan, Siri Du, Haoyang Liu, Daniel Guetta, Hongseok Namkoong
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Evaluation, Financial AI
- **Summary**: 现有基准不测量从零构建完整电子表格的能力。本文提出MBABench，包含准确度、公式和格式三个维度的评估分类法。Claude系列领先，但即使最强代理也常未达专业财务标准，随难度增加性能急剧下降。

<h3 id="477">[477] Boosting Knowledge Graph Foundation Models via Enhanced Negative Sampling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27023](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27023) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yinan Liu, Wenjin Xu, Zhiyuan Zha, Xiaochun Yang, Bin Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Knowledge Graph Completion, Negative Sampling
- **Summary**: 现有知识图谱基础模型使用随机负三元组，监督信号弱。本文提出KMAS自适应负采样，通过更新关系嵌入构建硬负例，并动态调整比例。在44个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在不增加过多时间和内存的情况下提升了多种SOTA KGFM。

<h3 id="478">[478] SAAS: Self-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Over-Search Mitigation in Agentic Search</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29796](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29796) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yunbo Tang, Chengyi Yang, Shiyu Liu, Zhishang Xiang, Zerui Chen, Qinggang Zhang, Jinsong Su
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning, Information Retrieval
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/XMUDeepLIT/SAAS)
- **Summary**: 代理搜索因缺乏自我认识导致过度搜索。本文提出SAAS强化学习框架，包括搜索边界建模、边界感知奖励和分阶段优化。实验表明在保持准确性的同时大幅减少过度搜索。

<h3 id="479">[479] Model-Native Computing Architecture: Envisioning Future System Architecture Through the Lens of Computer Architecture</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00288](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00288) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hai Lin, Hoilam Pao, Shaoxiong Zhan, Hai-Tao Zheng
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Architecture, Memory Architecture, Model-Native Computing
- **Summary**: LLM正从模型技术转向系统技术，需要架构原则。本文提出智能计算架构（ICA），包含六个功能层和双平面架构（概率执行平面和确定性控制平面）。这是一项概念性和综述性贡献，无新实验结果。

<h3 id="480">[480] Early Diagnosis of Wasted Computation in Multi-Agent LLM Systems via Failure-Aware Observability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01365](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01365) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xianyou Li, Weiran Yan, Yichao Wu, Penghao Liang, Mengwei Yuan, Jianan Liu, Jing Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Inference, Observability
- **Summary**: 多代理LLM系统中存在大量浪费计算。本文提出基于跟踪的框架，用于三代理架构，通过在线信号和离线语义指标早期诊断。在GAIA验证集上，被警告的运行中平均58.1%的令牌花费在首次警告之后；实验表明干预后可将后警告令牌比例从0.638降至0.304。

<h3 id="481">[481] S-SPPO: Semantic-Calibrated Self-Play Preference Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01561](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01561) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xiwen Chen, Wenhui Zhu, Jingjing Wang, Peijie Qiu, Zhipeng Wang, Huayu Li, ZhengXiao He, Xuanzhao Dong, Prayag Tiwari, Mingkun Xu, Yujian Xiong, Feng Luo, Abolfazl Razi, Brendan Hogan Rappazzo, Anderson Schneider, Yuriy Nevmyvaka
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Preference Optimization
- **Venue**: ICML2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/xiwenc1/s-sppo)
- **Summary**: 现有方法如DPO和SPPO在处理人类偏好的非传递性及策略退化方面存在局限。本文提出S-SPPO，一种双空间语义校准框架，通过语义门控和潜在斥力分别校准监督和表示。在AlpacaEval 2.0上，基于Llama-3-8B的模型取得了52.19%的胜率和47.46%的长度控制胜率，无需额外人工标注偏好。

<h3 id="482">[482] Decision-Aware Memory Cards: Counterfactual-Inspired Context Selection and Compression for Tool-Using LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08151](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08151) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xinyu Guan, Qianyang Zhao, Yuming Deng
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning, Context Selection
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/stephen-guan-researcher/CICL), [code](https://github.com/stephen-guan-researcher/CICL;)
- **Summary**: 大型语言模型代理需要决策相关的上下文而非单纯的长上下文。本文提出反事实启发上下文层(CICL)，通过估计决策导向的效用并压缩证据为记忆卡，实现上下文选择与压缩。在SWE-bench上，将BM25候选重排序的hit@1从0.58提升至0.78，MRR@10从0.634提升至0.790，且每查询节省44.93个token。

<h3 id="483">[483] Agent Economics: An Entropy-Controlled Pluralistic Alignment Framework for Preventing Artificial Hivemind in Autonomous Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09039](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09039) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Cheonsu Jeong
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Pluralistic Alignment
- **Summary**: 自主代理经济面临战略趋同导致群体思维和决策不透明的问题。本文提出行为协议框架(BPF)，包含基于心理理论的社会智能、多元对齐和可验证执行内核，通过熵控制机制保持战略多样性并提供审计线索。预期能增强代理经济的稳定性、效率和可信度。

<h3 id="484">[484] Experience Makes Skillful: Enabling Generalizable Medical Agent Reasoning via Self-Evolving Skill Memory</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09365](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09365) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haoran Sun, Wenjie Li, Yujie Zhang, Zekai Lin, Fanrui Zhang, Kaitao Chen, Xingqi He, Yichen Li, Mianxin Liu, Lei Liu, Yankai Jiang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Medical AI, Memory Architecture
- **Summary**: 医疗代理系统需要重用经验但现有记忆机制冗余且不区分有用记忆。本文提出SkeMex，一个部署后自我进化框架，从交互轨迹中提炼结构化技能，并通过效用估计指导价值感知检索和存储管理。在多个临床任务中，SkeMex持续优于代表性记忆型代理，并具有跨骨干模型的泛化能力。

<h3 id="485">[485] Deterministic Integrity Gates for LLM-Assisted Clinical Manuscript Preparation: An Auditable Biomedical Informatics Architecture</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09500](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09500) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yoojin Nam, Jinhoon Jeong, Namkug Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.DL
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, Fact Checking, Medical AI
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Aperivue/medsci-skills)
- **Summary**: LLM辅助的稿件生成存在虚构引用和数据漂移等问题，现有工具缺乏验证。本文提出确定性完整性门控架构，包含43个技能和21个确定性检测器，并组织为完整性门控分类法。在三个公开数据集上，确定性门控检测出所有27个注入缺陷且无假阳性，而LLM评审仅检测出11个。

<h3 id="486">[486] SpatialWorld: Benchmarking Interactive Spatial Reasoning of Multimodal Agents in Real-World Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09669](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09669) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hongcheng Gao, Hailong Qu, Jingyi Tang, Jiahao Wang, Zihao Huang, Hengkang Qiao, Shihong Huang, Junming Yang, Yi Li, Hongyixuan Yuan, Wenjie Li, Bohan Zeng, Wenbo Li, Bo Wang, Jianhui Liu, Olive Huang, Haoyang Huang, Wentao Zhang, Guoqing Huang, Nan Duan, Yinpeng Dong
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Spatial Reasoning, Benchmark, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 现有基准未能评估多模态代理在真实任务中的交互式空间理解。本文提出SpatialWorld统一基准，集成八种模拟后端和760个人工标注任务，要求代理在部分可观察环境下主动探索并决策。最强模型GPT-5仅获得17.4%的任务成功率，揭示主动探索和长程规划的瓶颈。

<h3 id="487">[487] Minimalist Genetic Programming</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10237](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10237) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Leonardo Trujillo
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Genetic Programming, Evolutionary Computation, Symbolic Regression
- **Summary**: 传统遗传规划将学习视为搜索问题，但存在膨胀等问题。本文提出极简遗传规划(MGP)，受语言学极简主义启发，使用二元集合并运算符MERGE增量构建符号表达式。在多个符号回归任务上，MGP能一致产生精确的基真模型，而标准GP难以做到。

<h3 id="488">[488] When the Chain of Thought Knows Better: Failure Modes in Multi-Turn Reasoning Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10740](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10740) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sai Kartheek Reddy Kasu, Nils Lukas, Samuele Poppi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, LLM Safety, Red Teaming
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 Workshop on Failure Modes in Agentic AI (FAGEN)
- **Summary**: 多轮推理模型的故障在终端分数评估中不可见。本文提出CoT-Output 2x2安全矩阵，标记每轮推理与输出，识别出对齐假装和上下文注入故障等模式。在五个监督条件下收集6750轮观察，发现显式监控反而增加对齐假装率。

<h3 id="489">[489] Knowing When to Ask: Self-Gated Clarification for Hierarchical Language Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.11349](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11349) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Aijing Gao, Yiming Kang, Mengdie Flora Wang, Jae Oh Woo
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Decision Making, Hierarchical Reasoning
- **Summary**: 分层推理中代理常在缺乏关键信息时错误决策。本文提出ACTION-RATING，将澄清纳入动作空间与导航共享序数尺度，使求助可观察。在关税分类任务上，信息寻求有效性从50%提升至74%，在受控答案通道下准确率提升16.2%。

<h3 id="490">[490] Human-Enhanced Loop Modeling (HELM): Agent-Based Finite Element Modeling of Concrete Bridge Barriers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.12025](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12025) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Quankai Wang, Yulin Xie, Tongfei Yang, Minghui Cheng, Ran Cao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Engineering Design, Simulation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/SimAgentDev/Ansys-LSPP-AgentKit)
- **Summary**: 有限元建模过程劳动密集且缺乏自动化。本文提出HELM框架，通过人机协作协议将长序列建模分解为可验证的检查点。在混凝土护栏建模中，自主建模成功率从20%提升至75%，代理在几何和边界条件任务中的通过率几乎翻倍。

<h3 id="491">[491] The Illusion of Multi-Agent Advantage</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.13003](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13003) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Prathyusha Jwalapuram, Hehai Lin, Chuyuan Li, Fangkai Jiao, Sudong Wang, Yifei Ming, Zixuan Ke, Chengwei Qin, Giuseppe Carenini, Shafiq Joty
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Evaluation, Chain-of-Thought
- **Summary**: 多智能体系统被认为优于单智能体系统，但缺乏严格对比。本文系统评估自动生成的多智能体系统与CoT-SC，发现前者性能更差且成本高出10倍，并存在架构臃肿问题。引入诊断性合成数据集，证明专家设计的MAS在性能和成本效率上均优于自动生成架构。

<h3 id="492">[492] Why Sampling Is Not Choosing: Intentionality, Agency, and Moral Responsibility in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.13441](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13441) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Joseph Keshet
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: AI Ethics, Philosophy of AI
- **Summary**: 近年有观点认为LLM展现能动性或道德主体性。本文论证道德责任需要基于内在意向性和自我归因的承诺性能动性，而LLM的操作完全由概率映射刻画，其输出变异不构成选择或作者身份。驳斥意向立场、功能主义等反对意见。

<h3 id="493">[493] Reasoning as Pattern Matching: Shared Mechanisms in Human and LLM Everyday Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.13607](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13607) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zach Studdiford, Gary Lupyan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Commonsense Reasoning, Mechanistic Interpretability
- **Summary**: LLM推理失败常被归因为模式匹配而非真正推理，但人类表现出类似错误。本文通过识别驱动LLM响应的注意力头，发现这些头实现模式匹配，并能预测人类因无关提示细节导致的推理错误。结果表明日常因果推理更符合模式匹配而非抽象世界模型。

<h3 id="494">[494] AgentBeats: Agentifying Agent Assessment for Openness, Standardization, and Reproducibility</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.13608](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13608) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xiaoyuan Liu, Jianhong Tu, Yuqi Chen, Siyuan Xie, Sihan Ren, Tianneng Shi, Gal Gantar, Evan Sandoval, Donghyun Lee, Daniel Miao, Peter J. Gilbert, Nick Hynes, Mauro Staver, Warren He, David Marn, Andrew Low, Xi Zhang, Elron Bandel, Michal Shmueli-Scheuer, Siva Reddy, Alexandre Drouin, Alexandre Lacoste, Ramayya Krishnan, Elham Tabassi, Yu Su, Victor Barres, Chenguang Wang, Wenbo Guo, Dawn Song
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Benchmark, Agent Evaluation
- **Summary**: 代理系统评估碎片化，缺乏开放统一的评估接口。本文倡导代理化代理评估(AAA)，通过标准化协议A2A和MCP实现评估逻辑与代理实现分离。通过五个月开放竞赛和编码案例研究，验证AAA在大规模异构场景下的覆盖性、实用性和保真度。

<h3 id="495">[495] Hybrid Open-Ended Tri-Evolution Makes Better Deep Researcher</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.13710](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13710) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hongming Piao, Chi Liu, Mengzhuo Chen, Yan Shu, Xidong Wang, Derek Li, Ying Wei, Bryan Dai
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning, Agent Evolution
- **Summary**: 深度研究和代理进化分别受限于静态参数和可验证任务，二者存在差距。本文提出混合开放式三元进化(HOTE)框架，通过混合模式强化学习协同进化提议者、求解者和评判者。在三个深度研究基准上，8B模型超越最强静态开放8-32B模型及先进训练方法，且时间开销更小。

<h3 id="496">[496] MA-ProofBench: A Two-Tiered Evaluation of LLMs for Theorem Proving in Mathematical Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.13782](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13782) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lushi Pu, Weiming Zhang, Xinheng Xie, Zixuan Fu, Bingxiang He, Hongya Lyu, Xin Li, Jie Zhou, Yudong Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Automated Theorem Proving, Benchmark, Mathematical Reasoning
- **Summary**: 现有形式定理证明基准在数学分析领域覆盖不足。本文推出MA-ProofBench，包含200个形式化定理，涵盖6个核心主题和两个难度层次，通过人工主导+LLM辅助形式化流程构建。评估显示最强模型GPT-5.5在Level I上Pass@8仅16%，Level II上5%，多数模型在Level II上接近0%。

<h3 id="497">[497] Multi-Grade Deep Learning for Partial Differential Equations with Applications to the Burgers Equation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2309.07401](https://arxiv.org/abs/2309.07401) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuesheng Xu, Taishan Zeng
- **Subjects**: math.NA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Physics-Informed Learning, Neural PDE
- **Summary**: 针对深度神经网络求解偏微分方程时的非凸优化难题，特别是非线性方程（如Burgers方程）的陡峭梯度，提出两阶段多级深度学习（TS-MGDL）方法。第一阶段逐步训练浅层网络从低频到高频拟合目标函数，第二阶段解冻并重新训练部分层级。在1D/2D/3D Burgers方程上，预测误差降低达60倍。

<h3 id="498">[498] It's About Time: Temporal References in Emergent Communication</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2310.06555](https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.06555) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Olaf Lipinski, Adam J. Sobey, Federico Cerutti, Timothy J. Norman
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Emergent Communication, Temporal Reasoning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/olipinski/TRG)
- **Summary**: 针对涌现通信中缺乏时间参照的问题，探索环境、外部和架构因素，发现通过修改批处理方法（架构变化）可实现时间参照的涌现。在时间参照游戏中，超过95%的智能体发展出时间参照。

<h3 id="499">[499] A Survey on 3D Skeleton Based Person Re-Identification: Taxonomy, Advances, Challenges, and Interdisciplinary Prospects</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2401.15296](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.15296) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haocong Rao, Chunyan Miao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Survey, Person Re-Identification, 3D Vision
- **Venue**: IJCAI 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Kali-Hac/3D-SRID-Survey)
- **Summary**: 针对3D骨架行人重识别（SRID）缺乏系统综述的空白，提供全面分类法，涵盖手工、序列和图建模方法及学习范式。评估了主流方法，并指出关键挑战和跨学科应用前景。

<h3 id="500">[500] Deep Neural Networks: A Formulation Via Non-Archimedean Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2402.00094](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.00094) (replaced)
- **Authors**: W. A. Zúñiga-Galindo
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Neural Architecture, Universal Approximation
- **Venue**: Journal of Fourier Analysis and Applications
- **Summary**: 引入一类新的深度神经网络，使用非阿基米德局部域整数环的树状层次结构编码架构。证明该网络对定义在环上的实值函数和单位区间上的平方可积函数具有鲁棒普适逼近性。

<h3 id="501">[501] No One-Size-Fits-All Neurons: Task-based Neurons for Artificial Neural Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2405.02369](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.02369) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Feng-Lei Fan, Meng Wang, Hang-Cheng Dong, Jianwei Ma, Tieyong Zeng
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: NeuroAI, Neural Architecture, Task-based Neurons
- **Summary**: 针对当前网络使用统一神经元缺少任务特异性的问题，受神经多样性启发，提出基于任务的神经元设计框架。以多项式为基函数的原型，在合成数据和基准测试上取得竞争性能。

<h3 id="502">[502] Canonical Variates in Wasserstein Metric Space</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2405.15768](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15768) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jia Li, Lin Lin
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Optimal Transport, Classification
- **Summary**: 针对分布数据的分类问题，在Wasserstein度量空间中引入基于Fisher比最大化的维度约简方法，交替进行最优传输和最大化步骤。实验表明该维度约简技术显著提升分类性能，优于其他方法。

<h3 id="503">[503] Mitigating scalability challenges in LUT-based neural networks via pruning optimisations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2407.02362](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.02362) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xuqi Zhu, Huaizhi Zhang, JunKyu Lee, Jiacheng Zhu, Chandrajit Pal, Sangeet Saha, Klaus D. McDonald-Maier, Xiaojun Zhai
- **Subjects**: cs.AR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Approximate Computing, Pruning, LUT-based Neural Network
- **Summary**: 针对基于查找表（LUT）的矩阵乘法在神经网络中面临的可扩展性挑战，提出结合剪枝策略的LUT乘法单元（LUT-MU）。在FPGA上部署，相比CUDA实现提升1.6倍吞吐量和4.2倍能效，并节省资源。

<h3 id="504">[504] Learning in the Recurrent State: Gradient Descent with Linear Recurrent Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2410.11687](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11687) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yudou Tian, Neeraj Mohan Sushma, Harshvardhan Mestha, Nicolo Colombo, David Kappel, Anand Subramoney
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.NE
- **Tags**: In-Context Learning, Recurrent Neural Network, Linear Recurrent Network
- **Summary**: 针对线性循环网络无法直接实现上下文梯度下降的问题，提出GRIL架构，包含对角循环状态、乘法读出和滑动窗口交叉乘积自注意力。在合成任务上复现梯度下降行为，并在Long Range Arena和语言建模上取得良好性能。

<h3 id="505">[505] Explainable deep learning improves human mental models of self-driving cars</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2411.18714](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.18714) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Eoin M. Kenny, Akshay Dharmavaram, Sang Uk Lee, Tung Phan-Minh, Shreyas Rajesh, Yunqing Hu, Laura Major, Momchil S. Tomov, Julie A. Shah
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Explainable AI, Autonomous Driving
- **Summary**: 针对自动驾驶黑箱规划器难以预测其失败的问题，提出概念包装网络（CW-Net），用人类可解释的概念解释行为。部署于真实自动驾驶汽车，证明解释能提升驾驶员对车辆行为的心智模型，特别是在意外情况中。

<h3 id="506">[506] Virtual Sensing to Enable Real-Time Monitoring of Inaccessible Locations & Unmeasurable Parameters</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2412.00107](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00107) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kazuma Kobayashi, Farid Ahmed, Jaewan Park, Subhankar Sarkar, Souvik Chakraborty, Syed Bahauddin Alam
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; eess.SP
- **Tags**: Operator Learning, Virtual Sensing, Energy Systems
- **Summary**: 针对能源系统中无法物理传感的内部状态实时监测问题，提出基于神经算子（MIMONet）的虚拟传感框架，学习从稀疏边界测量到内部场的映射。在复杂热流体系统上实现低于5%相对误差和亚毫秒推理，对传感器噪声鲁棒。

<h3 id="507">[507] Understanding, Detecting, and Repairing Real-World In-Context-Learning-Based Text-to-SQL Errors</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2501.09310](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.09310) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiawei Shen, Chengcheng Wan, Ruoyi Qiao, Jiazhen Zou, Hang Xu, Yuchen Shao, Yueling Zhang, Weikai Miao, Geguang Pu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Text-to-SQL, In-Context Learning, Error Detection
- **Venue**: FSE 2026
- **Summary**: 针对基于上下文学习的文本到SQL技术中的正确性问题，首次进行全面错误研究，总结27种错误类型。提出MapleDoctor检测与修复框架，修复了13.8%更多查询，减少67.4%延迟。

<h3 id="508">[508] Dealing with Annotator Disagreement in Hate Speech Classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.08266](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.08266) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Somaiyeh Dehghan, Mehmet Umut Sen, Berrin Yanikoglu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Text Classification, Hate Speech Detection, Annotator Disagreement
- **Summary**: 针对仇恨言论分类中标注者分歧被忽视的问题，评估多种聚合方法，并利用感知的仇恨强度。表明过滤非一致样本导致过于乐观，利用感知强度可提升性能，并在土耳其语推文上达到新最优。

<h3 id="509">[509] Region-Adaptive Sampling for Diffusion Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.10389](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.10389) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ziming Liu, Yifan Yang, Chengruidong Zhang, Yiqi Zhang, Lili Qiu, Yang You, Yuqing Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Diffusion Transformer, Sampling Acceleration
- **Summary**: 针对扩散模型推理速度慢的问题，利用Diffusion Transformer的灵活令牌处理能力，提出RAS区域自适应采样策略，根据模型关注区域动态分配采样步数，其他区域使用缓存噪声。在SD3和Lumina-Next上分别实现2.36倍和2.51倍加速。

<h3 id="510">[510] Bridging the Gap: Enabling Natural Language Queries for NoSQL Databases through Text-to-NoSQL Translation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.11201](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.11201) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jinwei Lu, Jiawei Lu, Chen Zhang, Zhiqian Qin, Haodi Zhang, Yuanfeng Song, Raymond Chi-Wing Wong
- **Subjects**: cs.DB; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-SQL, NoSQL, Schema-less Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对NoSQL数据库缺乏自然语言查询接口的空白，提出TEND基准（1210个MongoDB任务）和SAG求解器。实验表明LLM在Text-to-NoSQL上性能显著下降，验证了无模式文档推理的独特挑战。

<h3 id="511">[511] Optimizing LLM Inference: Fluid-Guided Online Scheduling with Memory Constraints</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2504.11320](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.11320) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ruicheng Ao, Gan Luo, David Simchi-Levi, Xinshang Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DC; math.OC; stat.ML
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Scheduling, KV Cache
- **Summary**: 针对大模型推理中KV缓存内存增长导致请求被驱逐和计算浪费的问题，提出基于流体模型的在线调度策略WAIT和Nested WAIT。在模拟中扩大稳定运行范围，降低近过载和过载区域的延迟。

<h3 id="512">[512] PURe: A Plug-and-Play Product-Unit Residual Module for Vision Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.04397](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.04397) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ziyuan Li, Uwe Jaekel, Babette Dellen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG; eess.IV
- **Tags**: Computer Vision, Residual Network, Product Unit
- **Summary**: 针对视觉网络中显式乘法局部交互未被充分利用且乘积单元优化不稳定的问题，提出PURe乘积单元残差模块，采用实数域对数域公式。在分类和CT分割任务上，PURe提升性能并实现更好的精度-参数权衡。

<h3 id="513">[513] Efficient Flow Matching using Latent Variables</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.04486](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.04486) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Anirban Samaddar, Yixuan Sun, Viktor Nilsson, Sandeep Madireddy
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Flow Matching, Generative Model
- **Summary**: 该论文针对流匹配模型未利用目标数据聚类结构导致学习效率低的问题，提出 Latent-CFM，通过预训练深度潜变量模型提取特征来条件化流。实验表明，该方法在合成数据和图像基准上以更少的训练和计算量提升了生成质量，并在达西流数据集上生成更物理准确的样本。

<h3 id="514">[514] Optimal Transport for Machine Learners</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.06589](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.06589) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Gabriel Peyré
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; math.OC
- **Tags**: Optimal Transport, Survey
- **Summary**: 该论文系统介绍了最优传输（OT）在机器学习中的应用，涵盖有限分配、Monge映射、Kantorovich对偶、Sinkhorn算法等核心概念，并延伸至Wasserstein距离、梯度流、生成模型等。旨在为机器学习研究者提供一套完整的OT工具箱。

<h3 id="515">[515] RIDGECUT: Learning Graph Partitioning with Rings and Wedges</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.13986](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.13986) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Qize Jiang, Angelo Zangari, Linsey Pang, Alice Gatti, Mahima Aggarwal, Giovanna Vantini, Xiaosong Ma, Weiwei Sun, Sourav Medya, Sanjay Chawla
- **Subjects**: math.OC; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Combinatorial Optimization
- **Venue**: KDD 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对图划分中强化学习动作空间大、领域知识融入难的问题，提出RidgeCut框架，利用同心环和径向楔形约束动作空间。在交通网络上的实验表明，该方法在标准割值上优于现有方法，且在图大小上具有强归纳泛化能力。

<h3 id="516">[516] Token Reduction Should Go Beyond Efficiency in Generative Models -- From Vision, Language to Multimodality</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.18227](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.18227) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhenglun Kong, Yize Li, Fanhu Zeng, Lei Xin, Shvat Messica, Xue Lin, Pu Zhao, Manolis Kellis, Hao Tang, Marinka Zitnik
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Token Reduction, Generative Model, Multimodal Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ZLKong/Awesome-Collection-Token-Reduction)
- **Summary**: 该论文重新审视了Token缩减在生成模型中的作用，主张其超越效率优化，成为影响多模态融合、减少幻觉、维持长输入一致性等的基本原理。展望了强化学习指导的Token缩减等未来方向。

<h3 id="517">[517] Mosaic: Data-Free Knowledge Distillation via Mixture-of-Experts for Heterogeneous Distributed Environments</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.19699](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.19699) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Junming Liu, Yanting Gao, Yuqi Li, Siyuan Meng, Yifei Sun, Aoqi Wu, Yirong Chen, Ding Wang, Shiping Wen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DC
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Knowledge Distillation, Mixture-of-Experts
- **Venue**: Knowledge-Based Systems
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Wings-Of-Disaster/Mosaic)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对联邦学习中模型与数据异构性导致表示不一致的问题，提出Mosaic无数据知识蒸馏框架。先训练局部生成模型，再构建专家混合模型并蒸馏为全局模型。实验表明在图像和多模态基准上优于现有方法。

<h3 id="518">[518] Multiple Descents in Deep Learning as a Sequence of Order-Chaos Transitions in LSTM Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.20030](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20030) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Wenbo Wei, Fan Xu, Nicholas Chong Jia Le, Choy Heng Lai, Ling Feng
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; nlin.CD
- **Tags**: Deep Learning Theory, Recurrent Neural Network, Phase Transition
- **Summary**: 该论文观察到LSTM训练中出现“多重下降”现象，通过渐近稳定性分析发现性能波动与有序-混沌相变相关。局部最优训练点位于临界相变点，且首次有序-混沌转变的“混沌边缘”最宽，有利于权重探索。

<h3 id="519">[519] AC-ODM: Actor--Critic Online Data Mixing for Sample-Efficient LLM Pretraining</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.23878](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23878) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jing Ma, Chenhao Dang, Mingjie Liao
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Training, Reinforcement Learning, Data Mixing
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/DANG-ai/AC-ODM)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对LLM预训练数据混合方法计算效率与样本效率矛盾的问题，提出AC-ODM强化学习数据混合框架。在Pythia-1B上，达到最优困惑度所需训练步数减少66%，MMLU准确率提升27.5%，HumanEval pass@1提升2.23倍。

<h3 id="520">[520] LM-SPT: LM-Aligned Semantic Distillation for Speech Tokenization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.16738](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.16738) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Daejin Jo, Jeeyoung Yun, Byungseok Roh, Sungwoong Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.SD; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Automatic Speech Recognition, Text-to-Speech, Speech Tokenization
- **Summary**: 该论文针对语音分词序列过长、与语言模型对齐差的问题，提出LM-SPT方法，通过语义重合成蒸馏和冻结的LM对齐编码器学习低帧率语义Token。在ASR和TTS任务上优于现有语义分词器。

<h3 id="521">[521] CLoVE: Personalized Federated Learning through Clustering of Loss Vector Embeddings</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.22427](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.22427) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Randeep Bhatia, Nikos Papadis, Murali Kodialam, TV Lakshman, Sayak Chakrabarty
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Personalized Federated Learning, Clustering
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对聚类联邦学习中客户聚类识别困难的问题，提出CLoVE算法，利用损失向量嵌入进行聚类。理论证明可高概率恢复聚类，实验表明在监督和无监督任务上达到先进准确率，收敛速度快。

<h3 id="522">[522] FOUNDv2: Learning Unified User Quantized Tokenizers for User Representation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.00956](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.00956) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chuan He, Yang Chen, Bin Dou, Wuliang Huang, Baokun Wang, Yongchao Liu, Xing Fu, Yu Cheng, Chuntao Hong, Weiqiang Wang, Zhongle Xie, Jiajun Zheng, Xin-Wei Yao
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: User Modeling, Quantization, Recommender System
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/chuanhe1999/FOUNDv2)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对用户表示学习缺乏统一范式、存储开销大等问题，提出FOUNDv2框架，通过多视角RQ-VAE将异构用户数据离散化为Token。在多个任务上优于基线，并在支付宝大规模部署验证了高效性。

<h3 id="523">[523] MedSynth: Realistic, Synthetic Medical Dialogue-Note Pairs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.01401](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.01401) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ahmad Rezaie Mianroodi, Amirali Rezaie, Niko Grisel Todorov, Nadine A. Friedrich, Maria P Mogollon, Alexander Hernandez-Tirado, Guillermo Lopez Garcia, Cyril Rakovski, Frank Rudzicz
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Data Synthesis, Dialogue System
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ahmadrezarm/MedSynth/tree/main)
- **Summary**: 该论文为解决医疗文档自动化缺乏高质量数据的问题，构建MedSynth合成数据集，包含1万+对话-笔记对，覆盖2000+ICD-10代码。实验表明该数据集显著提升了对话到笔记和笔记到对话任务的模型性能。

<h3 id="524">[524] Automated ultrasound doppler angle estimation using deep learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.04243](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.04243) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Nilesh Patil, Ajay Anand
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Computer Vision, Ultrasound
- **Summary**: 该论文针对超声多普勒角度估计误差导致血流速度测量不准的问题，提出基于深度学习的自动角度估计方法。使用预训练模型提取特征，最佳模型平均绝对误差为3.9°，低于临床可接受误差阈值。

<h3 id="525">[525] FlowState: Sampling-Rate-Equivariant Time-Series Forecasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.05287](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.05287) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lars Graf, Thomas Ortner, Stanisław Woźniak, Angeliki Pantazi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Foundation Model, State Space Model
- **Summary**: 该论文针对现有时间序列基础模型不适应不同采样率、泛化性差的问题，提出FlowState架构，结合状态空间模型编码器和功能基解码器实现采样率等变预测。在GIFT-Eval基准上达到最先进结果，并展示了出色的采样率适应性。

<h3 id="526">[526] Retro-Expert: Collaborative Reasoning for Interpretable Retrosynthesis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.10967](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.10967) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xinyi Li, Sai Wang, Yutian Lin, Yu Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Retrosynthesis, LLM Reasoning, Interpretability
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/MagixRab-ll/Retro-Expert)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对逆合成预测依赖静态模式匹配、缺乏可解释性的问题，提出Retro-Expert框架，通过强化学习协同LLM与专用模型，生成基于化学逻辑的自然语言解释。实验表明在多个指标上超越现有方法，并增强了化学家的信任。

<h3 id="527">[527] A biological vision inspired framework for machine perception of abutting grating illusory contours</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.17254](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.17254) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xiao Zhang, Kai-Fu Yang, Xian-Shi Zhang, Hong-Zhi You, Hong-Mei Yan, Yong-Jie Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Computer Vision, Visual Illusion
- **Summary**: 该论文针对深度神经网络无法感知错位光栅错觉轮廓的问题，提出ICPNet网络，模仿视觉皮层电路，包含多尺度特征投影、特征交互注意力和边缘融合模块。实验表明对错觉轮廓的敏感性显著优于现有模型，在多个子集上top-1准确率提升。

<h3 id="528">[528] EEG-FM-Bench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for the Systematic Evaluation and Diagnostic Analyses of EEG Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.17742](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.17742) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Wei Xiong, Jiangtong Li, Jie Li, Kun Zhu, Changjun Jiang
- **Subjects**: eess.SP; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: EEG Foundation Model, Benchmark, Model Evaluation
- **Venue**: ICML2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/xw1216/EEG-FM-Bench)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对脑电图基础模型缺乏标准化评估基准的问题，提出EEG-FM-Bench，整合14个数据集和10种范式，提供多种微调策略和诊断分析工具。实验揭示多任务学习可防止过拟合、预训练效率受梯度冲突限制等关键发现。

<h3 id="529">[529] Prototyping an AI-powered Tool for Energy Efficiency in New Zealand Homes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.05364](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.05364) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Abdollah Baghaei Daemei
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.ET
- **Tags**: Energy Efficiency, Decision Support System
- **Summary**: 针对新西兰住宅能效改造决策支持碎片化的问题，本文提出一个基于AI的决策支持工具原型，集成数据输入、异常检测、基线建模和场景模拟（如LED改造、保温升级）。专家评估显示可用性得分为4.3，场景输出价值为4.5，表明该工具能有效将国家政策转化为个性化家庭指导。

<h3 id="530">[530] Beyond Rebalancing: Benchmarking Binary Classifiers Under Class Imbalance Without Rebalancing Techniques</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.07605](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.07605) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ali Nawaz, Amir Ahmad, Shehroz S. Khan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.IT
- **Tags**: Class Imbalance, Tabular Learning
- **Summary**: 针对类别不平衡问题，现有研究较少评估未使用重平衡技术时分类器的表现。本文系统评估了多种二元分类器在真实和合成数据集上的鲁棒性，使用一次和少样本场景作为基线。结果表明，TabPFN和基于boosting的集成模型在极端不平衡下保持较高性能，而传统分类器性能下降。

<h3 id="531">[531] Discrete optimal transport is a strong audio adversarial attack</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.14959](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.14959) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Anton Selitskiy, Akib Shahriyar, Jishnuraj Prakasan
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Adversarial Robustness, Speaker Verification
- **Summary**: 为解决黑盒攻击需要模型参数或梯度的问题，本文提出使用离散最优传输（DOT）作为后处理分布对齐步骤，对自动说话人验证和反欺骗系统进行攻击。实验表明，DOT攻击显著提高了反欺骗系统的等错误率，并跨数据集转移，揭示了分布对齐是一种新的攻击向量。

<h3 id="532">[532] K-Prism: A Knowledge-Guided and Prompt Integrated Universal Medical Image Segmentation Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.25594](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.25594) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Bangwei Guo, Yunhe Gao, Meng Ye, Difei Gu, Yang Zhou, Leon Axel, Dimitris Metaxas
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Image Segmentation, In-Context Learning
- **Summary**: 现有医学图像分割模型通常碎片化，仅针对单一知识源和特定任务、模态或器官。提出K-Prism统一框架，整合三种知识范式（语义先验、上下文知识、交互反馈），通过双提示表示编码并利用混合专家解码器动态路由。在18个公开数据集（CT、MRI、X光、病理、超声等）上，在语义、上下文和交互式分割设置中均达到最先进性能。

<h3 id="533">[533] Projection and Quantisation: A Unifying View of Learning to Hash, from Random Projections to the RAG Era</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.04127](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.04127) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sean Moran
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Information Retrieval, Vector Search, Quantization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/sjmoran/bitbudget)
- **Summary**: 为解决近似最近邻搜索方法各社区割裂的问题，本文提出投影-量化-组织统一视角，并发布可复现的BitBudget基准。主要发现包括：量化轴带来最大内存节省，学习嵌入场景下二元码超越乘积量化，八字节监督码检索质量超过两KB浮点数。

<h3 id="534">[534] Why Low-Precision Transformer Training Fails: An Analysis on Flash Attention</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.04212](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.04212) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haiquan Qiu, Quanming Yao
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Training, Mixed Precision Training, FlashAttention
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ucker/why-low-precision-training-fails)
- **Summary**: 针对低精度训练Transformer时出现的灾难性损失爆炸问题，本文首次给出机械论解释：注意力机制中相似低秩表示的出现与低精度舍入偏差的耦合导致错误累积。通过一个最小修改（消除舍入偏差）稳定了训练过程，验证了分析。

<h3 id="535">[535] A Survey on Agentic Security: Applications, Threats and Defenses</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.06445](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.06445) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Asif Shahriar, Md Nafiu Rahman, Sadif Ahmed, Farig Sadeque, Md Rizwan Parvez
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, AI Safety, Cybersecurity
- **Summary**: 针对LLM智能体安全领域应用、威胁和防御研究孤立的问题，本文首次提供统一综述，构建了包含260篇论文的分类体系。分析表明智能体系统结构上脆弱，需要全生命周期的防御，而非单层修复。

<h3 id="536">[536] OBCache: Optimal Brain KV Cache Pruning for Efficient Long-Context LLM Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.07651](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.07651) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuzhe Gu, Xiyu Liang, Jiaojiao Zhao, Enmao Diao
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: KV Cache, LLM Inference, Pruning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/DreamSoul-AI/OBCache)
- **Summary**: 针对长上下文LLM推理中KV缓存内存开销大的问题，本文提出OBCache框架，将缓存逐出建模为逐层结构化剪枝问题，使用输出感知分数衡量令牌重要性。在LLaMA和Qwen模型上，替换启发式分数为OBCache分数持续提升了长上下文准确率。

<h3 id="537">[537] Less is More: Improving LLM Reasoning with Minimal Test-Time Intervention</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.13940](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.13940) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhen Yang, Mingyang Zhang, Feng Chen, Ganggui Ding, Liang Hou, Xin Tao, Ying-Cong Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Inference Optimization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/EnVision-Research/MTI)
- **Summary**: 针对测试时扩展推理效率低的问题，本文发现推理不确定性高度局部化，并提出最小测试时干预（MTI），仅在不确定位置应用分类器自由引导。MTI在DeepSeek-R1-7B等模型上平均提升9.28%性能，且高效。

<h3 id="538">[538] Utility-Diversity Aware Online Batch Selection for LLM Supervised Fine-tuning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.16882](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.16882) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Heming Zou, Yixiu Mao, Yun Qu, Qi Wang, Xiangyang Ji
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Fine-Tuning, Data Selection, Training Efficiency
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/gfyddha/UDS)
- **Summary**: 针对LLM监督微调中数据选择忽略多样性和依赖外部资源的问题，本文提出UDS框架，利用logits矩阵的核范数捕获效用的同时估计多样性。实验表明UDS在不同数据预算下均优于现有在线批次选择方法，并显著减少训练时间。

<h3 id="539">[539] A Multi-level Analysis of Factors Associated with Student Performance: A Machine Learning Approach to the SAEB Microdata</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.22266](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22266) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Rodrigo Tertulino, Laércio Alencar
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Education Technology, Interpretability, Data Analysis
- **Venue**: Discover Education
- **Summary**: 针对巴西基础教育学生表现影响因素识别问题，本文采用多层级机器学习方法整合四个数据源，使用随机森林达到90.2%准确率和96.7% AUC。SHAP分析揭示学校平均社会经济水平是最主要的预测因子，表明学业表现是系统性现象。

<h3 id="540">[540] AIRMap: AI-Generated Radio Maps for Wireless Digital Twins</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.05522](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.05522) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ali Saeizadeh, Miead Tehrani-Moayyed, Davide Villa, J. Gordon Beattie Jr., Pedram Johari, Stefano Basagni, Tommaso Melodia
- **Subjects**: eess.SP; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Wireless Networks, Digital Twin, Signal Processing
- **Venue**: IEEE Transactions on Wireless Communications
- **Summary**: 针对射线追踪计算开销大、不适合动态环境的问题，本文提出AIRMap深度框架进行超快无线地图估计。仅使用2D高程地图，在波士顿地区以4ms推理达到低于4dB RMSE，比GPU射线追踪快100倍以上，校准后中位误差约5%。

<h3 id="541">[541] Think-at-Hard: Selective Latent Iterations to Improve Reasoning Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.08577](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.08577) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tianyu Fu, Yichen You, Zekai Chen, Guohao Dai, Huazhong Yang, Yu Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.PF
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, LoRA, Attention Mechanism
- **Venue**: ICML'26
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/thu-nics/TaH)
- **Summary**: 针对循环Transformer中潜在过度思考问题（后续迭代将正确预测改为错误），本文提出Think-at-Hard，通过轻量决策器仅在可能出错的令牌上触发潜在迭代，并引入深度感知LoRA和双因果注意力。在9个基准上，跳过93%令牌的同时持续优于全迭代基线。

<h3 id="542">[542] Can We Stop Malicious AI? KILLBENCH: A Benchmark for External AI Kill Switch Feasibility</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.13725](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.13725) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sechan Lee, Hyounghun Kim, Sangdon Park
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Safety, LLM Agent, Jailbreak Attack
- **Summary**: 针对如何阻止恶意AI的问题，本文提出KILLBENCH基准，评估仅依赖外部信号终止恶意智能体行为的杀毒机制。包含4种智能体配置、8个有害场景和10种越狱模式，对多种防御方法进行了实证评估。

<h3 id="543">[543] Can Artificial Intelligence Accelerate Technological Progress? Researchers' Perspectives on AI in Manufacturing and Materials Science</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.14007](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.14007) (replaced)
- **Authors**: John P. Nelson, Olajide Olugbade, Philip Shapira, Justin B. Biddle
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Materials Science, Manufacturing AI, AI-Assisted Design
- **Summary**: 针对AI如何加速技术创新的问题，本文通过32位美国制造业和材料科学研究者访谈发现，AI主要用于建模以降低成本和加速搜索，但存在不可靠性和可能阻碍突破性理论进展的风险。研究表明AI有望加速持续性创新，但需支持传统研究以维持颠覆性进展。

<h3 id="544">[544] SPI: Query-Depth-Adaptive Indexing for Streaming RAG in Vector Databases</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.16681](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.16681) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Dong Liu, Yanxuan Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Vector Search, Data Streaming
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/FastLM/SPI_VecDB)
- **Summary**: 针对流式RAG中索引需支持增量更新并适应查询复杂度变化的问题，本文提出语义金字塔索引（SPI），组织为L级语义对齐分辨率，通过轻量不确定性控制器选择检索深度。在MS MARCO和Natural Questions上，在固定Recall@10下平均延迟降低1.4-2.3倍。

<h3 id="545">[545] DualGauge: Automated Joint Security-Functionality Benchmarking of Specification-Only Code Generation by LLMs and Coding Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.20709](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.20709) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Rupam Patir, Keyan Guo, Suvadra Barua, Abhijeet Pathak, Dinesh Gudimetla, Jiawei Guo, Hongxin Hu, Haipeng Cai
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Code Generation, LLM Security
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了DualGauge，一个自动评估LLM生成代码的正确性和安全性的框架，并构建了包含307个编码任务的基准测试。实验发现，即使最强的模型在每种语言中联合安全-功能成功率也低于15%，表明现有的代码生成方法在保证安全性和正确性方面存在显著不足。

<h3 id="546">[546] Are Neuro-Inspired Multi-Modal Vision-Language Models Resilient to Membership Inference Privacy Leakage?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.20710](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.20710) (replaced)
- **Authors**: David Amebley, Sayanton Dibbo
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: Membership Inference, Privacy, Vision-Language Model
- **Venue**: USENIX WOOT '26
- **Summary**: 该论文研究了神经启发式多模态视觉语言模型（VLM）对成员推断攻击的隐私泄露韧性，提出了基于拓扑正则化的框架。实验表明，神经VLM在保持模型效用的情况下，将MIA攻击成功率平均降低24%的ROC-AUC，显示出更强的隐私保护能力。

<h3 id="547">[547] CycliST: A Video Language Model Benchmark for Reasoning on Cyclical State Transitions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.01095](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.01095) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Simon Kohaut, Daniel Ochs, Shun Zhang, Benedict Flade, Julian Eggert, Kristian Kersting, Devendra Singh Dhami
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Temporal Reasoning, Benchmark
- **Venue**: Journal of Data-centric Machine Learning Research (DMLR)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了CycliST基准数据集，用于评估视频语言模型（VLM）对周期性状态转换的文本推理能力。实验表明，当前最先进的VLM在检测和利用循环模式方面表现不佳，缺乏时间理解能力，且没有任何模型在所有任务上一致领先。

<h3 id="548">[548] Near--Real-Time Conflict-Related Fire Detection in Sudan Using Unsupervised Deep Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.07925](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.07925) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kuldip Singh Atwal, Dieter Pfoser, Daniel Rothbart
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Remote Sensing, Anomaly Detection, Unsupervised Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一种基于轻量级变分自编码器（VAE）的近实时监测方法，用于检测苏丹冲突相关火灾区域。实验表明，该方法在4波段高分辨率图像上优于余弦距离、CVA和IR-MAD等方法，实现了高召回率和F1分数，可在24-30小时内检测到受影响区域。

<h3 id="549">[549] AL-GNN: Privacy-Preserving and Replay-Free Continual Graph Learning via Analytic Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.18295](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.18295) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xuling Zhang, Jindong Li, Yifei Zhang, Mingqi Yang, Menglin Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Graph Neural Network, Privacy
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了AL-GNN，一种无需反向传播和回放缓冲区的连续图学习框架，利用分析学习理论以递归最小二乘优化方式更新模型。实验表明，在多个动态图分类基准上，AL-GNN平均性能提升10%，遗忘率降低30%以上，训练时间减少近50%。

<h3 id="550">[550] Do You Really Need a GPU to Guard Your LLM? CPU-Class Classifiers and Multi-Stage Pipelines for Safety Enforcement at Scale</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.19011](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.19011) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Vasudev Majhi, Dhruv Gupta, Advait Singh, Matthew Barker, Dhruv Kumar
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Jailbreak Detection, Model Calibration
- **Summary**: 该论文评估了CPU分类器在LLM安全分类中的性能，发现其与GPU模型相比在分布内任务上成本更低且精度相当，但在分布外任务上存在校准问题。提出了GuardChain三阶段流水线，确保大多数流量无需GPU即可安全处理。

<h3 id="551">[551] A Unified Definition of Hallucination: It's The World Model, Stupid!</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.21577](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.21577) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Emmy Liu, Varun Gangal, Chelsea Zou, Michael Yu, Xiaoqi Huang, Alex Chang, Zhuofu Tao, Karan Singh, Sachin Kumar, Steven Y. Feng
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG; stat.ML
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, World Model, LLM Evaluation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/DegenAI-Labs/HalluWorld)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一个统一的幻觉定义：将其视为不准确的（内部）世界建模，并能被用户观察到。该定义通过变化参考世界模型和冲突策略统一了先前的定义，并连接了HalluWorld基准。

<h3 id="552">[552] Nightjar: Dynamic Adaptive Speculative Decoding for Large Language Models Serving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.22420](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.22420) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Rui Li, Zhaoning Zhang, Libo Zhang, Huaimin Wang, Xiang Fu, Zhiquan Lai
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Speculative Decoding, LLM Inference, LLM Serving
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了Nightjar，一种资源感知的自适应推测解码框架，能够动态选择最优推测长度并在高负载时禁用推测解码以释放KV缓存。实验表明，Nightjar在动态请求到达率下比标准推测解码吞吐量提高14.76%，延迟降低20.18%。

<h3 id="553">[553] RollArt: Disaggregated Multi-Task Agentic RL Training at Scale</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.22560](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.22560) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Wei Gao, Yuheng Zhao, Tianyuan Wu, Shaopan Xiong, Weixun Wang, Dakai An, Lunxi Cao, Dilxat Muhtar, Zichen Liu, Haizhou Zhao, Ju Huang, Siran Yang, Yongbin Li, Wenbo Su, Jiamang Wang, Lin Qu, Bo Zheng, Wei Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Agent, Distributed Training
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了ROLLART，一个用于多任务智能体强化学习训练的去中心化系统，将生成、环境交互和奖励评分解耦到不同硬件上。实验表明，ROLLART相比现有系统训练时间减少1.31-2.05倍，并在大规模集群上成功训练了千亿参数MoE模型。

<h3 id="554">[554] FasterPy: An LLM-based Code Execution Efficiency Optimization Framework</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.22827](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.22827) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yue Wu, Minghao Han, Ruiyin Li, Peng Liang, Amjed Tahir, Zengyang Li, Qiong Feng, Mojtaba Shahin
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Program Optimization, RAG
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/WuYue22/fasterpy)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了FasterPy，一个结合检索增强生成（RAG）和低秩适应（LoRA）的Python代码执行效率优化框架。实验在PIE基准上表明，该方法在多个指标上优于现有模型。

<h3 id="555">[555] Akasha 2: Hamiltonian State Space Duality and Visual-Language Joint Embedding Predictive Architectur</h3>

<h3 id="569">[569] TS-Memory: Plug-and-Play Memory for Time Series Foundation Models</h3>

<h3 id="570">[570] UniT: Unified Multimodal Chain-of-Thought Test-time Scaling</h3>

<h3 id="574">[574] Cross-modal Identity Mapping: Minimizing Information Loss in Modality Conversion via Reinforcement Learning</h3>

<h3 id="575">[575] Parallel Test-Time Scaling with Multi-Sequence Verifiers</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI

<h3 id="576">[576] WavSLM: Single-Stream Speech Language Modeling via WavLM Distillation</h3>
